Each `CREDIT_NOTE` transaction row now reports remaining (not face) via `runningBalance` and exposes `expiresAt` (consumed by the buyer wallet tile). `advanceBalance`/`pendingOrdersAmount` (separate `AdvancePayment` concept) are untouched — never netted into `availableCredit`. **`email` is OPTIONAL** (DTO `@IsOptional`): `create` mints a unique `no-email+<uuid>@placeholder.local` for the required `User.email` and leaves `Customer.email` null; `sendPortalInvite` ignores `@placeholder.local` fallbacks. **`mergeCustomers` (T1-16#7 fix)** re-points the secondary's `CustomerAuthorization`+`AuthorizationOverride` to the primary before deleting it — both FK-cascade on customer delete, so without this a merge silently DESTROYED the secondary's regulated licenses. `CustomerAuthorization` is unique per `(customerId,trackedCategoryId)`, so per-category collisions keep the stronger auth via `authRank`/`authSecondaryWins` (live VERIFIED > pending > lapsed/expired > rejected > none; later expiry breaks ties; else primary kept), deleting the loser so the re-point never trips the constraint. Spec: `customers.service.spec.ts` (`mergeCustomers (regulated authorizations)` block; prisma-mock gained `estimate`/`estimateItem`/`customerTagAssignment`).
- **VOID-payment AR fix (P5-12 fallout, money-critical — SYSTEMIC, multi-file)** — a bounced check flips its `InvoicePayment.status` to `VOID` (PaymentStatus = `DRAFT|PAID|VOID`) and reverts the invoice to OPEN/PARTIAL, so any all-time sum of payment amounts that ignored VOID _under-stated_ what the customer owed (and, for cash/income sums, over-stated receipts). An INVOICE-status filter does NOT neutralize it — a VOID payment rides on a still-open invoice; the VOID exclusion must be at the PAYMENT level. Canonical pattern = `buyer/statement.service.ts` `receivableAt`. A find→adversarially-verify sweep confirmed the bug across 4 services; all now `select` `status` + `.filter((p) => p.status !== "VOID")` (or `where.status:{ not: "VOID" }` on standalone `invoicePayment` queries):
  - **`customers.service.ts` (6 sites)** — `findAll` receivables, `getMyStatement`, `getStatementForOperator`, `exportCustomers` CSV, live-write `applyAdvancePaymentToInvoice` (over-stated `alreadyPaid` was blocking the advance-apply), `getIncomeChart`. Spec: `P5-12 — VOID payments excluded from AR snapshots`.
  - **`orders.service.ts`** — `assertWithinCreditLimit` (P5-08b credit gate; HIGH — a bounce let a buyer slip under their limit). Reused by `assertCreditForProjectedOrder` (P5-09). Spec: new VOID case in the `credit-limit guard (P5-08b)` block.
  - **`invoices.service.ts`** — `findAll` list `balanceDue`/`isOverdue` (HIGH, customer-facing; the list analogue of the `findOne` filter) + live-write `deletePayment` remaining-sum (was mis-recomputing status to PAID). Spec: `P5-12 — VOID payments excluded from balance math`.
  - **`bookkeeping.service.ts` (15 sites)** — `recordPayment` (write; blocked legit re-payment after a bounce), `getSummary`, `getMobileDashboard`, `getFinanceDashboard`/`getPeriodSummary`, AR-aging (`getArAgingInvoices`/`getArAgingDetails`), `getCustomerBalanceSummary`, `getInvoiceDetailsReport`, `getReceivableSummary`, `getBadDebtsReport`. Operator finance reports/dashboards.
  - **Follow-up (#265) — 2 sites the sweep's areas missed** (a whole-API grep audit of every `invoicePayment` aggregate + `.payments` relation-load caught these): `import/import.service.ts` payment-import status recalc (was mis-flipping live invoices to PAID/PARTIAL) + `invoices/invoice-pdf.service.ts` (the invoice PDF template `invoice-pdf-template.tsx:348` sums payments into the headline **customer-facing** balance-due — fixed at the query with a scoped `payments: { where: { status: { not: "VOID" } } }` include so the template is template-agnostically correct). Verified NOT bugs (already guarded / different model): `credit-notes.service.ts` (filters VOID, P5-13), buyer payment-history (display-only), routes reopen-guard + `bookkeeping/invoice.service.ts` (dead `Transaction`/`Payment` model), `vendor-bills` (`BillPayment`, payables — no bounce lifecycle).

### `drivers/`

- **controller** `drivers` — `me`/`@Patch me`/`me/location`; per-id: get/patch/status/delete, history, metrics. **Addon-gated 2026-08-28:** class-level `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, AddonGuard)` + `@RequireAddon("recurring_routes", "order_delivery", "developer_mode")` (either-gate — drivers are shared by both delivery features); `drivers.module.ts` imports `BillingModule`. See `billing/` → "Dispatch API gate".
- **service** — `findAll`, `findOne`, `create`, `update`, `updateStatus`, `updateLocation`, `remove`, `getMetrics`, `getRouteHistory`. side effects: Driver/DriverLocation writes; Socket.io location broadcast.
- **`remove()` is dual-role aware (2026-08-28)**: after the scheduled/in-progress run guard and the route/routeRun/deliveryMutation unlinks, it reads the linked `user.role` inside the tx and branches — a `DRIVER`-role login is still deleted with the profile, but **OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN dual-role staff keep their login** and get `canActAsDriver: false` in the same transaction. Previously `DELETE /drivers/:id` hard-deleted the linked User unconditionally: on an admin with restrict-FK children (messages, stock counts, device tokens) the user-delete failed → the whole tx rolled back → the driver row "came back", and when it succeeded it would have deleted the tenant's ADMIN LOGIN. Clearing the flag is what makes the deletion stick — `users.service.toggleDriverPermit` (unchanged, the explicit Settings → "Act as driver" opt-in) is the ONLY path that recreates the Driver row. Spec: `drivers.service.spec.ts` `describe("remove")`.
- **Ad-hoc trips home base (2026-08-24):** `Driver.homeLat`/`homeLng`/`homeAddress` (nullable, migration `20260904000000_adhoc_trips_and_fulfillment`) on `UpdateDriverDto`; `update()` best-effort geocodes a changed `homeAddress` via the shared `common/geocode.util.ts` — failure clears coords to `null` rather than throwing (never blocks the save). Consumed only by `trips.service.ts` `resolveOrigin`'s DRIVER tier. Written from the OPERATOR driver profile only (web `drivers/_components/EditDriverModal.tsx` "Home Base" section → `PATCH /drivers/:id`); no driver-facing UI reads/writes these fields.

### `products/`

- **controller** `products` — `@Get categories` (distinct non-null categories for the tenant; declared BEFORE `:id` or `findOne("categories")` 404s — see #194), `barcode/:barcode`, `@Get/:id`, import, `@Post bulk-assign-parent` (OPERATOR, before `:id` routes), `@Delete clear-all|bulk|:id`, `@Patch :id`, images add/remove. **`InvoiceItem.notes` (migration `20260711080000_invoice_item_notes`, APPLIED to prod, #207)** is copied from the order line by `invoices.service buildInvoiceItemData(li.notes)`; `OrderItem.notes` already persisted (`itemNote||notes`).
- **service** — `findAll`, `findByBarcode`, `findOne`, `create`, `update`, `bulkAssignParent`, `delete`, `import`, `add/removeImage`. `findAll` accepts `scanCode` (ListProductsDto, ignored when `search` set): OR of `contains` over name/sku/barcode/unitSku for EVERY normalizeScanCode candidate via `scan-search.ts` `buildScanSearchOr` (cap 5 candidates; spec `scan-search.spec.ts`) — makes the clients' scan-fallback rung decoder-independent (iOS 13-digit vs 12-digit codes stored in numeric product NAMES). Kept OUT of common/barcode-normalize.ts (byte-compared to its mobile mirror). `create` defaults a new product's `costingMethod` to the tenant `costing.method` via `resolveCostingMethod()` (WEIGHTED_AVERAGE→AVCO; explicit DTO wins; unset tenant → schema default FIFO) — ProductsModule imports SystemConfigModule (pos-cost-roles §1). **Variant inheritance in `create`:** when `parentProductId` set, unset DTO fields inherit the parent — `priceTier2..5` (parent tiers, NOT dto.pricePerUnit), `category`, `unitsPerBox`, `costingMethod` (bypasses tenant-default resolution), `standardCost`, `isTobacco` (inherited flag skips the addon re-check — parent already passed it); DTO-explicit always wins; standalone creates unchanged. **`bulkAssignParent(BulkAssignParentDto{parentProductId, assignments[{id,variantName}]})`** — parent must exist + be standalone (400 otherwise), loops SEQUENTIALLY through `update(id,{parentProductId,variantName,name:variantName})` per item in try/catch → `{succeeded[], failed[{id,reason}]}` (per-item failure isolation); consumed by web `useBulkAssignParent` (one POST, was N parallel PATCHes). side effects: Product/ProductImage writes; image upload; inventory ledger. **P5-01: `UpdateProductDto` gained `isFeatured/isNew/isDeal` merch flags** (pass-through in `update`). **#225 mobile-parity fixes:** `findAll` OUT_OF_STOCK filter now AND-s its stock disjunction with any search `where.OR` via `where.AND=[{OR:search},{OR:stock}]` instead of merging into one `where.OR` (was collapsing search AND stock-status into OR, matching every out-of-stock row regardless of search text — mobile products list/buyer catalog); `CreateProductDto`/`UpdateProductDto` `pricePerUnit`/`standardCost` gained `@Transform(toOptionalDecimalString)` (mobile sends these as JS numbers, not decimal strings — the bare `@IsDecimal()` 400'd every mobile product create/edit; tier fields already had the transform); `update()` now syncs `variantName = name` whenever a variant (has `parentProductId`) is renamed via the generic `name` field (mobile's edit form has no dedicated flavor input) — was leaving `variantName` stale. **P5-03:** `findOne` gains additive `stockAlertCount` (count of PENDING `StockAlert` rows for the product; separate tenant-scoped count, not `_count` gymnastics) — rendered by web operator product-detail as "N waiting for restock"; never surfaced to buyers. **(2026-07-19, in-flight regulated-isolation PR):** `ListProductsDto` gains `section?` (`"any"|"none"|<sectionId>` via `@Matches` — forbidNonWhitelisted requires the whitelist) → `findAll` filters `trackedCategoryId` (`{not:null}` / `null` / id; AND-folds when the internal buyer exclusion occupies the key); section NAMES resolve client-side from `useTrackedCategories` (deliberately NO relation include — findAll serves 10k-row fetch-alls). Companion: `tracked-categories` assign/unassign now clear `trackedSubcategoryId` (stale-subcategory invariant fix, movers-only count) and `inventory.getStockOverview` select += `trackedCategoryId`. **(2026-07-23) dual SKU (case vs unit):** new `Product.unitSku String?` (optional retail-unit code; NULL ⇒ unit shares case `sku`; `@@unique([tenantId,unitSku])`+`@@index`, migration `20260730000000_add_product_unit_sku`). `findByBarcode` widened to `findMany OR:[{barcode},{sku},{unitSku}]` + deterministic JS rank barcode>sku>unitSku (route/name kept → all `/products/barcode/:code` callers resolve EITHER code, no client change); `findAll` search OR += unitSku. create/update reject a `unitSku` colliding with any product's sku/barcode/unitSku, and reject a sku/barcode colliding with another product's `unitSku` (safe — new all-null column); sku↔barcode intentionally left collidable (pre-existing, findByBarcode ranks it deterministically). DTOs: create `@Transform(emptyToUndefined) unitSku?`, update `@Transform(emptyToNull) unitSku?` (empty clears). Invoice PDF prints `invoiceItemCode(p)=unitSku??barcode??sku` (`invoices/invoice-item-code.ts`; select+L132 in `invoice-pdf.service`). Vendor-bill `product-matcher.matchLine` + catalog select match unitSku; Zoho `import.service` second-chance unitSku lookup. Web/mobile forms label "Case code"/"Unit code". Specs: products.service.spec (ranking/collision), product-matcher.spec, invoice-item-code.spec.

### `promotions/` (P5-01 + P5-04 pricing)

- **controller** `promotions` `@Roles(OPERATOR)` — CRUD + `PATCH :id/active`. **service** `PromotionsService`: tenant-scoped promo CRUD (typed rules PERCENT/FIXED/QTY_BREAK/BUY_N_GET_M × scope ALL/CATEGORY/PRODUCTS, windowed; `validateRule` guards + **`assertNoZeroPricedProducts` — the $0.00 guard**) + `activeForCatalog(now)` (in-window active promos, flattened `productIds`) consumed by buyer `GET /buyer/promotions`. Prices NOTHING itself — the `@routeflow/pricing` evaluator applies rules. Models `Promotion`/`PromotionProduct` + migration `20260711000000_promotions_merch_flags` (additive). **Zero-price guard (2026-08-21, no migration):** `create`/`update` refuse a rule that would sell in-scope products for $0.00 with a 400 `{code:"PROMOTION_ZERO_PRICE", zeroPriceCount, inScopeCount, examples}` unless the DTO carries `allowZeroPrice: true` (confirmation flag, NEVER persisted). `mergedRule(dto, existing)` judges the rule a PATCH would LEAVE (a `{type:FIXED,value:35}` patch inherits the live ALL scope); the catalogue scan is `product.findMany` (isActive + scope pushed into SQL) → `scanPromotionZeroPrice`, skipped entirely by `ruleCanZeroPrice` for ordinary % rules AND for BUY_N_GET_M (structurally cannot zero — see `@routeflow/pricing` above). Counted on **list price** (`pricePerUnit`) — a lower tier price can still zero an uncounted product. `StoredPromotion.type`/`scope` are plain strings so a new Prisma enum member cannot break this file's compile. `setActive` is deliberately NOT gated (pause/resume is a switch, not a rule edit — grandfathered rows must stay pausable). Spec `promotions.service.spec` (`zero-price guard`) + `packages/pricing/src/pricing.spec.ts` (`promotion zero-price guard`). **BUY_N_GET_M (2026-08-21, WP2):** the `PromotionType` enum value is added by migration `20260823010000_add_buy_n_get_m_promotion` (additive `ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE`), and the rule **reuses `minQty` = N (buy qty, whole selling units) + `value` = M (free qty) — NO dedicated columns**, documented at the Prisma enum, `CreatePromotionDto` and the web admin form. `validateRule` gained a branch requiring BOTH to be integers ≥ 1 (else 400 "Buy N Get M promotions require an integer buy/free quantity (minQty/value) >= 1"), because a fractional or `< 1` N/M makes `floor(qtyUnits/(N+M))*M` nonsensical. Spec `promotions.service.spec`.
- **P5-04 pricing-time application (SHIPPED):** `@routeflow/pricing` gained pure `applyBestPromotion(base, promos, {productId,category,qtyPieces,qtyUnits})` + `promotionMatchesProduct` — PERCENT/QTY_BREAK = % off, FIXED = $ off per SELLING UNIT (box price when boxed), QTY_BREAK gated on total PIECES ≥ minQty, best-net wins (deterministic id tie-break), net floored at 0, only if it lowers. `orders.service` injects `PromotionsService`, `loadActivePromotions(role)` (CUSTOMER only, else `[]`), and `resolveBuyerLinePrice(product,tier,promos,qtyPieces,qtyUnits,rememberedPrice?)` (tier base → best promo → net unitPrice + originalPrice strikethrough + `PriceType.PROMO` + `freeUnits`; falls back to SPECIAL/STANDARD) — wired into **`create()`** line map AND the **`updateOrderItems()` CUSTOMER (buyer-merge) branch** (also **fixed a pre-existing bug**: merge billed LIST, now bills TIER). Invoice inherits net/originalPrice via `buildInvoiceItemData` (discount:0 — no double-count). `getCustomerPriceHistory` excludes `PriceType.PROMO` (transient promo price must not become the remembered operator price). `PriceType` enum += `PROMO` (migration `20260713000000_add_pricetype_promo`, additive). Regressions: `packages/pricing/src/pricing.spec.ts` (`applyBestPromotion`) + `orders.service.spec` (buyer-promo integration + staff-unchanged). Adversarial money review clean (0 money defects). Web cart + operator/buyer order-detail + invoice-detail render the PROMO strikethrough/badge; mobile cart UI deferred (P5-16) — the shared `@routeflow/pricing` money math was already correct there, only the UI never got the strikethrough/badge treatment.

### `orders/`

> **F06 updateOrderItems authorization + line build (2026-09-01, batch F06 — no migration).**
> Six register bugs in `updateOrderItems` (now L2735-3856) and its
> buyer-portal caller. New pure module **`orders/merge-items.ts`** — F30's `foldMergeItems` +
> `deriveUnitsPerBox` MOVED out of `orders.controller.ts` byte-identical (staff `POST /orders` merge
> unchanged, still pinned by REG-B199) plus TWO new denomination normalizers, because a box-UNAWARE
> line of a BOXED product states SELLING UNITS in `qty` while the fold counts loose PIECES:
> `normalizeBoxUnawareSnapshots(lines, liveUpbByProductId)` rewrites the STORED side (only for
> products the incoming payload denominates explicitly) and
> `normalizeBareIncomingSellingUnits(incoming, lines, liveUpbByProductId)` expands a BARE incoming
> qty (buyer selling units) into a box split when the STORED line is box-SPLIT — the two sides only
> agree while BOTH are bare, so each pair is reconciled on its own side, never wholesale. Changing: B47 buyer merge folds denomination-aware (`buyer.controller.createOrder`
> stops summing `Number(li.qty)+item.qty`); B51 CUSTOMER can no longer author/reprice/drop unlisted
> lines (deleteMany scoped to `productId not null` on the buyer path, client `!productId` entries
> skipped); B63 CUSTOMER items-edit refused 403 on OUT_FOR_DELIVERY/PARTIALLY_DELIVERED/DELIVERED
> (role-scoped — DRIVER post-dispatch edits unchanged; **ownership is settled BEFORE the status gate**
> so a non-owner keeps the bare 403 and cannot read another customer's order status off the message,
> and `computeEditWindow` mirrors the gate for CUSTOMER readers); B60 staff replaceAll preserves +
> rescales buyer-earned `promoFreeUnits` via the existing `rescaleBogoFreeUnits`/`bogoPromos`
> **and carries the counterpart's stored `originalPrice`/`overrideReason`/`overriddenBy` across the
> re-create** (a counterpart can be SPECIAL or MANUAL, not just PROMO — a no-op save must not erase a
> strikethrough base or who authorized the price); B78 new
> `applyBuyerMergeHeader` carries notes/urgent/date onto a merged order. ⚠️ The register's B60 premise
> was WRONG — `loadActivePromotions` is `[]` for staff by design (P5-04), so the "PROMO priced at full
> price" case is unreachable; the real defect is replace-all destroying the snapshot.
> ⚠️ **The B60 counterpart map is keyed to UNAMBIGUOUS pairs only** — exactly one pre-edit line AND
> one incoming line for that product. An order may legitimately hold several lines of one product and
> a `replaceAll` payload carries no line ids, so a productId-keyed map handed the snapshot (and the
> override attribution) to a sibling that never earned one: two prod-X lines echoed by a no-op save
> billed the second `5.00 x (5-1) = 20.00` instead of `25.00`. There is no sound N-to-N mapping to
> recover, so any other shape falls through to the pre-F06 behavior rather than guessing. Caught by
> the Fable final pass, pinned by `REG-B60: a sibling line of the same product never inherits…`,
> hand mutation-probed. ⚠️ `merge-items.ts`'s SCOPE comment names TWO gaps F06 deliberately did not
> close — a bare buyer qty for a product with NO stored line (`updateOrderItems` re-splits it as loose
> PIECES via a pre-F06 clause; needs a client-contract decision) and the STAFF scan-merge caller,
> still unnormalized on its stored side because R12 froze it byte-identical. Both filed. A third
> filed residual: the fold's price-survival contract is inert on the buyer path, because the CUSTOMER
> branch re-prices every catalog line and never reads `item.unitPrice` (so an operator's MANUAL
> courtesy price does not survive a buyer merge — the fix belongs in that branch, F07's region).

- **controller** `orders` (+ `route-runs`) — `active`, `price-history` (GET, OPERATOR, ?customerId → last-given price per product), `@Get/:id`, `:id/tracking`, `@Patch :id/status|items|urgent`, `:id/reopen`, `sweep-pending`, `force-consolidate/:customerId`, bulk/single delete; RouteRun stop complete. **(2026-08-24) `PATCH :id/fulfill-path`** (OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN, same precedent as the commission-rate override) sets `Order.fulfillPath`; rejects `OUT_FOR_DELIVERY`/`DELIVERED`/`CANCELLED` (400 — the shipping/routing decision is locked once fulfillment has started), and additionally rejects `→ SHIP` while the order sits on an **active** run (`routeRunStopId != null` + run `SCHEDULED`/`IN_PROGRESS`, message names the driver) — the dispatch sweep attaches orders while they're still `CONFIRMED`, so status alone would let a dispatched order read as "Shipped" while still standing as a stop on a driver's live run; stale attachments (finished/cancelled runs — `routeRunStopId` is never cleared) and `→ ROUTE` are unaffected. Same ON_ACTIVE_RUN predicate as `trips.service.ts checkEligibility`.
- **`Order.fulfillPath FulfillPath @default(ROUTE)`** (migration `20260904000000_adhoc_trips_and_fulfillment`, additive, REUSES the pre-existing `FulfillPath` enum — no redeclare, no backfill) on list/detail payloads. `ListOrdersDto.fulfillPath` filters `findAll` — omitted ⇒ no `where` key added (spec-pinned, so existing "all orders" list calls are unaffected). `create()` defaults `dto.fulfillPath ?? customer.fulfillPath ?? ROUTE`. `SHIP` orders are the ones eligible for `PATCH /orders/:id/fulfill-path`; ad-hoc trip eligibility (`trips.service.ts checkEligibility`) requires `fulfillPath: ROUTE`.
- **service** — `findAll`, `findOne` (lineItems `orderBy [position asc, createdAt asc]` — **R2 scan-order**: `OrderItem.position` [additive nullable, migration `20260727000000_add_orderitem_position`] is set from the client array index on every write [`create` map index; `updateOrderItems` replace branches use a running counter, the incremental/merge branch appends at `max(existing)+1`]; legacy/merge/change-request-append lines are null and sort LAST [PG NULLS LAST]; the invoice already reads in insertion order), `create`, `changeStatus(id,status,role)` (CUSTOMER can cancel PENDING), `updateOrderItems`, `markUrgent`, `reopen`, `sweepPending`, `forceConsolidate`, `getOrderTracking` (**F2-002 ownership gate**: route has no RolesGuard, so a CUSTOMER caller is checked against `order.customerId` via their `userId`→customer lookup — mirrors `findOne` — else any customer could enumerate order ids for another's route/driver/window). **(`completeStop` REMOVED — the dead `POST /route-runs/:id/stops/:stopId/complete` shadow handler + its `RouteRunDeliveryController` + `orders/dto/complete-stop.dto.ts` were deleted; the live plain-`/complete` handler is `routes.service.completeStop`, and at-door payment goes through `routes.service.completeWithPayment` — see routes.service below.)** **P5-08 open-order editing (R1-relaxed):** `updateOrderItems` HAD an **edit-window gate (G7)** — threw 409 `EDIT_WINDOW_CLOSED/DISPATCHED` once `routeRun.status !== "SCHEDULED"` — but **R1 REMOVED it**: items are now editable at EVERY live stage (CONFIRMED-even-dispatched / OUT_FOR_DELIVERY / PARTIALLY_DELIVERED / DELIVERED, **operator + driver**); only a CANCELLED order is rejected (BadRequest). A **`postDeliveryEdit`** (status ∈ OFD/PARTIALLY_DELIVERED/DELIVERED) **SKIPS** the pre-mutation `revertLinkedInvoicesForOrderEdit` (which throws on paid invoices) and, after the mutation, calls **`invoicesService.resyncOrderInvoicesForEdit(orderId)`** instead of `reconcileOrderDraftInvoice(basis:"order")` — rebuilding the order's finalized/paid/delivery-batch invoice(s) IN PLACE at the edited order qty (keeps payments, recomputes status/balance, re-syncs the regulated ledger, bills new/unprovenanced lines on the primary invoice, **bails on an unclean partition** — see invoices.service). `shouldRevert` (edit → PENDING re-confirm) now fires ONLY for a **non-dispatched CONFIRMED** order (dispatched/delivered edits stay in place — a revert would rewind the lifecycle). Drivers reach this via the mobile stop's "Edit items" (list pricing). After a successful edit it calls **`appendOrderRevision`** (private) which writes one immutable `OrderRevision` row (max+1 `revisionNumber` inside a `tenantTransaction`, snapshot COPIES stored subtotal/unitPrice/etc verbatim — never recomputes). `findOne` now includes `revisions` + `routeRun` and attaches a non-persisted `editWindow` via **`computeEditWindow(order, role?)`** (**R1**: `{editable: status≠CANCELLED, editableUntil:null, closedReason:STATUS|null}` — dispatch no longer closes it for staff; **B63/F06**: a **CUSTOMER** caller instead gets `{editable:false, closedReason:"DISPATCHED"}` on the module-level **`BUYER_EDIT_CLOSED_STATUSES`** [OFD/PARTIALLY_DELIVERED/DELIVERED] — the SAME list `updateOrderItems`' buyer gate refuses on, so the read descriptor can never promise an edit the write gate answers 403; single source of truth the web/mobile `canEdit` trust). **Regulated guard already re-runs on edit.** **P5-08b:** `updateOrderItems`' whole mutation phase (both role branches + recompute + guards + header update) is now wrapped in ONE `tenantTransaction` (25 `forTenant()`→`tx` sites) so a blocked edit rolls back cleanly; reference reads (`getTaxRate`/`loadActivePromotions`/`getCustomerPriceHistory`) are **hoisted BEFORE the tx** (they use separate pooled connections — running them inside the tx risked connection-pool-starvation deadlock under concurrent edits; adversarial-review catch). Two new private guards run inside the tx (DRAFT-exempt, before `order.update`): **`settleStockForEdit`** (WP1 2026-08-26, renamed from `assertStockAvailableForEdit` — **validate THEN write**, delta-based over the UNION of held/requested product ids: an increase over the order's already-held qty needs coverage [same guard as before] and is then decremented; a decrease/removed line is credited BACK to `currentStock` **CLAMPED to the undelivered remainder** `max(0, held − Σ deliveredQty)` [delivered goods physically left the warehouse and never come back through an edit — same call the at-door path makes by refusing `LINE_ALREADY_DELIVERED`; a fully-delivered line credits NOTHING and issues no write at all]; zero-delta products never lock/read/write; each delta is rounded to the `Decimal(10,3)` qty scale BEFORE the `!== 0` test so float residue can't write a phantom adjustment. `SELECT...FOR UPDATE` locks the touched **Product** rows first, mirroring `create()`. Signature `(db, order{id,status}, heldItems, finalActiveItems, user?)` — **`heldItems` is a PRE-EDIT snapshot read INSIDE the caller's transaction**, not the pre-tx `order.lineItems`: both call sites now `SELECT id FROM "Order" … FOR UPDATE` at the top of their `tenantTransaction` and then `tx.orderItem.findMany({where:{orderId}, select:{productId,qty,deliveredQty,status}})`, so a web PATCH racing an at-door approve of the same order serializes instead of settling a wrong-MAGNITUDE (permanently wrong) stock delta off a stale snapshot. Hard-block CUSTOMER/DRIVER `409 INSUFFICIENT_STOCK` with no write; operators warn-and-the-write-still-proceeds [may go negative]; DRAFT orders skip entirely; no StockMovement rows, matches `create()`. Applies on DELIVERED/OUT_FOR_DELIVERY edits too) + **`assertWithinCreditLimit`** (`creditLimit` null = no check; exposure MIRRORS `customers.getStatementForOperator` — Σ open-invoice balances [status ∉ PAID/VOID/WRITTEN_OFF, `total−Σpayments`] + Σ open-order totals [PENDING/CONFIRMED/OUT_FOR_DELIVERY], **deduped** so a mirror-invoiced order isn't double-counted, **edited order excluded** and represented by the projected total; `Transaction` deliberately unread; blocks ALL roles `409 CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`). Spec: orders.service.spec `updateOrderItems — edit window + revisions (P5-08)` + `credit-limit guard (P5-08b)` + `updateOrderItems — stock guard + settle (P5-08b + WP1)` + `WP1 settleStockForEdit — applies deltas over the union (a-j)` (direct-call cases on the private method, same pattern as `linePieceQty`; (i)/(j) pin the delivered clamp — fully-delivered removal writes nothing, 24-with-10-delivered reduced to 0 credits exactly 14) + `WP1 — boxed-line qty basis on CREATE (regression)` (pins `create()`'s mixed pieces/selling-units decrement basis that `settleStockForEdit` must reproduce). (SALE recording **delegates to `InventoryService.recordSale`** in-tx — no inline SALE writes; OrdersModule imports InventoryModule), **`getCustomerPriceHistory(tenantId,customerId)`** (returns `Record<productId,{lastPrice,listPriceAtTime}>` — only lines with `originalPrice` set; used to pre-fill the price field when scanning). side effects: Order/OrderItem writes; Transaction ledger; notifications; invoice auto-create on DELIVERED (per-batch lines use `computeLineSubtotal` for boxed proration + `discount: 0` for overrides — never `qty*unitPrice`); delivery-mutation tracking. **RF-4 per-category regulated tax:** `create()` fetches each regulated product's `TrackedCategory` (`categoryMap`) and sets `OrderItem.categoryTaxAmount = computeCategoryTax({taxType,rate,unitBasisQty: qtyPieces, lineSubtotal, priceIncludesTax})`; every order-total site (create + `updateOrderItems` + both merges + `approveChangeRequestAtStop`) folds `categoryTax = Σ line.categoryTaxAmount` into `total` (the `tax` column stays regular-only). Private helpers **`linePieceQty(li)`** (box-split ⇒ qty is pieces; else qty × snapshot unitsPerBox) + **`recomputeLineCategoryTaxes(tx, lines)`** (re-derives + persists each regulated line's `categoryTaxAmount` from stored subtotal/qty + its section config, returns Σ) run at every recompute site so edited/merged lines stay correct. Category tax is SECTION-level (subcategory never taxes).
  - **Tax rate unit (2026-08-18, money-critical):** private `getTaxRate()` reads SystemConfig `settings.taxRate` and returns `taxRateFractionFrom(stored)` (`common/tax-rate.ts`) — **the stored value is a PERCENT and is divided by 100 here**. It used to `parseFloat` the string and use it raw, i.e. a tenant setting of `"5"` (5%) taxed at 500×. All five call sites (create / updateOrderItems / both merges / approveChangeRequestAtStop) already multiply by a fraction and were NOT touched. The spec mock returns `"10"` (= 10%) and every money expectation is unchanged — if one shifts, a call site was misread.
  - **`updateOrderItems` replace vs merge (gotcha):** operator path picks `replaceAll = dto.replaceAll ?? allNewItems` (`allNewItems = items.every(no id)`). `replaceAll` ⇒ deleteMany + recreate (mobile full-list pattern); else merge — id-less items are CREATED (appended), absent items left untouched. **Web edit UI sends `replaceAll: false`** so an add-only diff doesn't wipe untouched lines (was the data-loss bug). OLD mobile clients omit the flag ⇒ legacy heuristic ⇒ replace-all; the current mobile editor sends an incremental diff (`lib/order-item-diff.ts`, `replaceAll:false`). CUSTOMER uses a separate always-replace branch. **A4 (2026-08-19, was live data loss): a diff-shaped payload (`replaceAll === false` OR any entry with `id`/`action`/`substituteProductId`) routes a DRIVER through the operator MERGE branch instead of the replace branch** — pre-A4 a driver saving ANY edit wiped every untouched line (diff entries carry no `productId` → deleteMany + recreate nothing). Driver posture kept: `unitPrice`/`overrideReason` stripped from catalog-linked entries before the merge (fresh adds price at catalog, qty edits keep the line's STORED price incl. operator overrides; unlisted lines keep client price — B13), and `replaceAll` is forced false for drivers in the merge branch. A diff-shaped CUSTOMER payload **400s** (both buyer portals send only full `{productId, qty}` lists). Specs: `orders.service.spec` "driver diff routing (A4)". **Boxed-denomination gate:** a boxed line's incoming `qty` is re-split (BOX proration via `normalizeBoxesPieces`) when the line is already piece-denominated (`boxes|pieces` set) OR — **buyer path only** (`isBuyerEdit`) — the product is BRAND-NEW to the order (no existing line); pre-existing box-UNAWARE selling-unit lines keep `qty*price`, DRIVER keeps the strict piece-denominated gate. Without the new-product clause a boxed product added via the buyer create/merge path (`buyer.controller.createOrder` collapses items to `{productId,qty}`) was stored as a BOX count → `unitsPerBox`× over-order/over-charge. Regression: `orders.service.spec` "customer ADD of a NEW boxed line … splits + prorates by the box".
  - **Substitution branch — box split + staff override (D1/B2/B3, 2026-08-19, money-critical):** the `item.substituteProductId` handler inside `updateOrderItems` no longer bills the substitute's LIST price at a bare inherited qty. **Split:** the incoming `boxes`/`pieces` are re-run through `normalizeBoxesPieces` against the **SUBSTITUTE's** `unitsPerBox` and honored **only when that substitute is case-packed** (`upb > 1`) — the client's split is denominated in the REPLACED product's box size, so multiplying it by a loose substitute's `unitsPerBox` (null/1) collapsed a whole-case line to its loose pieces (qty 0); a loose substitute falls back to `item.qty ?? existingQty` and writes `boxes/pieces/unitsPerBox = null` (it must never keep the replaced product's split). A resolved `qtyVal <= 0` `continue`s rather than writing a free line. **Override (B3):** when the caller is OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN **and** `item.unitPrice` is present **and** differs from the substitute's `pricePerUnit`, that net price is stored under the house convention — `priceType` DISCOUNTED below list / MANUAL above, `originalPrice` = the substitute's list price, `overrideReason` + `overriddenBy: user.sub` set. Non-staff callers (and an absent or list-equal `unitPrice`) keep the previous STANDARD/list behavior with `originalPrice: null` — B13: non-staff never set prices on this path. (Drivers/customers route through the always-replace branch and never carry `substituteProductId`, but the role check is explicit so a crafted DTO can't buy price control.) `subtotal` still comes from `computeLineSubtotal` over the NORMALIZED split, and the post-mutation `recomputeLineCategoryTaxes` + totals fold are untouched. Both clients now send the matching shape (see mobile `lib/substitute-line.ts` + `buildOrderItemDiff`, web `substituteDtoUpdate`). Spec: `orders.service.spec` → `updateOrderItems — incremental vs replace-all` (8 substitution cases: DISCOUNTED/MANUAL/equal-price/no-override regression pin/driver-ignored/loose-substitute/foreign-case-size normalize/qty-0 skip).
  - **Sale-flow terms/due-date override (2026-08-23, client-blocker fix):** `CreateSaleDto` gained optional `dueDate` (`@IsDateString`) + `terms` (≤5000) — the web/mobile "New sale" forms previously computed both on screen and never sent them, so every sale invoice got tenant-default +30 while the "Terms:" label came from an unrelated source (TenantConfig T&C text). `createSale` threads `{dueDate, terms}` into `createInvoiceFromOrder(orderId, txClient?, overrides?)` — overrides are the 3rd param (2nd is the pre-existing `txClient`); same optional-overrides shape on `createInvoiceFromOrderWithTenant` + `createPartialFromOrder` (all three keep byte-identical default behavior when absent; `createPartialFromOrder` precedence: explicit dto → override → default). Also UTC-safe calendar-date rendering in `messaging.helpers.formatDate` + `invoice-pdf-template.tsx` (UTC midnight was printing the previous day for US viewers; timestamps stay local). Specs: orders.service.spec (sale with/without explicit dueDate), messaging.helpers.spec (TZ regression).
  - **Backdated orders (2026-08-07):** `Order.orderDate` (migration `20260807000100_...`, `@@index([orderDate])`) is the BUSINESS date — the day the order actually happened — as opposed to `createdAt`, the entry timestamp, which several analytics still key on and which stays untouched. Accepted on `CreateOrderDto` + `CreateSaleDto` and gated by a private `parseOrderDate(raw, role)`: **OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN only** (`POST /orders` is reachable by CUSTOMER and DRIVER — buyers must never backdate), rejects a future date and anything older than 2 years. The future bound is END-of-UTC-day, not `Date.now()`: `YYYY-MM-DD` parses to midnight UTC, so a strict `> now` check would reject _today_ for any operator east of UTC. `createSale` (the already-delivered van-sale path) stamps `deliveredAt` from it instead of `new Date()`, and `changeStatus`→DELIVERED now stamps `deliveredAt: order.deliveredAt ?? order.orderDate ?? new Date()` (it previously wrote status only, leaving the column permanently null on every non-van-sale order). Backdating + auto-merge into a live order is rejected rather than silently dropping the date. Ripple: invoice dating → regulated period bucket (see `invoices/`).
  - **A dated order is never a merge participant (invariant):** `create()` writes `skipAutoMerge: orderDate != null || (options.skipAutoMerge ?? false)`. Without this a backdated order stays in the auto-merge set (`PENDING`, `skipAutoMerge:false`, no invoice/return/route) that `mergeAllPendingForCustomer` + the boot/hourly `sweepAllPendingOrders` cron sweep unattended — as a merge LOSER it is hard-deleted (`tx.order.delete`), destroying its orderDate and order number and re-billing those goods on today's invoice; as a WINNER it drags today's items into a past issueDate and, for regulated lines, a past filing bucket. `OrdersController.create` additionally **rejects `mergeChoice:"merge"` + `orderDate`** with a 400 (deliberately not a 409 — the web catch keys its merge modal off the `MERGE_CHOICE_REQUIRED` 409 payload and a second 409 would re-open it); that branch merges via `updateOrderItems` and never calls `OrdersService.create`, so `parseOrderDate` would otherwise never run and the date would vanish silently. The service-side `orderDate && !skipAutoMerge` guard survives as UX policy, no longer as the data-safety net.
  - **UPSELL (sell ABOVE catalog, SHIPPED):** an operator can price a line above list; stored as the existing override convention (net `unitPrice` + `originalPrice` = catalog + `discount:0`), **direction derived** `priceType===MANUAL && unitPrice>originalPrice` — no enum/migration (`isUpsellLine` in `@routeflow/pricing`). `create()` gained an `else if (isStaffRole && overridePrice>listPrice)` MANUAL branch; **`updateOrderItems` individual-edit anchors `originalPrice` to `product.pricePerUnit`** (was the prior net — the anchor fix that keeps an edited-down upsell classified correctly). **Customer redaction** (`common/upsell-redaction.ts` `redactUpsellForCustomer`): upsell lines have `originalPrice`→null, `priceType`→STANDARD, and `product.pricePerUnit`/`averageCost` dropped before any customer sees them — applied in `orders.findOne`/`invoices.findOne` (role CUSTOMER) **and** `buyer.controller` `getOrders`(OPERATOR pseudo-role)/`getInvoice`(no user)/`getActiveOrder`. **Sticky upsell**: `resolveBuyerLinePrice(…,rememberedPrice)` + `effectiveBuyerPrice(tier,list,remembered)` (remembered price ABOVE list overrides tier, precedes promos) make a remembered upsell the buyer's effective price in `create()` (+ price-race check), buyer-merge edit, and `buyer-catalog.service` (getCatalog/getProductDetail/getFavorites) — `BuyerCatalogService` now injects `OrdersService`. Carry-forward (`getCustomerPriceHistory`) already returns upsells; web/mobile pre-fill clamps relaxed. Operator sees a **green "Upsell"** indicator (web order/invoice detail + PriceEditRow; mobile order detail/edit/invoice). Specs: `pricing.spec` (isUpsellLine/effectiveBuyerPrice), `upsell-redaction.spec`, `orders.service.spec` (raise + anchor-guard), `invoices.service.spec` (upsell no-double-count), `buyer-catalog.service.spec` (sticky).
  - **Order shipping fee (2026-07-18):** optional `dto.shippingFee` on create/sale/update-items (`@Min(0) @Max(1_000_000)`); create total = `subtotal + tax + categoryTax − discount + shippingFee` (fee NEVER taxed, added after tax); ALL FIVE total-derivation sites read the STORED fee (create / mergeIntoWinner / forceConsolidateCustomer / updateOrderItems / approveChangeRequestAtStop) — an item edit without `dto.shippingFee` preserves the fee (wipe regression guarded by spec); only OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN dto values change it (`isStaffFeeEdit`); credit-limit guard consumes the fee-inclusive total; revision snapshots carry `shippingFee`. Specs: orders.service.spec "optional shipping fee" + "updateOrderItems — shipping fee".
  - **P5-09 post-dispatch change requests:** `ChangeRequest` model (types ADD_ITEM/CHANGE_QTY/REMOVE_ITEM/NOTE; status PENDING/APPROVED/DECLINED; FK-less actor snapshots; `payload Json`; `nextOrderId` plain pointer) fills the gap once G7 closes the direct-edit window (`routeRun.status !== SCHEDULED`) — `POST /orders/:id/change-requests` (OPERATOR/CUSTOMER/DRIVER; 409 `EDIT_WINDOW_OPEN` while still SCHEDULED, 409 `CHANGE_WINDOW_CLOSED` once COMPLETED/CANCELLED) creates a PENDING row; buyer twin `POST /buyer/orders/:id/change-requests`. `POST /orders/:id/change-requests/:crId/resolve` dispatches to **`OrdersService.approveChangeRequestAtStop`** (merge into the live order), a next-delivery draft-order path, or decline. **G6 first-resolution-wins**: every resolution path claims the row via `changeRequest.updateMany({where:{id,status:PENDING}})`; `count!==1` → 409 `CHANGE_REQUEST_ALREADY_RESOLVED`; for at-stop merge the claim runs INSIDE the same `tenantTransaction` as the line writes/guards so a guard throw rolls the claim back too. **Guards re-run on approval** (a CR itself is created with no guard checks): at-stop approve re-runs the regulated `authGuard` (pre-tx) + `settleStockForEdit` (DRIVER hard-blocks, operator warns — **WP1 2026-08-26: now also settles the delta into `Product.currentStock`**, not just validates; `order.status` is always PENDING/CONFIRMED/OUT_FOR_DELIVERY here per the `CHANGE_WINDOW_CLOSED` guard above, so the DRAFT no-op never triggers on this call site) + `assertWithinCreditLimit` (blocks all roles) inside the tx on the recomputed line set — same guards as `updateOrderItems`, not a new formula; next-delivery approve runs the regulated guard + `assertCreditForProjectedOrder` (stock deliberately unchecked for deferred lines). **Money invariant**: the merge reuses the stored-subtotal path — existing lines keep their stored `unitPrice`, every written subtotal comes from `computeLineSubtotal`/`normalizeBoxesPieces`/`roundMoney` (never `qty*unitPrice`), new lines price only via `resolveBuyerLinePrice`. **Stock note (superseded 2026-08-26 by WP1):** this used to say `Product.currentStock` is never touched here (recordSale-only at delivery) — that is no longer true; `settleStockForEdit` now decrements/increments it symmetrically with `create()`, no StockMovement rows either way (spec `EXTRA-1: an approved at-door qty INCREASE decrements currentStock by the delta`). This tx also opens with the `SELECT id FROM "Order" … FOR UPDATE` + in-tx `heldItems` snapshot described under `settleStockForEdit` above. Writes a `DeliveryMutation` row (ADD_ON/REFUSED) + post-commit `reconcileOrderDraftInvoice` + `appendOrderRevision(...,"CHANGE_REQUEST",...)`. Decline requires a reason and notifies the requester (reason included); approvals notify too — both via `NotificationsService`, failures swallowed. `findOne` now includes `changeRequests` (newest first). **P5-11:** `findAll`'s `include` gained a PENDING-filtered `_count.changeRequests` (list-badge count, additive).
  - **BUY_N_GET_M order engine (2026-08-21, WP2 — migration `20260823010000_add_buy_n_get_m_promotion`, additive enum value + nullable `OrderItem.promoFreeUnits`):** `resolveBuyerLinePrice(...,qtyUnits,...)` returns `freeUnits` from `applyBestPromotion`; a BOGO line keeps `priceType PROMO`, `unitPrice = base`, `originalPrice = null` and reduces only the SUBTOTAL via `computeLineSubtotal`'s `freeUnits` param, so 12 units @ $35 with 2 free = exactly $350.00. `qtyUnits` = whole SELLING units (boxes for a box-split line, qty otherwise) — loose pieces never count. Snapshot persisted as `promoFreeUnits` at `create`, the buyer replace-all edit, and the at-door ADD_ITEM new line; **cleared** on CANCEL/DELETE-zeroing, on a substitution (different product), and on a manual price override. Spec `orders-promo-bogo.spec.ts` (owner's table, pieces-never-count, exactness, best-of vs PERCENT, malformed rule ignored, invoice-verbatim).
    - ⚠️ **Free units are a function of the QUANTITY, so every path that re-quantifies a line at its stored (agreed) price RE-DERIVES them** via private **`rescaleBogoFreeUnits({promos,productId,category,unitPrice,storedFreeUnits,oldUnits,newUnits,canEarnNew})`** and writes the result back to `promoFreeUnits`: `mergeBoxedContributions` (both pending merges — the merged line earns free units for the COMBINED qty and the loser-only line carries the discount over), the `updateOrderItems` operator qty/price edit, and `approveChangeRequestAtStop`'s CHANGE_QTY + ADD_ITEM-onto-an-existing-line. Re-applying the snapshot verbatim was wrong in BOTH directions — a merge repriced 12+6 boxes at full price ($630 instead of $525) while keeping a stale `promoFreeUnits: 2`, and a door edit from 12 → 2 boxes billed **$0.00**. Rule: re-run the LIVE matching BUY_N_GET_M rule against the new whole-unit count (exact); if none still matches (promo ended after the sale) rescale the earned snapshot at its own rate, capped at the snapshot, so an agreed discount is never silently revoked. Promos are loaded (`loadActivePromotions(CUSTOMER)`, pre-tx) **only when a line already carries a snapshot** (or the merge is buyer-initiated, below), and the column is written only when it has something to say — so non-BOGO merges/edits issue no extra query and write byte-identical payloads. **`canEarnNew` — a line with NO snapshot may EARN free units only on `mergeAllPendingForCustomer(customerId,{buyerInitiated:true})`** (the buyer's own portal/app consolidation: `buyer.controller` createOrder both branches, `orders.controller` create for a CUSTOMER caller) **and only when EVERY contribution is buyer-priced** (`isBuyerPricedLine` = STANDARD/SPECIAL/PROMO with no `overriddenBy`): a buyer splitting one cart in two (3 + 3 boxes under a live buy-5-get-1) must still get the 1 free the merged 6 earn. The hourly `sweepAllPendingOrders` cron, `forceConsolidateCustomer` and every staff path leave it false, so an operator/driver-priced line is never silently discounted by a customer promo. Pins: the `a re-quantified line re-derives its free units` block in `orders-promo-bogo.spec.ts`.
    - ⚠️ **The INVOICE line carries the snapshot too** — `InvoiceItem.promoFreeUnits Int?` (migration `20260823020000_add_invoice_item_promo_free_units`, additive) + `CreateInvoiceItemDto.promoFreeUnits`. `buildInvoiceItemData` copies the order line's free units, allocated across partials by a cumulative floor (Σ partials == the order line's) — and on such a line the subtotal telescopes on the **PAID** qty (raw − free units × unit size) instead of the raw qty, so the partial's stored money equals `computeLineSubtotal(split, promoFreeUnits)` exactly (a raw-qty proration disagreed by up to one unit price, and `update()` re-prices from the snapshot, so a no-op DRAFT re-save moved the money: $145.83 → $175 on a 5-of-12-box partial). Σ partials is still exactly the stored subtotal; non-BOGO lines are byte-identical. Money still comes from the stored subtotal — the snapshot exists because `invoices.service update()` **delete-and-recreates every line from the payload** and re-prices it: without it a plain re-save of a delivered order's DRAFT billed 12 × $35 = $420 for the agreed $350. `create()`/`update()` both feed it to `computeLineSubtotal`'s `freeUnits` via private **`clampLineFreeUnits(free, boxes, qty)`** (whole SELLING units only; capped at `units − 1` so no edit yields an entirely free line), persist it back, and `recomputeOrderFromInvoices` mirrors Σ billed free units onto the order line beside the subtotal. Clients MUST round-trip it (web `invoices/[id]/edit`, mobile `invoiceLineDto`) — `forbidNonWhitelisted` means an absent field silently means zero. Pins: `invoices.service.spec.ts` "update() — boxed line proration" (BOGO re-save, boxed free CASES, stale-snapshot clamp, non-BOGO unchanged) + `buildInvoiceItemData` ("keeps a BOGO partial's stored money and free-unit snapshot in agreement", full-bill verbatim) + the `carries promoFreeUnits onto the invoice line` block in `orders-promo-bogo.spec.ts`.

  - **SHIP-path status wording (2026-08-24):** `changeStatus` gained one additive `else if` branch choosing carrier-facing copy ("Mark shipped" / "Out for Delivery" reads as "Shipped") when `order.fulfillPath === "SHIP"` — the notification branch, transition map, and DELIVERED branch are byte-unchanged; a SHIP order's DELIVERED invoicing is deep-equal to a ROUTE order's (same `createInvoiceFromOrderWithTenant` call, `deliveredQty` still governs billed qty).
  - **Qty-zeroing money-bug fix + line invariant (2026-08-25, sale-integrity phase 2 WP1):** `create()`'s item loop switched from a non-null `boxes`/`pieces` check to a POSITIVE check — `if ((item.boxes ?? 0) > 0 || (item.pieces ?? 0) > 0)` — before calling `normalizeBoxesPieces`; the web New-sale screen sends `boxes: 0, pieces: 0` on plain-qty lines, and the old non-null check let that overwrite a real `qty` with 0 (live bug: line stored `qty 0.000` at full `unitPrice`, order total $0, invoice ungeneratable). `boxes`/`pieces` are now always initialized `null` and only set from the split when the positive check passes (never stored as `0`). Immediately after qty/boxes/pieces are finalized, a hard invariant throws `BadRequestException` when `!(Number(qty) > 0)` — no order line can persist qty ≤ 0. The same non-null trap and fix are applied surgically inside `updateOrderItems`' add/replace/merge branches (new-item creation, individual-edit `editHasSplit` detection) via the same `(item.boxes ?? 0) > 0 || (item.pieces ?? 0) > 0` predicate. **Every one of those `normalizeBoxesPieces` calls also threads the caller's `qty` through** (`create`, `updateOrderItems`' new-item + `editHasSplit` branches, and the change-request `ADD_ITEM` add) — `normalizeBoxesPieces` only honours a `boxes`/`pieces` split when `unitsPerBox > 1`, so a product with `unitsPerBox: 1` (the web sends `boxes: 1` for ANY truthy `unitsPerBox`) fell into the non-boxed branch with no `qty` at all and derived 0: pre-invariant that persisted the live `qty 0.000` line, post-invariant it hard-400s a legitimate sale. With `qty` supplied the non-boxed branch keeps the operator's quantity and stores `boxes`/`pieces` null; boxed products are unaffected (the split still wins). Specs: `orders.service.spec` boxed/non-boxed zero-payload cases + regression pin for real box entry (byte-equal) + the all-zero-never-throws case + `createSale` with `boxes:0,pieces:0` lines generates a non-empty invoice.
  - **Universal one-step demotion + reopen DELIVERED + delete-any (2026-08-25, sale-integrity phase 2 WP3):** `changeStatus`'s `allowed` map gained `PENDING → DRAFT` and `DELIVERED → [CONFIRMED, PARTIALLY_DELIVERED]` (owner reversed BUG-ORD-01: reopening a delivered order is legal again) — every state may now step back one stage. The new transitions join the requires-reason demotion set and are staff-only for free: the role gates that run BEFORE the map already limit CUSTOMER to cancelling its own PENDING/DRAFT order and DRIVER to `PENDING → CONFIRMED`. A DELIVERED demotion additionally (a) 409s (`ConflictException`) when `order.routeRunStopId` points at a `RouteRunStop` whose `status === "COMPLETED"` — one extra lookup, only on this transition, message sends staff to reopen the run stop instead (that path reverses stock and payments correctly) — and (b) writes `deliveredAt: null` so a reopened order leaves delivered-on-date reports. The DELIVERED branch's side effects (draft reconcile / auto-invoice / credit settle) are keyed on `dto.status === DELIVERED` and so fire on NEITHER demotion. Mirrored byte-for-byte by `apps/mobile/lib/order-status-flow.ts`'s `ORDER_STATUS_TRANSITIONS`/`demotionRequiresReason`. — **Delete-any:** `deleteOrder(id, user?)` / `bulkDeleteOrders(ids, user?)` (`user` optional so change-requests' abandoned-draft cleanup keeps its single-arg call). Money, not status, is the gate now: staff (OPERATOR/TENANT_ADMIN, and any internal caller passing no user) may delete an order in ANY status; a non-staff caller keeps the old `Only DRAFT, PENDING, or CANCELLED` allowlist verbatim. Two 409s precede the delete — `Return` rows against the order (the one restrict-linked child; every other one cascades or is deleted in the transaction, so a delivered-and-partly-returned order would otherwise blow up with a raw FK error) and blocking payments off `cancelImpact` (external cash only; #341's wallet behaviour is preserved — credit-note/advance money is still handed back and the delete proceeds). The old `assertCancellableOrThrow` 400 became that ConflictException so the client shows "void the invoice first". Controller routes `changeStatus`/`bulkDelete`/`remove` are widened to TENANT_ADMIN. Specs: `orders.service.spec` "changeStatus — one-step-back demotions" + "deleteOrder — delete-any".
  - **F07 — the status machine and its teardown CONSERVE what they touch (2026-09-01, money/stock-critical; B56/B64/B65/B105/B108/B116).** Cancel and delete used to destroy value silently. Now: **(B56)** `cancelImpact` also returns **`deliveredUnits`** (3-dp Σ `deliveredQty` over non-CANCELLED lines) and `canCancel` additionally requires `deliveredUnits <= 0.001`, so DELIVERED/PARTIALLY_DELIVERED → CANCELLED is REFUSED once any goods left the warehouse — the rule `settleStockForEdit` already enforced, now consulted by the cancel guard. `assertCancellableOrThrow` throws the external-payment message FIRST (byte-identical) and the delivered-goods message otherwise. **(B64)** the CANCELLED branch credits the creation-time decrement back through `settleStockForEdit(tx, order, activeItems, [])` — clamped to `max(0, qty − deliveredQty)`, same FOR UPDATE lock, **no StockMovement rows** (matches `create()`/edit) — and then flips surviving `OrderItem`s to CANCELLED AFTER the invoice voids; `reopenOrder` reads those item-CANCELLED lines BEFORE reverting them and re-decrements exactly what the cancel credited. ⚠️ **The credit and the mark are ONE decision** (`cancelReturnsStock = order.status !== DRAFT`): a DRAFT never decremented at create, so marking a line it never credited would make cancel→reopen take stock the order never held. A pre-F07 cancel left lines untouched, so reopen re-decrements nothing for it — both eras stay consistent. **(B65)** `deleteOrder`'s per-invoice teardown now calls `ledger.reverseInvoiceEntries({invoiceId, db: tx})` FIRST in each iteration (OrdersModule imports RegulatedModule; OrdersService injects `RegulatedLedgerService` — the DI addition ripples to every spec that builds the testing module). **(B105)** the DELIVERED no-draft path AWAITS `createInvoiceFromOrderWithTenant` (3 attempts, ConflictException only) instead of fire-and-forget, and reports failure as a `ConflictException` naming the real control "Generate Invoice (full order)" — **raised AFTER the notification block**, so an invoicing failure never suppresses the customer's delivery push; the DELIVERED write is NOT reverted. **(B108)** the delivery credit settle retries 3× (100/300ms) and logs at ERROR; the substantive half is in `invoices.service` (see below). **(B116)** the create stock tx opens with `{timeout: 20000, maxWait: 5000}` and the per-line decrement loop is ONE set-based `UPDATE "Product" … FROM (VALUES …)` with **per-product qty aggregated first** (the join applies at most one row per key, so two lines of the same product MUST decrement their sum) and an explicit `tenantId` predicate (raw SQL bypasses the tenant proxy the ORM call had). Cancel/reopen transactions carry `timeout: 15_000`, matching the other `settleStockForEdit` call sites. Specs: `orders.lifecycle-conservation.spec.ts` (the red-gate half) + `orders.lifecycle-conservation.pins.spec.ts` (green-pre-fix pins, incl. the DRAFT flip guard and the P2002 create-retry pin the test plan had wrongly assumed existed); 9/9 mutation probes caught.
  - **Delivery-date picker (2026-08-25, sale-integrity phase 2 WP4):** `CreateSaleDto.deliveredOn?: string` (`@IsDateString`) REPLACES `deliveredNow`'s binary in `createSale` when present (`deliveredNow` still honoured alone for older/mobile clients; when both are sent `deliveredOn` wins). A date > end-of-today UTC ⇒ deliver-later: the order stays PENDING with its DRAFT mirror invoice and `requestedDeliveryDate = deliveredOn`. A past/today date ⇒ delivered: it runs through `parseOrderDate` (so backdating keeps its staff-only gate and 2-year floor) and becomes the order's `deliveredAt` — overriding `orderDate`'s fallback — then the invoice is generated and issued as usual. Specs: `orders.service.spec` `createSale` WP4 block (past/future/today, non-staff backdate 403, deliveredOn-absent path unchanged).

  - **F03 payment-status truth (2026-08-31, batch F03 — no migration):** `invoices/payment-predicates.ts`
    (NEW) is the single source of truth — `CONFIRMED_PAYMENT` = `{status:"PAID"}` and
    `sumConfirmed(rows)`. ⚠️ **Every payment SUM narrows to PAID; every payment LISTING keeps all rows
    and carries `status` through to the renderer** — narrowing a listing hides the DRAFT row the badge
    exists to show, which is the opposite of R2. Routed: `invoices.service` (findAll `balanceDue`,
    `recomputeStatus` feeders, the PDF query), `bookkeeping.service` (three dashboards PLUS `getSummary`,
    `getArAgingInvoices`, `recordPayment` and the balance reports), `customers.service`,
    `buyer/statement.service`, `payment-requests.service`. ⚠️ `getCashFlow` and
    `getPaymentsReceivedReport` are **byte-untouched on purpose** — they were already PAID-only and are
    the reference reads the rest were aligned to. The original three-function fence WAS the defect: it
    left the same tenant reading $200 outstanding on `/bookkeeping/summary` and $500 on
    `/bookkeeping/dashboard`. ⚠️ Fixtures asserting `status: "RECORDED"` were corrected to `"PAID"` —
    `PaymentStatus` is exactly `DRAFT|PAID|VOID`, so RECORDED was a fiction no writer can produce and it
    was masking three unrouted sites. Documents: `invoice-pdf.service`/`invoice-pdf-template` and
    `email.service` sum confirmed rows only, list DRAFT rows under a "Pending confirmation" label, and a
    reminder now demands the BALANCE not the total; the shared item type is `invoices/invoice-pdf-item.ts`
    (`originalPrice`/`priceType`/`promoFreeUnits` for the strikethrough + N-free disclosure).
    ⚠️ **`T-B50s`, never a bare `REG-B50` token** — `REG-B50` also selects F04's shipped
    `pricing-parity.spec.ts` tests, which makes a red gate structurally incapable of reporting 0 passed.
    It pins the F04 oracle cap on the SERVER telescope (`Math.min(billedThrough(x), basisQty)` on BOTH
    points); mutation-verified by hand — removing the cap from ONE point goes red. `scripts/repair-f03.mjs`
    is the D4 repair tool (dry-run default, `--execute` + `--i-have-a-fresh-backup`, per-row
    compare-and-set that SKIPS drifted rows, JSONL log under `local-assets/`); **it has never been run
    against production.**
  - **F30 server half (2026-08-31, batch F30 — migration `20260910000000_order_idempotency`, additive):**
    - **`Order.idempotencyKey String?` + `@@unique([tenantId, idempotencyKey])` (R8, REG-B196).** `POST /orders`
      reads an `Idempotency-Key` **header** (`@Headers`, `@ApiHeader(required:false)`) — never a DECLARED
      body field, so a client can't set it as body input; the controller threads it onto the dto object and
      `create()` reads it back as `(dto as any).idempotencyKey`. ⚠️ That cast is the contract, not an
      oversight — declaring it on `CreateOrderDto` would re-open the body as a second source. ⚠️ The header is raw,
      entirely client-chosen input: the controller's private **`normalizeIdempotencyKey(raw)`** bounds it at the edge
      (trim; blank ⇒ absent; else a single printable-ASCII token ≤ 128 chars or 400) — unbounded it lands in a btree
      unique index and an oversized one 500s with a raw index-row-size error. ⚠️ And a key match is NEVER a replay on
      its own: `create()` resolves the caller's own `customerId` FIRST, then looks the key up, and a hit belonging to a
      DIFFERENT customer is a client-side collision ⇒ `ConflictException`, not a replay (the bare lookup handed buyer B
      buyer A's order — business name, every line, every unit price — while B's own order was never written). The P2002
      branch applies the same ownership gate. A caller with NO tenant (SUPER_ADMIN, admitted by RolesGuard's hierarchy,
      for whom `forTenant()` returns the UNSCOPED client) is refused the key path outright. Service helpers
      `findOrderIdByIdempotencyKey(key, customerId)` / `recordIdempotencyKey(orderId, key)` (both `forTenant()`-scoped to
      match the compound unique; `recordIdempotencyKey` SWALLOWS P2002 — the merge it raced is already
      written, so a lost claim must not fail a completed write, and an order holds at most one key, the most
      recent operation's); `create()` persists the key and treats the P2002 on that unique as a REPLAY
      (fetch + return the existing order, not a second one). ⚠️ Postgres treats NULLs as distinct (mirrors the `orderNumber` unique), so every
      pre-existing row and every client that sends no key is untouched — no backfill, no writer before this deploy.
      ⚠️ **The staff auto-merge branch in the CONTROLLER returns without ever reaching `create()`**, so it checks
      and stamps the key itself, and stamps it only AFTER the fold landed (a merge that threw must re-run, not replay).
    - **`foldMergeItems(existingLines, incoming)` + `deriveUnitsPerBox(row)` (R11, REG-B199, money-critical),**
      exported from the pure module **`orders/merge-items.ts`** — MOVED byte-identical out of
      `orders.controller.ts` by F06 (in-flight note above), which added `normalizeBoxUnawareSnapshots` +
      `normalizeBareIncomingSellingUnits` beside them (buyer-merge only — the staff scan path's bare qty
      really IS loose pieces, so `orders.controller.ts` calls neither). Two callers: `orders.controller.ts` (staff `POST /orders` auto-merge) and `buyer.controller.ts`
      (buyer cart merge) — **never re-inline a second copy.**
      A box-split line stores qty in PIECES (`boxes×upb + pieces`);
      summing that flat against a plain qty fed the wrong number into box-price proration (a 2-box + 5-piece line
      became 7 BOXES, repriced ×`unitsPerBox`). Boxes fold with boxes, pieces with pieces, an incoming item with no
      box data is loose pieces, `normalizeBoxesPieces` re-derives the split; `unitPrice`/`notes` are preserved from
      the EXISTING line; unlisted (`productId: null`) lines pass through as their own items. ⚠️ `OrderItem.unitsPerBox`
      is null on pre-snapshot rows — `deriveUnitsPerBox` falls back to the row's own `(qty − pieces) / boxes` rather
      than reading null as 0 (which collapsed a boxed line to its loose pieces). The result is the order's COMPLETE
      new line set, so the caller passes `replaceAll: true` explicitly.
    - **Customer-level merge serialization (R11, REG-B199, PR-2 2026-09-03):** the staff auto-merge branch of
      `create()` runs its read→fold→write section inside `withAdvisoryLock({ family: "order-merge", key:
dto.customerId, mode: "wait", waitMs: 10_000 })` from `src/common/db-locks.ts` (orders.controller.ts:~147);
      `mergeAllPendingForCustomer` (orders.service.ts:~824) and `forceConsolidateCustomer` (orders.service.ts:~1169)
      take the same customer-keyed lock at `waitMs: 20_000`; buyer `createOrder` (buyer.controller.ts:~547) also
      uses `waitMs: 10_000` (same mobile-client-abort reasoning as the staff branch). Request-path post-commit
      callers (the sibling sweep / post-create auto-consolidation) pass `{ lockMode: "try" }` instead — a
      contended lock is skipped with a warning, never blocks the request. The lock is a Postgres
      advisory lock on a dedicated pinned pg connection, so it is cross-replica; a 55P03 lock timeout surfaces as
      409 `MERGE_IN_PROGRESS`. The former in-process `withOrderMergeLock`/`mergeLocksByOrder` Map is DELETED —
      never re-add a second in-process lock (orders.controller.ts:~58-61 forbids it). ⚠️ Sibling sweeps outside
      the lock stay outside it by design. Money math, `foldMergeItems` and `updateOrderItems` are byte-identical
      to pre-PR-2; `updateOrderItems` is never handed a tx. `@Cron` jobs still assume one replica until the PR-3
      cron leader lock — the scaling guard is the comment in `apps/api/railway.toml` [deploy].
    - **`UpdateOrderItemsDto.replaceAll` is EXPLICIT-ONLY (R10, REG-B198).** The legacy heuristic
      (`replaceAll = dto.replaceAll ?? allNewItems`, "every item lacks an id ⇒ replace") is GONE — that is exactly the
      shape of a mobile per-scan "just add these" PATCH, which wiped the order. Omitted or `false` ⇒ incremental merge.
      ⚠️ This supersedes the "replace vs merge (gotcha)" bullet above for the operator path.
    - **No silent line drops in `updateOrderItems` (R9, REG-B197).** A pre-scan inside the transaction resolves every
      NEW catalog-linked add before anything is written; unresolvable ids (stale cache, cross-tenant id filtered to null)
      abort the WHOLE request with a `BadRequestException` instead of the old `if (!product) continue`, which returned
      HTTP 200 with a dropped line. The in-loop `!product` case now throws too (belt-and-braces — never a silent drop).
      ⚠️ ONE batched `tx.product.findMany({ id: { in: distinct ids } })` + a Map serves BOTH the pre-scan and the add
      loop below it (the same shape the replace-all branch above uses) — a `findUnique` per line in each ran the same
      read twice, 60 sequential round-trips for a 30-line scan batch, all inside the transaction already holding
      `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the order.
    - Specs: `orders/orders.scan-hardening.spec.ts` (NEW — idempotent create/replay, the merge fold's denominations,
      `replaceAll` explicit-only, the unresolvable-add abort) + extensions in `orders/orders.service.spec.ts`.
      ⚠️ Its last block (T-B200/R12) tests **`BuyerController.scanProduct`**, not this module — R12 shipped as the
      buyer-catalog rung, so the block moved there rather than pinning `ProductsController` (whose staff-only
      `@Roles(OPERATOR, DRIVER)` it now asserts stays closed). Its idempotency-scoping case binds the fake's tenant
      partition to `forTenant()` and leaves the ROOT prisma client unscoped, so a lookup that skips `forTenant()`
      reads across tenants in the test exactly as it would in production.
  - **F11 (B129/B146 contract).** `getOrderTracking`: a CANCELLED run ⇒ `{ status, tracking: null }`
    (checked immediately after the existing `!order.routeRunStop` short-circuit) — the buyer card
    must never show a driver, stop number or "you're next" for a called-off run; rows stranded
    before the deploy stay covered by this branch until the D4 repair frees them. A SKIPPED
    own-stop on a live or completed run keeps the FULL payload (`stopStatus: "SKIPPED"`,
    `stopsAhead` unchanged) — the server invents no "skipped shape", the client branches on
    `stopStatus`. Spec `orders.service.tracking-contract{,.pins}.spec.ts`.

- **F13 / REG-B48 — two buyer-pricing methods are now PUBLIC, shared with `OrderTemplatesService`.**
  `loadActivePromotions(role)` and `resolveBuyerLinePrice(product, tierForProduct, promos,
qtyPieces, qtyUnits, lastPrice, boxCtx)` dropped `private`; bodies are byte-identical. A standing
  order is the CUSTOMER's order whoever triggers it (06:00 cron, operator "Generate now", buyer
  "Reorder"), so `order-templates.service.generateOrder` prices through these instead of keeping a
  second, list-price-only pricing writer. **They are a contract now — do not narrow them back, and
  do not fork a second copy.** Callers: `orders.service.create` (buyer path) and
  `order-templates.service.generateOrder`.

### `routes/` & `route-optimization/`

- **Addon-gated 2026-08-28:** `RoutesController`, `RouteRunsController`, `RouteOptimizationController` and `RouteTemplateOptimizationController` all carry class-level `AddonGuard` (after `JwtAuthGuard`) + the either-gate `@RequireAddon("recurring_routes", "order_delivery", "developer_mode")` — an ad-hoc delivery materializes a route template + run, so a delivery-only tenant must keep these. `route-optimization.module.ts` gained `BillingModule` (routes.module.ts already had it). Pinned by `routes/dispatch-addon-gate.spec.ts`; details in `billing/` → "Dispatch API gate".
- **routes controller** `routes` (+ `route-runs`) — customer-assignments, live, route get/patch/delete, stops add/reorder/remove; runs: `my-runs`, `my-stats` (declared before `:id`), get, stop complete / complete-with-payment, stop patch, run patch/status/delete. **complete-with-payment carries `DriverPaymentsGuard`** (`routes/driver-payments.guard.ts`, spec `routes.controller.security.spec.ts`): at-door money collection is per-tenant opt-in via the `driver_payments` TenantAddon (owner 2026-08-24: affa yes, bb-distro no) — body-aware, 403s only `payment.amount > 0` without the addon; $0/on-account completions pass for every tenant (they need this endpoint's delivered-basis invoice reconcile). Client constant `DRIVER_PAYMENTS_ADDON` in `packages/types`; mobile `useDriverPayments()` hides the collection UI (payment.tsx/short-pick/stop index relabel to on-account); demo-seed grants the addon to the demo tenant.
- **service** — `findAll`, `findOne`, `create`, `updateRoute`, `addStop`, `reorderStops`, `deleteStop`, `createRun` (passes `podPhotoUrls: []`), `completeStop(runId,stopId,mutations,podPhotos)`, `completeWithPayment`, `attachPodArtifact`, `getStopPod`, `updateRunStop`, `getMyRuns`, `getMyStats`, `updateRunStatus`, `reopenStop` — reverses a delivery's mutations, item/order/stop state and, since F05, **clears `settlementNote`/`settlementVariance`** (reopening changes what was owed, so the old reconciliation is void and its gates re-arm). ⚠️ **Since F10 it writes NO stock at all** — the old "compensating positive-qty SALE" loop was a double-credit, not a reversal (delivery never decremented; `currentStock` is settled at order creation and re-settled only on item edit), so it was deleted outright; see the F10 bullet below. Plus `settleRun` and the private `getRunCashCollections` (F05, below).
- **Durable POD artifacts (2026-08-28, no migration):** `RouteRunStop.podPhotoUrls`/`signatureUrl` now hold STORAGE KEYS (`tenants/<tenantId>/pod/<stopId>/<kind>-<artifactId>.<ext>`) instead of device-local strings. Pure helpers `routes/pod-artifacts.util.ts` (`parseImageDataUrl` base64+percent-encoded, `isPodStorageKey` tenant-pinned, `podArtifactKey`; spec'd). Ingest: both completion paths call `ingestPodCapture` (after the idempotency check, before the tx) — a data-URL `signatureUrl`/`podPhotoUrls` entry is sharp-compressed (`compressImage`; SVG→raster support pinned by a `compress.util.spec.ts` case) and stored via `StorageService`, the key persisted; legacy strings (`file://`, `"native-captured"`) pass through unchanged so old driver builds keep completing. New endpoints: `POST /route-runs/:id/stops/:stopId/pod-artifact` (`dto/attach-pod-artifact.dto.ts` — JSON `{kind, dataUrl, artifactId?}`, deliberately NEVER multipart so the mobile offline queue can replay it; idempotent per artifactId; pre/post-completion both allowed) and `GET .../pod` (presigns own-tenant pod keys ONLY, passes data URLs through, legacy → `legacyPhotoCount`/`signatureCaptured` flags + age/ID capture fields). Regulated signature gate untouched (still an opaque non-empty string). `RoutesModule` += `StorageModule`. Mobile signature = stroke-built SVG data URL (`apps/mobile/lib/pod-artifacts.ts`), sent inline on completion; photos attach individually pre-completion. Specs: `routes.service.spec.ts` POD block (9 cases) + `pod-artifacts.util.spec.ts`.
- **Driver Collect-payment now records the InvoicePayment (money-critical fix)** — `completeWithPayment`'s payment block previously looked up the invoice by a CLIENT-supplied `dto.payment.invoiceId` and decremented a **non-existent `Invoice.balance` column**. But mobile always sent `invoiceId: undefined` (Order has NO `invoiceId` scalar; the run/stop payload never carries one), so the guard was always false and **every driver-collected cash/check/card payment silently vanished** (stop completed, customer told "paid", no `InvoicePayment` row). Fix = new tx-safe **`InvoicesService.recordDeliveryPaymentInTx(tx, orderIds, amount, method, reconcileOrderIds?)`** that resolves the invoice SERVER-side: per delivered order ensure a DRAFT invoice (`createInvoiceFromOrder` when none), then **reconcile it to the DELIVERED quantity** (`reconcileOrderDraftInvoice(basis:"delivered")` — bills `OrderItem.deliveredQty` so short/refused lines don't over-bill), then apply the lump-sum across the orders' **PAYABLE** invoices (`DRAFT|SENT|PARTIAL|OVERDUE` — excludes VOID **and** terminal WRITTEN_OFF, so a bad-debt invoice can't swallow the cash) **oldest-first** (base before regulated `-R#` siblings), each **`FOR UPDATE` row-locked then paid FRESH** (serializes a concurrent back-office payment), capped at each remaining, **finalizing DRAFT→SENT** (recomputeStatus treats DRAFT as terminal) and recording each as an `InvoicePayment` + `recomputeStatus` over non-VOID sums — NEVER a `balance`. Refuses `CREDIT_NOTE`/`ADVANCE` (need a source-balance debit). **Delivered-basis is double-gated (adversarial-review catches): (1)** reconcile only orders in `reconcileOrderIds` — the subset ACTUALLY delivered this completion (`completeWithPayment` builds `deliveredOrderIdSet` from `dto.deliveries`' orderItems and passes it) so an order merely linked to the stop but delivered elsewhere (deliveredQty 0) is never zeroed; **(2)** the delivered-basis rebuild is **sibling-aware** — **`reconcileOrderDeliveredInvoices(orderId, tx)`** rebuilds EACH of the order's open DRAFTs from ONLY its own lines (the `InvoiceItem.orderItemId` provenance `createSplitInvoices` established) at `deliveredQty`, so a regulated `SEPARATE_INVOICE` order's `-R#` sibling bills its delivered qty and the base never absorbs the regulated line (no double-bill); a refused line bills 0. Order tax is re-allocated across siblings by delivered subtotal (remainder to the largest → Σ==order tax on a clean delivery); each line's `invoicedQty` is SET to its sibling's delivered qty. Single-group orders are byte-identical to `reconcileOrderDraftInvoice(basis:"delivered")`. **Clean-partition GUARD (adversarial-review catch):** it fetches ALL non-void order invoices and BAILS (returns null, leaves invoices as created — never a double/over-bill) if provenance isn't a clean per-line partition — a line shared by two open drafts, or also billed by a finalized/delivery-batch invoice (reachable via `createPartialFromOrder` or a prior group-unaware `basis:"order"` reconcile); rebuilding those from a prior of 0 would double/over-bill, so the conservative full-qty bill wins there. Feeding it: `completeWithPayment`'s delivery loop writes `OrderItem.deliveredQty` per line (`REFUSED`→0 else `quantityDelivered`); mobile `payment.tsx` sends an EXACT-qty deliveries array for every line of every stop order (no `Math.round` — the DTO accepts decimals and billing is now on deliveredQty). `StopPaymentDto.invoiceId` is `@IsOptional()` (server ignores it); mobile sends `{amount, method}` only. `RoutesModule` gained an `InvoicesModule` import (acyclic). Specs: `invoices.service.spec` `recordDeliveryPaymentInTx` (10 cases) + `reconcileOrderDeliveredInvoices` (9 cases: split clean / short-pick / refused, tax invariant Σ==order total, single-group, no-draft, 2 guard-bails [shared-line + finalized-share], base-SENT-clean) + `routes.service.spec` (payment WITHOUT invoiceId → resolver called; delivered subset passed). **Remaining deferrals (not double/under-bills):** (a) ~~the regulated-sales ledger is NOT re-synced to delivered qty~~ **FIXED** — the sibling-aware reconciles now re-sync the regulated-sales ledger via **`resyncInvoiceLedger(invoiceId, orderId, items, db)`** in `rebuildSiblingDrafts` (reverse the invoice's prior SALE rows via `reverseInvoiceEntries`, then `writeSaleEntries` from the rebuilt items → a short/refused regulated line reports its delivered qty, current-period). Called ONLY from the sibling-aware paths (NOT the group-unaware legacy single-draft rebuild, which would double-count a folded line vs a live sibling SALE). **`reverseInvoiceEntries` is now NET-AWARE** — it aggregates each line's remaining un-reversed net (Σ signed SALE+REVERSAL rows) and reverses only that, so a void/reconcile of a partially-RETURNED line reverses the remainder (not the full sale, not skip); a fully-reversed line nets ~0 and is skipped (double-void/full-return no-op). Fixes the old "any reversal ⇒ skip" over-report (void-after-partial-return now nets to 0). The reconcile RE-SYNC preserves prior returns: `resyncInvoiceLedger` calls `reverseInvoiceEntries({preserveReturns:true})` — which EXCLUDES return/credit-note reversals (those carrying a `returnId`/`creditNoteId`) from the remaining, cancelling only the sale-record + prior re-sync reversals, so a re-reconcile after a return nets to (delivered − returned), idempotent (a prior re-sync reversal has no returnId → counted). Void/delete keep `preserveReturns` false (reverse full net → 0). ~~REMAINING follow-up: the return/credit cumulative cap keys on `invoiceItemId`, which a reconcile rotates, so a 2nd full credit/return across a reconcile can over-reverse~~ **FIXED** — the cumulative cap in `reverseReturnEntries`/`reverseCreditNoteEntries` now keys on the **ORDER LINE** (`orderItemId ?? invoiceItemId` fallback for freeform lines), and computes the per-line remaining as the NET of ALL that line's ledger rows (Σ signed SALE+REVERSAL across old+new invoice items) so it survives the reconcile's invoiceItemId rotation — a 2nd full return/credit after a reconcile books nothing (SUM floors at 0, never negative), while a valid partial after a reconcile still nets to (delivered − alreadyReturned). Credit-note CREATION also gained a **cumulative per-line cap** (`credit-notes.service.create`: Σ prior non-void `CreditNoteItem.amount` per `invoiceItemId` + new amt ≤ line subtotal) so a multi-line invoice's under-credited-line slack can't let another line be over-credited past its subtotal (the header invoice-total cap alone missed this). Single-group non-SEPARATE regulated edits via the legacy path remain unsynced (pre-existing); (b) ~~the `basis:"order"` reconcile double-bills split orders on the edit/merge paths~~ **FIXED** — `reconcileOrderDraftInvoice` is now sibling-aware for splits via **`reconcileSplitOrderDrafts`** (partitions CURRENT lines by category through `groupOrderLinesForInvoicing` — handles edits/added lines, unlike provenance — matches each group to its draft, rebuilds via the shared **`rebuildSiblingDrafts(order, pairs, basis, db)`** core also used by the delivered path; bails to the untouched legacy single-draft rebuild for single-group / finalized-present / count-mismatch / ambiguous-category, so single-group stays byte-identical).
