Pinned by **`routes/dispatch-addon-gate.spec.ts`** — a reflection test asserting each class's exact key array, AddonGuard-after-JwtAuthGuard ordering, and the `updatePlanning` override. Per-endpoint recurring-vs-either splitting beyond `/trips` is deliberately NOT attempted.
- side effects: TenantSubscription/Invoice writes on payment; Stripe API; guard cache invalidation. Specs: `addon.guard.spec.ts` (any-of, legacy-string metadata, internal-key redaction), `routes/dispatch-addon-gate.spec.ts`.
- **plans-as-data (P1–P6)** — versioned catalog (`plan-catalog.service.ts` PlanVersion/PlanDefinition/AddonSku), `entitlements.service.ts` (flags/caps + 30s cache), `subscription(-mutation).service.ts` (subscribe/upgrade/downgrade/cancel/resume/addons), `proration.service.ts`, `billing-event.service.ts` (append-only MRR ledger — `amountDelta` = SIGNED monthly run-rate change), `billing-cron.service.ts` (trial-expiry/grace/scheduled-downgrade/**scheduled-cancellation**/roll-cycle). Entitlement logic drives off string `planKey`; TenantPlan enum = legacy shadow.
  - **Pinned-version addon-SKU fallback (2026-08-22, PR-B)** — `entitlements.service.ts compute()`:
    when an active addon's SKU has no row in the tenant's PINNED catalog version (the SKU shipped
    in a LATER version — e.g. MSRP, first in v9), it lazily fetches `getVersionForTenant(null)`
    (published) once and resolves the meta there. Without this, enabling a newer addon on a
    grandfathered tenant granted NOTHING while the addon-keyed web gates (raw `TenantAddon` keys
    via `useHasAddon`) turned on — every gated write 403'd, incl. ordinary product saves that
    spread the msrp key. Pinning protects plan PRICING, never addon grantability. Spec:
    `entitlements.service.spec.ts` "falls back to the PUBLISHED catalog".
  - **`flag.msrp` + the `MSRP` AddonSku (2026-08-22, PR-B — ⚠️ IN FLIGHT, NOT on master)** —
    added to `FLAG_KEYS`/`ADDON_SKUS`/`LEGACY_ADDON_KEY_TO_SKU`/`FLAG_TO_ADDON_SKU`, published by
    `prisma/publish-plan-catalog-v9.ts` (clone of v8) which grants the flag to **NO plan**, so it
    is **OFF for every tenant** until an admin enables the `msrp` addon on the platform-admin
    tenant page. **Gating pattern worth copying:** the shared product/customer-price routes gate
    **inside the service**, only when `dto.msrp !== undefined` — decorating those routes with
    `@RequirePlanFlag` would 403 ordinary product edits for un-flagged tenants. Only the new
    `POST /products/msrp/bulk` carries the decorator. Client UI keys off `item.msrp != null`
    rather than the flag, so historical invoices still render if the addon is later removed.
  - **`GRACE_DAYS` (=7) lives in `plan-catalog.constants.ts`**, not in the cron. `BillingCronService.expireGrace()` clears expired windows hourly; synchronous cap gates apply the SAME threshold without waiting for it. One number, one place. **Exception: an expired `CUSTOMERS` window is NOT cleared while the tenant is still over cap** (`stillOverCustomerCap()` → `MeterService.read`, injected for this). A cleared window reads as "no grace yet" to `assertCustomerCapNotExceeded()`, which would let `maybeStartCustomerGrace()` open a brand-new 7-day window on the next create — the cap could then never engage (blocked ≤1h per 7 days). It clears + emits `grace.expired` once the tenant is back within cap (pack bought / upgraded / customers deleted); a meter lookup failure also clears it (fail open).
  - **Starter/Growth/Scale rename (v8, 2026-08-21)** — `PLAN_KEYS = STARTER/GROWTH/SCALE/ENTERPRISE`; superseded versions keep their `TEAM`/`BUSINESS` rows, so **every planKey comparison normalizes first** via `normalizePlanKey()` / `planRank()` (`LEGACY_PLAN_KEY_ALIASES`: TEAM→GROWTH, BUSINESS/PROFESSIONAL→SCALE). `normalizePlanKey()` reads that map through `Object.hasOwn`, never a bare index — a bare index makes prototype keys (`constructor`, `toString`, `valueOf`, `hasOwnProperty`, `__proto__`) return a truthy inherited member that slips past `?? null` and gets ranked/keyed as if it were a plan (same guard as the `customers.service.ts` sort allowlist). **Every lookup of a STORED planKey in a catalog version goes through `findPlanDefinition(definitions, planKey)`** (`plan-catalog.constants.ts`) — normalizes BOTH sides, so a v7-pinned tenant carrying SCALE matches the BUSINESS row and a v8 tenant carrying legacy TEAM matches GROWTH. A raw `d.planKey === key` there silently degrades the tenant to the fallback plan / prices it at 0. Fixed sites: `entitlements.service.ts` definition lookup (then reports the pinned version's OWN key so planKey/caps/flags stay consistent), `billing-cron.service.ts` `planMonthly()` + `applyScheduledDowngrades()` `targetDef` (seat cap + basePriceSnapshot), `subscription-mutation.service.ts` `planMonthly()` (upgrade nets against the OLD plan — an unmatched legacy key charged the full new price instead of the difference), and `proration.service.ts` `rankOrDefault()` (included-at-plan check — a raw PLAN_KEYS map silently priced a bundled add-on as paid on a legacy key). Lookups of a CLIENT-SUPPLIED key (`subscribe`/`upgrade`/`downgrade` inputs, `proration.quote`) stay raw on purpose: an unknown key must 400, not resolve.
    - **The Prisma `TenantPlan` enum was NOT renamed** (still `STARTER|TEAM|BUSINESS|PROFESSIONAL|ENTERPRISE`) — it is a legacy shadow, and the migration stays additive. So **every write to `Tenant.plan` / `TenantSubscription.currentPlan` goes through `planKeyToEnum()`** (`plan-catalog.constants.ts`; inverse of `planKeyFromEnum()` — GROWTH→TEAM, SCALE→BUSINESS, else STARTER/ENTERPRISE). The old `planKey as TenantPlan` cast compiles but throws Prisma enum validation (500) the moment a GROWTH/SCALE key reaches it, i.e. on the first subscribe/upgrade after v8 publishes. Sites: `subscription-mutation.service.ts` `subscribe()` (upsert create+update, tenant.update) and `upgrade()`, `billing-cron.service.ts` `applyScheduledDowngrades()`. Entitlements never read the shadow.
  - **Catalog-driven Stripe pricing + custom tenant fees (2026-08-21, IN PROGRESS on branch
    `feat/platform-billing-pricing`)** — `platform-pricing.service.ts` (new) is the resolver:
    `resolveTenantPricing(tenantId)` → `{planKey, planName, monthly, annual, source, currency}`
    and `checkoutPriceData(tenantId, interval)` → Stripe `price_data` in INTEGER CENTS.
    `billing.service.ts` gains `syncStripeSubscriptionPrice(tenantId)`; admin endpoints live on
    `platform-admin.controller.ts` (`GET .../billing/pricing`, `PATCH .../billing/price-override`,
    `PATCH /platform-admin/plans/:planKey/prices`, and `POST .../billing/checkout` now takes
    `{interval}`). Web: `lib/api/platform-pricing.ts` + `TenantPricingCard.tsx` + `admin/plans/page.tsx`.
    **Four traps the adversarial review caught, all now fixed and commented in place — do not
    regress them:** (1) the plan-price fan-out must match `planVersionId: version.id` **OR NULL**
    — that column is nullable with no backfill, so filtering on the id alone reached no live
    subscription; (2) an **annual-only** override must be honoured — gating the override branch on
    `priceOverrideMonthly != null` alone silently billed the catalog rate over a negotiated annual
    price; (3) every price change must also write `basePriceSnapshot` + a signed `BillingEvent`, or
    reported MRR silently diverges from what Stripe actually charges; (4) `createCheckoutSession`
    refuses when the tenant already has a live `stripeSubId` (a second checkout creates a DUPLICATE
    subscription — existing subscribers re-price through `syncStripeSubscriptionPrice` instead).
    Web-side twin of (2): `TenantPricingCard` prefills its inputs from the **raw override columns**
    the endpoint returns, never the resolved figures — blanking the annual input makes the next
    save send `annual: null` and destroy the negotiated price. Migration
    `20260829000000_tenant_price_override` adds
    three additive columns to `TenantSubscription`: `priceOverrideMonthly` /
    `priceOverrideAnnual` (Decimal(10,2), null = fall back to the catalog) and
    `billingInterval` ("month" | "year", stamped when checkout completes). Resolution order
    for what a tenant is charged: **per-tenant override → the tenant's PINNED PlanVersion's
    `PlanDefinition` price** (never the latest version — 19 tenants are grandfathered on v7);
    annual = `annualPrice ?? monthlyPrice * 10` (owner's 2-months-free rule). Checkout stops
    using `StripeService.getPriceIdForPlan` / the `STRIPE_PRICE_*` env vars (which knew only
    STARTER/PROFESSIONAL/ENTERPRISE and THREW on GROWTH/SCALE's TEAM/BUSINESS shadows, so the
    two real mid tiers could never check out) in favour of inline Stripe `price_data` computed
    per click — prices are DATA, Stripe follows. Live-subscription price changes use
    `proration_behavior: "none"` (owner decision: next billing cycle, never mid-period), and a
    plan-price edit fans out only to latest-version tenants WITHOUT an override. Plan doc:
    `.claude/pipeline/plans/2026-08-21-platform-billing-pricing.md`. Note the tenant-facing
    self-service `SubscriptionMutationService` still never calls Stripe — unchanged by design.
  - **`customersIncluded` is a first-class catalog column** (null = unlimited). Every per-column list in `plan-catalog.service.ts` must carry it: the `createDraft()` clone payload, `PlanDefinitionPatch` + `updateDefinition()`, and the `getPublicCatalog()` plan projection. These enumerate fields explicitly, so a new axis omitted from the clone silently NULLs on the next admin draft→publish and `EntitlementsService` reads NULL as unlimited — the cap disappears with no error. Add a new column to all four sites at once.
- **CUSTOMERS soft-cap (2026-08-21)** — `CustomersService.create()` calls `assertCustomerCapNotExceeded()` before and `maybeStartCustomerGrace()` after. Over-cap creates ALWAYS succeed and open a grace window; only a brand-new create after that window expires (while still over cap) throws the `buildPlanGateBody` 403 naming `CUSTOMER_PACK_100`. **Fails open** on any meter/catalog error. `maybeStartCustomerGrace()` **upserts** `TenantSubscription` (never `update`): most tenants have NO subscription row while still getting a cap from the `tenant.plan` fallback, so `update` threw P2025 on every over-cap create and the window was never recorded. The created row leaves `planKey` null (a grace window pins no catalog version, so snapshot MRR — `mrr.service.ts`, which filters `planKey != null` — ignores it) but **must stamp `currentPlan` from `tenant.plan`**: the column defaults to STARTER, and platform-admin reads `currentPlan` raw out of this table (`getBillingOverview()` + `_formatTenant`), so a defaulted grace row reports a Scale tenant as Starter on the admin billing/tenant screens. Same write `billing.service.ts` makes when minting a row. Both helpers are PUBLIC so `import/import.service.ts` `importContacts()` (the CSV/bulk path, which builds User+Customer rows directly and never calls `create()`) gates on the same rule — checked once up front, grace opened once after. `importInvoices()` auto-creates missing customers too: it tracks that in `autoCreatedCustomers` and calls `maybeStartCustomerGrace()` once at the end, but deliberately does **NOT** pre-gate — a finance import is never blocked by the customer cap. `MeterService.customersUsed()` counts `deletedAt: null` **and `supplierOnly: false`** — it must match `CustomersService.findAll()`'s definition of a customer, or vendor contacts created by the expense-CSV import (hidden from the Customers list) inflate the meter and open a grace window for headroom the tenant cannot see. Spec: `import/import-customer-cap.spec.ts`. Wiring: `CustomersModule` imports `EntitlementsModule` (MeterService + PlanCatalogService); `ImportModule` imports `CustomersModule` (no cycle — only `app.module` imports ImportModule). `platform-admin` gets the same services via `BillingModule`, which re-exports `EntitlementsModule`.
- **`mrr.service.ts` (P6)** — `computeOverview()` → server-side MRR = Σ(base + add-ons − discount) from per-tenant price SNAPSHOTS over ACTIVE tenants with `planKey != null` (add-on query mirrors that predicate); + `ledgerMrr` (Σ BillingEvent.amountDelta), `momDelta` (fixed trailing 30-day window), `byPlan`, paying/trial/readOnly counts. Endpoint `@Get billing/admin/mrr` (SuperAdminGuard). **Ledger reconciliation invariant**: churn emits a compensating NEGATIVE delta (`applyScheduledCancellations` = −(base+add-ons−discount)) AND deactivates the add-on rows; re-entry via `subscribe()` treats a non-ACTIVE prior status as run-rate 0 (+base) so cancel→reactivate nets exactly. Legacy Stripe churn/reactivation is ALSO reconciled: `billing.service.ts` `transitionAndEmit` (atomic status compare-and-swap via conditional `updateMany` + `emitPayingDelta` in ONE `$transaction`; `count===1` = idempotency key) emits signed deltas on `onSubscriptionDeleted`/`suspendOverdueTenants` (−) and `onPaymentSucceeded`/`onCheckoutCompleted` (+), gated on `planKey != null` (no-op for pure Stripe tenants). Event `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED`. Spec: `billing.service.spec.ts`. Web: platform-admin `admin/billing/page.tsx` consumes it (client `PLAN_PRICES` = degraded fallback only). Specs: `mrr.service.spec.ts`, `billing-cron.service.spec.ts`, `subscription-mutation.service.spec.ts`. **Platform billing pricing batch (2026-08-21, in progress)**: `TenantSubscription` gained `priceOverrideMonthly`/`priceOverrideAnnual`/`billingInterval` (per-tenant custom fee beats the catalog; annual = explicit or 10× monthly). New `platform-pricing.service.ts` (`PlatformPricingService.resolveTenantPricing` / `resolveCatalogPricing` (override-ignoring; the pre-write "can this tenant be cleared?" check) / `checkoutPriceData`) replaces `getPriceIdForPlan` env-price checkout. EITHER override column alone counts as a custom fee (annual-only override honoured, monthly then from the catalog); a catalog `annualPrice` of 0 is a real promo price, never "unset". `BillingService.syncStripeSubscriptionPrice` = resolve (unresolvable → `{synced:false, reason:"price_unresolvable"}`, never throws) → `pushResolvedPriceToStripe` (item `price_data` always carries a `product` ID — `product_data` is Checkout-only and is rejected on a subscription update; a missing product is created) → `reconcilePriceLedger` (writes `basePriceSnapshot` + one `PLAN_CHANGED` BillingEvent carrying the signed `amountDelta`, gated on `planKey != null`, so MRR follows the price actually billed). `createCheckoutSession` REFUSES a second checkout while a live (non-canceled) `stripeSubId` exists — a duplicate subscription would double-bill and be orphaned by the webhook. Specs: `platform-pricing.service.spec.ts`, `billing.service.spec.ts`.

### `estimates/`

- **controller** `estimates` — create, list, get, send, accept, decline, convert-to-invoice, void.
- **service** — `create`, `findAll`, `findOne`, `send`, `accept`, `decline`, `convertToInvoice`, `void`. side effects: Estimate(+Item) writes; Invoice on convert; email.

### `vendor-bills/`

- **controller** `vendor-bills` — create, list (`?needsMapping=true` filter), scan-invoice (field name `images` ONLY; 10 files × 25MB + 60MB aggregate guard), **`POST check-duplicate`** (declared BEFORE the `:id` routes or it gets swallowed), product-mappings CRUD (`POST` body `SaveProductMappingDto {supplierName, rawDescription, productId: string|null}` — a class, NOT an inline literal, or the global ValidationPipe has no metadata and validates nothing), per-id get/patch/receive (body `ReceiveVendorBillDto {acknowledgeUnlinked?, items?: [{itemId, qty}]}` + @CurrentUser)/revert-to-draft/void/payments/delete, bulk delete.
- **Duplicate-invoice guard (2026-08-07):** `VendorBill.supplierInvoiceNumber` (migration `20260807000000_...`, normalized uppercase/no-whitespace, `@@index([tenantId, supplierInvoiceNumber])`) finally gives the SUPPLIER's number a column — `billNumber` is our own `BILL-YYYY-NNNN`, and the supplier's lived only as free text in `notes`. Deliberately **NOT unique**: prod predates it, the same number recurs across suppliers, and a VOID bill must never block a re-scan; enforcement is app-level in `create()`. `create()` resolves the number as `dto.supplierInvoiceNumber ?? extractSupplierInvoiceNumber(dto.notes)` (so OLD web bundles that only send the notes phrase stay guarded + backfilled), persists it, and unless `dto.allowDuplicate` throws `ConflictException({code:"DUPLICATE_VENDOR_BILL", message, duplicate})` — same structured-conflict shape as `UNLINKED_ITEMS`, message self-explanatory because un-updated clients render `response.data.message` raw. `checkDuplicate(dto)` is the read-only pre-flight behind the endpoint. DRAFT match ⇒ `resumable` (finish it); RECEIVED ⇒ hard. **Only `matchedBy === "number"` may BLOCK a create** — an invoice number is document identity; a fuzzy (supplier + date ±1d + total) hit is a hint for a human, and hard-blocking on it made legitimate manual entry impossible (the web manual bill modal always sends supplierId + billDate, so every hand-entered bill went through the fuzzy layer; two same-day same-total deliveries from one supplier could never be entered). `checkDuplicate` still RETURNS fuzzy hits so the scan modal can warn. Backfill: `apps/api/scripts/backfill-supplier-invoice-number.mjs` — idempotent (`IS NULL` guard), batched in a transaction, normalization kept byte-identical to the service via a startup drift guard that greps `duplicate-match.service.ts` plus a before/after denormalized-count rollback. Note `VendorBill.id` is TEXT, not uuid: casting the batched VALUES rows to `::uuid` makes every UPDATE throw `operator does not exist: text = uuid`, and because the dry-run path returns early that failure only ever appears under `--execute`, i.e. against prod.
- **scan/create hardening (batch-scan companion):** `create()` folds optional `dto.taxAmount` into `totalOwed` and rounds the write (`roundMoney`) — scanned supplier invoices carry sales tax the line items don't. NOTE: `update()` is untouched — it recomputes `totalOwed` from `items` only, so a scan-created bill's tax is lost on the first edit after `revertToDraft()` (pre-existing gap, not fixed here; needs a persisted tax column to close). `scanInvoice()` maps failures to TYPED HttpExceptions the web modal switches on: Anthropic 401/403 → 400 `AI_KEY_INVALID`, other non-retryable 4xx (400/404/409/422 — bad/corrupt image, malformed request; excludes 429) → 400 `AI_SCAN_REJECTED`, genuine outage/rate-limit (429/5xx) → 503 `AI_UNAVAILABLE`, unparseable AI output → 422 `AI_PARSE_FAILED`; `new Anthropic({apiKey, maxRetries:0})` + per-request timeout 110s (client waits 120s) — `maxRetries:0` is required for the timeout to actually be a ceiling (the SDK default of 2 retries would each get their own fresh 110s window); the UI's own per-invoice Retry button is the intended retry path. An unreadable HEIC page is SKIPPED + disclosed in `notes` (throws only if every page unreadable). Specs in `vendor-bills.service.spec.ts` (SDK + sharp jest-mocked) incl. the 400→AI_SCAN_REJECTED / 429→AI_UNAVAILABLE boundary. **2026-08-28 metering:** key resolution unified through `platformConfig.resolveAnthropicKey(tenantKey)` (ConfigService injection dropped) and every call records an `AiUsageEvent` (`ocr.vendor_bill`, model `claude-haiku-4-5`, tokens on success / `success:false` on call failure) — cache-hit rescans record nothing.
- **Scan matching rework (2026-07-18):** `scanInvoice`'s Phase-2 matching delegates to the NEW pure module **`product-matcher.ts`** (`composedProductName` / `buildTokenWeights` / `matchLine`; zero-mock specs in `product-matcher.spec.ts`). Matches against COMPOSED "Parent - Variant" display names (catalog + mapping selects gained `parentProductId, parent{name}`) with document-frequency token weights (rare flavor/size tokens outweigh common words — fixes wrong-sibling wins on shared prefixes), deterministic tie-breaks (score→hits→prefix→name). **Scores 0.35–0.6 are NO LONGER auto-assigned** — line returns `matchedProductId:null, confidence:"low"` + ranked `candidates[]` (top 5 ≥0.25, composed names); thresholds 0.8/0.6/0.35 unchanged; learned mappings still first + authoritative (composed names). OCR prompt now extracts per-line **`sku`** (item code as printed; flows through the response spread) and exact scanned-SKU/barcode matches → high. Response additions (`sku`, `candidates`) are additive/optional. **(2026-07-18b, in-flight follow-ups PR):** prompt also extracts **`packSize`** (units per box/case — EXPLICIT-only, never guessed; additive on `ScannedItem` web/mobile + batch `ScanLine`) — used ONLY as an editable units-per-box PREFILL with a verify hint in the create-from-line forms, never auto-applied (boxed money math).
- **service** — `create`, `findAll` (needsMapping filter = DRAFT + items none|some productId null; meta always carries `needsMappingCount`), `findOne`, `update` (**DRAFT-only; item recreate now carries sku/packSize/lineTotal; refolds `taxAmount ?? bill.taxAmount` into `totalOwed` — tax no longer lost on edit; DTO whitelists `taxAmount`**), **`receive(id, ReceiveVendorBillDto?, userId?)`** — **G1/G2/G3 costing core (2026-08-12)**: idempotency guard on `receivedDate` + per-line `qtyReceived` (NOT status — recordPayment overwrites it); `dto.items` = partial receive plan (validated per line: on-bill, linked, ≤ remaining), omit = receive all remaining; `lineInventoryDelta` converts case lines (`packSize>1`) to pieces + per-piece cost (Product.averageCost is per PIECE); `lineReceivedQty` resolves the null-ambiguity (legacy fully-received vs tracked-0); per line: `nextAverageCost` (skipped for `costingMethod===STANDARD`) + StockLot + PURCHASE movement stamped at `billDate` + backdate replay (`recomputeProductInTx` when newer movements exist); `fireStockAlerts` after tx; status RECEIVED when complete / PARTIAL when short; UNLINKED_ITEMS 409 on first receive only. **qtyReceived writes go NESTED through the bill's update** — items are nested-created so their tenantId is null and scoped `vendorBillItem.update` can't see them (P2025). `recordPayment`, `revertToDraft`/`voidBill` — reverse ONLY `lineReceivedQty` (partial receipts reverse partially), same `lineInventoryDelta` denomination, `reverseAverageCost` (**keeps avg when stock empties**), `reverseBillLots`; revert clears qtyReceived (nested updateMany) + refuses never-received bills, void skips reversal for them. `delete`, `scanInvoice` (OCR), product-mapping CRUD. side effects: VendorBill(+Item)/BillPayment/StockLot writes; inventory PURCHASE on receive; expense entries. Specs: `vendor-bills.service.spec.ts` (76: G1 double-receive/phantom-unwind, G2 case conversion + symmetric revert, G3 partial/top-up/over-receive/legacy, G7 STANDARD/billDate/replay).

- **AP payment allocation + on-account supplier credit (PR-E, 2026-08-20).** `recordSupplierPayment(RecordSupplierPaymentDto)` = the AP mirror of `invoices.recordStandalonePayment`: one lump sum across N of ONE supplier's bills, one `randomUUID()` `paymentGroupId` on every `BillPayment` (new nullable column + index), all in one `tenantTransaction`. Guards, in order: tenant-scoped `supplier.findUnique` → 404 (without it an unknown/foreign id 500s on the SupplierCredit FK or mints an invisible credit row); per allocation `bill.supplierId === dto.supplierId` → 400; **`bill.status === "VOID"` → 400** (the ONE status comparison here — a void bill keeps `totalOwed` and has no offsetting rows, so the arithmetic still shows a balance and the update below would resurrect it as PAID); `amount ≤ totalOwed − ledgerPaid + 0.001` → 400. Eligibility is ALWAYS `totalOwed − Σ BillPayment.amount` (landmine 1: `PARTIAL` means short-receipt OR part-payment). Unallocated remainder → `SupplierCredit` (new model, AP mirror of `AdvancePayment`; no status machine, fully drawn = `balance 0`), never a rejection. `getSupplierStatement(supplierId)` = pure running-balance timeline (bills up, payments/credits down), `outstanding = Σ(totalOwed − totalPaid)` over non-VOID bills. **`applySupplierCreditToBill` (called from `create()` and from `update()`, inside their tx) draws against `totalOwed − totalPaid` and writes `totalPaid` ONLY — the bill stays DRAFT.** Flipping it to PAID/PARTIAL there locked a never-received bill out of its whole draft lifecycle (`update()` DRAFT-only, `revertToDraft()` rejects PAID and rejects PARTIAL with null `receivedDate`, `delete`/`bulkDelete` DRAFT|VOID-only, `findAll` needsMapping is DRAFT-scoped) — the list already shows owed/paid/balance arithmetically, so status stays the RECEIPT lifecycle. Each draw is a `BillPayment` with **`supplierCreditId`** (new nullable FK + index, mirrors `InvoicePayment.advancePaymentId`) — that column, never the operator-typable `reference: "SUPPLIER_CREDIT-<id8>"` text, is what `getSupplierStatement` excludes from the timeline and what `refundDrawnSupplierCredits(tx, billId)` matches. **`voidBill` (both branches, the non-reversal one now transactional), `delete`, and `bulkDelete` all call `refundDrawnSupplierCredits` first**: it restores each draw to its originating credit (capped at that credit's `amount`), deletes the draw rows so a void-then-delete cannot refund twice, and returns the total so `totalPaid` is corrected. **`update()` is credit-aware for the same reason**: because the draw leaves the bill DRAFT, an edit can move both the amount the draw was sized by and the supplier it was drawn from, so `update()` calls `refundDrawnSupplierCredits` FIRST (correcting `totalPaid` via `totalPaidAfterRefund`) and then re-applies against the edited `totalOwed`/`supplierId` — without it, correcting a total downwards stranded the difference (statement `outstanding` went negative) and re-pointing `supplierId` funded the new supplier's bill out of the old supplier's account. Specs: `supplier-payment.spec.ts`; the prisma mock predates `SupplierCredit`, so tests graft the model onto `prisma`, `forTenant()`, and the tx surface.
- **`saveProductMapping` is tenant-scoped, never an upsert (2026-08-18 — cross-tenant bug fix).** `ProductMapping`'s compound key `@@unique([supplierName, rawDescription])` carries **no tenantId**, so it is GLOBAL: the old `upsert` on `supplierName_rawDescription` let tenant B's "remember this match" correction either 500 on P2002 or silently overwrite tenant A's mapping. Now: `forTenant().findFirst({supplierName, rawDescription})` → `update` by `id` if found, else `create`, with the create in a try/catch that swallows **only** `Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError` code `P2002` (another tenant holds the global key — logged at debug, returns `null` rather than 500 the operator's correction); any other error rethrows. No migration — the schema key is unchanged. **`findFirst` is only safe with BOTH keys present** — Prisma drops `undefined` filter keys, so a body missing `rawDescription` would match this tenant's FIRST mapping for that supplier and repoint an unrelated description (the old compound-key upsert threw instead). Guarded twice: the controller's `SaveProductMappingDto` class at the HTTP boundary, plus a `typeof … !== "string"` → `BadRequestException` at the top of the service method for every other caller. Spec cases in `vendor-bills.service.spec.ts` (existing row ⇒ update-by-id no create / no row ⇒ create / P2002 ⇒ resolves / other error ⇒ rethrows / missing rawDescription ⇒ 400 before any query; the prisma mock needs `productMapping.findFirst`).
- **Partial-receipt schema (2026-08-12):** `VendorBillItem.qtyReceived Decimal? @db.Decimal(10,3)` (migration `20260812000000_vendor_bill_item_qty_received`, additive) — cumulative receipt per line in BILL denomination (cases when `packSize>1`). Null is AMBIGUOUS by design: on a bill whose `receivedDate` is set while EVERY line is null (legacy pre-tracking receive) it means fully received; on a tracked bill it means never received — `lineReceivedQty()` is the only reader.
- **`scripts/repair-costing.mjs`** — READ-ONLY prod costing diagnostic (deliberately NO `--execute`): §1 duplicate RECEIVED bills grouped by normalized supplier invoice number (KEEP first / EXTRA rest + per-product phantom stock/value), §2 case-cost suspect lines (`packSize>1` recorded, or boxed product with line cost ≥ per-piece sell), §3 sequenced repair proposal (void extras → fix movement denominations → recompute). Env: `REPORT_TENANT_SLUG`, `REPORT_RECEIVED_BEFORE` (bound §2 to pre-fix receives). Same `railway run --service postgres` URL-from-parts pattern as the other prod scripts; degrades when `packSize` column absent.

- **Customer purge ↔ regulated ledger (2026-09-01)** — `customers.service.ts` destroys invoices in THREE places, and all three now call `ledger.reverseInvoiceEntries({invoiceId, db: tx})` per invoice BEFORE the payment/item/invoice deletes (the ledger is append-only with no FK to Invoice; same ordering as `invoices.service deleteInvoice` and `orders.service deleteOrder`/B65). `CustomersModule` imports `RegulatedModule`; `CustomersService` injects `RegulatedLedgerService` — that DI addition ripples to the **nine** specs that construct the service. ⚠️ **A DRAFT invoice DOES carry ledger rows** (`createSplitInvoices` writes them in the tx that creates the DRAFT), so a status filter here would reintroduce the leak. Reachability differs per site and is the whole point: `deleteCustomer`'s hard-delete block is only reached when the pre-flight counted ZERO invoices, so its reversal is DEFENSIVE (it closes the TOCTOU window between that out-of-tx count and the delete); `deleteAllCustomers` blocks only on PAID/SENT and so really did destroy DRAFTs; `deleteImportedCustomers` has **no invoice guard at all**. Spec: `customers.purge-ledger.spec.ts`.
- **`scripts/data-integrity-report.mjs`** (2026-08-28/29) — READ-ONLY forensic integrity report: 25 SELECT/aggregate checks (24 named + one dynamic per-child-model `tenant-drift-<model>` generator) detecting the DB footprints of confirmed bugs (invoice-overpaid, invoice-paid-with-balance, invoice-unpaid-with-payments, payment-stuck-draft, payment-on-dead-invoice, invoice-header-math, invoice-lines-vs-subtotal, invoice-number-dup-null-tenant, orderitem-overbilled/-invoicedqty-underrun/-negative-counters, cancelled-order-delivered-goods, return-exceeds-delivered/-exceeds-ordered/-dangling-creditnote, creditnote-overdrawn/-used-drift, advance-balance-drift, product-negative-stock, stock-vs-last-movement [flagged unreliable — see below], recurring-duplicate-fire, recurring-stalled, stop-completed-no-timestamp, delivered-order-skipped-stop) plus general corruption (tenant-scope drift, arithmetic drift, orphans, duplicates). Session forces `default_transaction_read_only = on` before any check, every query is SELECT-only with a `statement_timeout`, never prints customer/product names — safe to run against production. `railway run --service postgres node scripts/data-integrity-report.mjs [--tenant <slug>] [--verbose]`; connection resolves like `prod-readonly-audit.mjs` (URL from `POSTGRES_*`/`RAILWAY_TCP_PROXY_*` parts when `DATABASE_URL` is the internal-only one). **Fable adversarial review (2026-08-29, VALIDATION STATUS header in the file):** recurring-duplicate-fire correctly detects B46's midnight re-fire pairs; payments-vs-status checks mirror `recomputeStatus` exactly; credit-note/advance drift sound but the dangling-pointer mechanism runs backwards; billing-counter checks are valid but B84's double-void leaves no negative scar to catch; returns-exceeding-delivered confirmed against the ordered-basis validator; tenant-scope drift confirmed (nullable tenantId class has fired before per the backfill scripts above); **`stock-vs-last-movement` REFUTED as a footprint** — `currentStock` is not reconstructible from movements by design, so the check would flood false positives (B55/B64 are still real, just not detectable this way). **F13/B48 adds three checks (2026-09-04):** `template-order-list-price-vs-tier` (high) — a template-generated `OrderItem` billed at the CURRENT list price while the customer's CURRENT tier/override price differs, i.e. B48 overcharge candidates on a **current basis** (promotion-era overcharges are unrecoverable and deliberately out of scope); `order-list-price-vs-tier-any` (info) — the same arithmetic over ALL orders as a **positive control**, so a 0-row high check can be told apart from a broken predicate; and `f13-gate-state` (info) — the closed gates (`monthly-templates-ever-run`, `template-generated-orders`, `template-orders-for-tiered-or-override-customers`) that make a 0-row B46/B48 result evidence of no damage rather than evidence of nothing to look at. Read the three together, never alone.
- **`scripts/f07-conservation-report.mjs`** (2026-09-01, F07/D4) — READ-ONLY forensic report for the two damage classes F07's code fix stops creating. **There is no `--execute` path in the file, by design**, and the session is forced `default_transaction_read_only = on` (mirrors `data-integrity-report.mjs`). **B64 — candidate set:** CANCELLED orders still holding a non-item-CANCELLED line with a `productId` (post-F07 every cancel marks its lines, so the set is closed and can only shrink). ⚠️ **It reports a per-product/per-tenant UPPER BOUND and stops there — per-row truth is NOT mechanically decidable.** An order created as DRAFT and promoted, or cancelled straight out of DRAFT, never took a creation-time decrement, and nothing persisted distinguishes it once the status reads CANCELLED: `OrderRevision` records EDITs only, this path writes no `StockMovement` rows, and status transitions are not audited. Crediting a promoted draft would INVENT inventory — strictly worse than the understatement. Recorded as unrepairable-without-owner-input. **B56 — identified set:** non-DRAFT VOID invoices on CANCELLED orders whose lines carry `deliveredQty > 0`, with the revenue total. Report-only because un-voiding is not a row patch: a void released `invoicedQty`, returned wallet credits, reversed regulated-ledger rows and clawed back commission accruals, so the remedy (re-invoice the delivered qty vs accept the write-off) is an operator decision. Emits ids/quantities/amounts/dates ONLY — never customer, product or business names — and writes `local-assets/f07-conservation-report-<ts>.jsonl`. Run: `railway run --service postgres node scripts/f07-conservation-report.mjs [--tenant <id>]`.
- **`scripts/repair-integrity.mjs`** — SURGICAL repair companion, scoped to the SPECIFIC rows `data-integrity-report.mjs` found on 2026-08-28/29 only (`overpaid-1a8fbb3d`, `paidbal-882e3615`, `deadpay-cf082424`, `overbill-6edd8f20`, `unpaidpay-{638eb534,bc48a97b}`, `hdrmath-1a62c9d9`, `linesum-ea63fb65`, `retdel-{36c21079,aa79cb93,2ef317f6}`, `tenantdrift-23fae434`, `orphantpl-15e9d9f1`, `stalledtpl-61bc82ba` — 23 rowIds total, hardcoded `ROWS` array); it never scans for new damage. Dry-run by default (same forced-read-only session); writing requires ALL of `--execute` + `--i-have-a-fresh-backup` (attestation) + `--confirm <rowId>` (repeatable, opt in per row) — rows flagged "needs a human decision" also need `--resolution <rowId>=<option>` (see `--list`), and the overpaid row's void-duplicate-payment path additionally needs `--payment <InvoicePayment id>`. One transaction per row: locks the row (`FOR UPDATE`), re-reads and byte-compares the before-state against the dry-run snapshot (drift aborts that row), re-runs the matching forensic check post-commit to confirm clean, and appends every executed repair to `local-assets/repair-log-<timestamp>.jsonl`. Refuses to run unless `current_database()` looks like production (`PROD_DB_NAMES = {"railway","routeflow"}`, excluding names matching `test|local|dev|qa|e2e`) — `--force-nonprod` overrides, loud and deliberate. Per the client-data-protection policy: these are LIVE CLIENT rows, run only at owner's explicit request, after a fresh verified backup (house method: `railway ssh` in-container `pg_dump`), dry-run first, one row at a time — full procedure in `scripts/REPAIR-RUNBOOK.md`. **Now imports `roundMoney` from `@routeflow/pricing` directly** (same as `feature-smoke.mjs`, `repair-f03.mjs`, and `repair-f17.mjs` — PR-4 `imp-01`; the local mirror is gone) — half-away-from-zero via the `toFixed(4)` re-render, NOT a `+ Number.EPSILON` nudge (F04/REG-B122). This script WRITES the values it rounds (`newPaid`, `newAmount`, `lineSum`, `newTotal`), so an EPSILON copy would round every exact half-cent DOWN and re-introduce the one-cent divergence it exists to repair — never let the two drift. `@routeflow/pricing`'s compiled `dist/` is built by the root `postinstall`.
- **`scripts/repair-f10-reopen-damage.mjs`** (F10, #591) — per-batch D4 repair lane for the
  damage `reopenStop` wrote before the B55 fix. Scope is **derived, not a hardcoded allowlist**:
  the only rows it considers are `StockMovement` where `type='SALE' AND quantity > 0 AND
reference LIKE 'Reopen stop %'`. That is safe because **nothing else in the repo writes a
  positive SALE movement** — order creation decrements `Product.currentStock` with **no movement
  row at all** — so the predicate cannot collide with legitimate inventory history. ⚠️ **That
  `reference` prefix is load-bearing twice over**: it is both the damage footprint and the ONLY
  historical trace of a pre-fix reopen (F10 added the `AuditLog` + `RouteRunStop.podHistory`
  trail only going forward), so a new writer reusing it would poison the repair query. Repairs
  B55 (decrement each product by its summed reopen quantity, annotate the movements
  `[F10-repaired]`, never delete them); **B54 is REPORT-ONLY and no money is ever mutated** —
  the payment really was collected, so whether the order state or the invoice is wrong is a
  per-case owner judgment. Same safety model as `repair-integrity.mjs` (dry-run default with the
  session forced read-only, `--execute` + `--i-have-a-fresh-backup` + per-product `--confirm`,
  one tx per product with the row locked and `currentStock` re-read and compared to the dry-run
  snapshot, scoped re-check after each commit, JSONL to `local-assets/`, refuses a database that
  is not `railway`/`routeflow`). A repair that would drive `currentStock` negative is skipped for
  an owner decision. Reopens of stops with no delivered mutation left no trace and are recorded
  as unrepairable, never reconstructed. Runbook table: `scripts/REPAIR-RUNBOOK.md`.
- **`scripts/repair-f11-stranded-orders.mjs`** (F11, B129/B211) — per-batch D4 repair lane
  mirroring the service's `releaseUndeliveredOrders` helper. Scope is **derived, not
  hardcoded**: `Order` rows with a non-null `routeRunStopId`, `status NOT IN
('DELIVERED','CANCELLED')`, whose run is `CANCELLED`, OR whose run is `COMPLETED` and its stop
  `SKIPPED`; optional `--tenant <slug>` narrows (resolved to an id first — an unknown slug exits
  non-zero rather than silently scanning zero rows). Repairs, per run in ONE transaction, the
  helper's three writes in order: (1) OUT_FOR_DELIVERY → CONFIRMED for the in-scope orders, (2)
  null `routeRunId`/`routeRunStopId`, (3) every PENDING `ChangeRequest` on the orders write 2
  actually released → DECLINED (`resolution='DECLINED'`, the helper's own
  `RELEASED_CHANGE_REQUEST_REASON`, `resolvedAt`/`updatedAt`=NOW()), keyed by the SAME locked id
  array as write 2. ⚠️ **Two stop-status re-checks, mirroring the forward fix's per-statement
  `routeRunStop: { status: { not: "COMPLETED" } }`:** a pre-apply re-read against the scan's
  candidate set (drives the printed "skipped (stop completed since scan)" count before any
  transaction opens) and an authoritative re-read INSIDE each run's transaction, under the same
  `FOR UPDATE` lock as the order rows, run BEFORE the drift comparison — an order whose stop went
  COMPLETED between the scan and the write is dropped from all three writes rather than aborting
  the whole run. A CONFIRMED CASH/CHECK `InvoicePayment` on a released order is reported (SQL
  column `attribution_moved`, printed/JSONL `attributionMoved`), never blocked — spec R15. Orders
  pinned to a COMPLETED stop are listed and never written (outside the forward fix's release
  scope). Same safety model as `repair-f10-reopen-damage.mjs`: dry-run default
  (`default_transaction_read_only = on`), `--force-nonprod` to target a non-prod db by name,
  `--execute --i-have-a-fresh-backup --confirm <runId>` (repeatable) or `--confirm-all-listed` to
  write, one tx per run with the run row + its order rows `FOR UPDATE`-locked and re-read/byte-
  compared to the dry-run snapshot (drift aborts that run), JSONL per repaired order to
  `local-assets/f11-repair-<ts>.jsonl` (`{ orderId, runId, stopId, before: { status, routeRunId,
routeRunStopId, pendingChangeRequestIds }, attributionMoved }` — `pendingChangeRequestIds` are
  the exact CR ids write 3 declined, so the decline is reversible row by row alongside the
  release). **`assertTestTenant` deliberately does NOT apply** — same as
  `repair-f03.mjs`/`repair-f17.mjs`, a D4 repair lane repairs live rows by design; `--tenant`
  narrows the scope, it never gates it. Runbook table: `scripts/REPAIR-RUNBOOK.md`.
- **`scripts/prod-readonly-audit.mjs`** (2026-08-15) — READ-ONLY production audit (no write
  path at all); run via `railway run --service postgres node apps/api/scripts/prod-readonly-audit.mjs`.

- **NULL-tenantId child-row backfills** — two dry-run-first repair scripts for the SAME root cause: a Prisma **nested create** bypasses the `forTenant()` tenant extension (`prisma.service.ts`), so the child lands with `tenantId = NULL` while its parent is scoped correctly. Because `forTenant()` also injects `where.tenantId` on READS, those rows are invisible to every tenant-scoped query and silently under-count reports. Both derive the value from the parent, fill NULLs only, never overwrite, abort pre-write on any child/parent tenant mismatch, and write in one transaction that rolls back on a post-write inconsistency. `railway run --service postgres node <script>` (add `--execute` to write); connection is built from `POSTGRES_*` + `RAILWAY_TCP_PROXY_*` parts, same as the other prod scripts.
  - **`scripts/backfill-invoiceitem-tenantid.mjs`** — `Invoice ← Customer`, `InvoiceItem ← Invoice`. Repaired ~3.4k rows from the May-2026 bulk import.
  - **`scripts/backfill-payment-movement-tenantid.mjs`** (2026-08-21) — `InvoicePayment ← Invoice`, `StockMovement ← Product`. Asks the question via the PARENT (the query shape `demo-verify.js`'s tenant-scoping check uses), prints every affected row plus a per-tenant summary before writing, and explicitly `set_config('app.current_tenant_id','')` so the `tenant_isolation` RLS policy's empty-setting escape hatch keeps the NULL rows visible instead of filtering them away. **Applied to prod 2026-08-21: 3 payments + 7 movements (all 2026-04-30, both `ux-audit-*` QA tenants); 0 NULLs remain in either table.** Left in place as the repair path for the next occurrence — a clean run reports zeros and exits.

### `supplier-statements/` (PR-F, 2026-08-20 — AI statement reconciliation)

- `SupplierStatementsModule` **is** registered in `app.module.ts` (next to `VendorBillsModule`), so `GET /supplier-statements`, `GET /:id`, `POST /scan` and `POST /:id/apply` are all live.
- **model** `SupplierStatementScan` + enum `SupplierStatementScanStatus` (SCANNED|APPLIED|DISCARDED), migration `20260822000000_add_supplier_statement_scan` (**additive only** — no DROP, no data migration). Deliberately shaped like `InvoiceScan`: `fileHash` dedups a re-upload without re-calling the model, `extractedPayload` Json keeps the model's verbatim output for audit, `appliedPaymentGroupId` makes an apply reconstructable (and undoable by voiding that group). Indexes `[tenantId, fileHash]`, `[tenantId, supplierId]`, `[tenantId, status]`; back-relations on `Tenant` and `Supplier`.
- **module** imports `PrismaModule, ConfigModule, SystemConfigModule, DuplicateMatchModule, StorageModule, PlatformAdminModule, BillingModule` (PlatformAdminModule for key resolution + AI metering 2026-08-28; BillingModule for the scan endpoint AddonGuard/@RequireAddon(ocr) gate 2026-08-29); providers/exports `SupplierStatementsService` + `StatementApplyService`. **`DuplicateMatchModule` directly, never `ImportModule` or `VendorBillsModule`** — it was split out precisely to avoid the `ImportModule ↔ VendorBillsModule` cycle.
- **controller** `supplier-statements` — class-level `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard)` + `@Roles(OPERATOR)`. `GET /` (list, `?status&page&limit`), `GET /:id`, `POST /scan`, `POST /:id/apply` (`@Body() ApplyStatementBodyDto` → `StatementApplyService.applyStatement(id, dto, user)`; `user` comes from `@CurrentUser()`, which carries both `sub` and `id`). **Field name is `files`, NOT vendor-bills' `images`** (multer matches exactly — a client sending `images` gets "No file provided"); 10 files × 25MB via `FilesInterceptor` plus a hand-rolled 60MB aggregate guard (multer's limit is per-file only), and a mime allowlist of jpeg/png/gif/webp/heic/heif/pdf.
- **service** `scanStatement(files, scannedById)` mirrors `vendor-bills.service.ts`'s `scanInvoice` closely — read that entry first, the differences are the point:
  - `hashFile(buffers)` (`../vendor-bills/invoice-scan.fingerprint`) → a prior non-DISCARDED scan with that hash returns immediately, **re-running the matcher against today's bills** (`rematchStoredScan`) but never re-calling the model.
  - **`STATEMENT_MODEL = "claude-sonnet-5"` is its own constant**, separate from the invoice scanner's, so the two can diverge: statements vary far more by supplier and a misread here moves money.
  - `new Anthropic({ apiKey, maxRetries: 0 })` + `{ timeout: 110_000 }`. **`maxRetries: 0` is load-bearing** — the SDK default would give each retry its own fresh 110s window and blow past the web client's 120s axios ceiling.
  - Key (2026-08-28): `platformConfig.resolveAnthropicKey(systemConfig.get("anthropic.apiKey"))` — tenant → platform → env; missing everywhere ⇒ plain `BadRequestException` with **no** `code`. Each model call records an `AiUsageEvent` (`ocr.supplier_statement`; tokens on success, `success:false` on call failure).
  - Same four typed error codes as the invoice scanner (401/403 ⇒ 400 `AI_KEY_INVALID`; other non-retryable 4xx excl. 429 ⇒ 400 `AI_SCAN_REJECTED`; 429/5xx/network ⇒ 503 `AI_UNAVAILABLE`; non-text block or `JSON.parse` throw ⇒ 422 `AI_PARSE_FAILED`). Same HEIC/HEIF → `sharp().rotate().jpeg({quality:85})` conversion with a failed page pushed to `skippedPages` and disclosed in `notes` rather than failing the upload; same ` ```json ` fence strip then `{[\s\S]*}` regex fallback before `JSON.parse`.
  - Helpers: `normalizeLines` (coerces `kind` to `STATEMENT_LINE_KINDS`, amounts to numbers), `resolveSupplierId` (name → tenant supplier), `matchAgainstBills`/`fetchMatchableBills`, `persistScan` + `storeScanFiles` (`supplier-statements/<scanId>/<n>.<ext>` via `StorageService`). Also `listScans`, `getScan`.
  - **`rematchStoredScan` also returns `appliedPayments`** (via `fetchAppliedPayments`, never throws): the `BillPayment` rows sharing the scan's `appliedPaymentGroupId`, each `{ id, billId, billNumber, amount, impliedPaid, paidAt }`. That is the read half of AC 9 — without it a scan reopened after the session that applied it shows a bare "already applied" date. `impliedPaid` is derived from the `STATEMENT-IMPLIED-` reference prefix `statement-apply.service.ts` stamps, the only thing distinguishing the two loops' rows.
- **`statement-matcher.ts` — PURE and deterministic; no Prisma import, no SDK, no model involvement.** Money-critical logic lives here specifically so it is auditable and unit-testable with zero mocks. Exports `MatchTier` (`EXACT_REF|FUZZY|UNMATCHED`), `MatchableBill`, `StatementLineMatch`, `MatchStatementLinesOptions`, `matchStatementLines(lines, bills, options)`.
  - Uses **`normalizeInvoiceNumber`** from `import/duplicate-match.service` (that is the real export name — the plan called it `normalizeNumber`).
  - Tolerances: amount `0.005`, date `±1 day`, closing balance `0.01`. VOID bills are filtered internally **whatever the caller passes**.
  - `preChecked` is true **only** for an `EXACT_REF` whose amount also agrees — an exact ref with a differing amount is still `EXACT_REF` (it IS the right document) but never pre-checked, and FUZZY is never pre-checked.
  - Collision resolution is **global, not per-line**: every (line, bill) opportunity is sorted by tier → amountDiff → dateDiff → lineIndex → billId and claimed greedily, so a bill is the PRIMARY match of at most one line and the same input always resolves identically. **`candidates` is deliberately left un-deduped** so the review screen's picker can still offer a bill another line won.
  - **Closing-balance sanity guard:** if `opening + Σ signed(lines)` doesn't reconcile to the stated `closingBalance`, **every** row is demoted out of `preChecked`. `signedAmount` treats INVOICE as +, PAYMENT/CREDIT as −, and passes ADJUSTMENT through with the model's own sign.
- **`statement-apply.service.ts`** — `StatementApplyService.applyStatement(scanId, dto, user)`, one `tenantTransaction`, PR-E's payment mechanics duplicated rather than importing `VendorBillsModule` (cycle). Exports the `ApplyStatement{ConfirmedLine,ImpliedPaid,Dto}` / `AppliedStatementPayment` / `ApplyStatementResult` shapes. Idempotency is checked **twice** — before the tx (cheap) and again inside it (closes the back-to-back race), mirroring `commitStockCountSession`; an already-`APPLIED` scan returns its stored `appliedPaymentGroupId` and writes nothing. Per confirmed bill, in order: bill exists → `bill.supplierId === scan.supplierId` → **`status !== "VOID"`** (the one status comparison; a void bill keeps `totalOwed` and the recompute would resurrect it) → a statement line actually backs it → `amount ≤ that line's own amount` → `amount ≤ totalOwed − Σ payments`. **The statement-line cap is re-derived by re-running `matchStatementLines` against CURRENT bills**, never a persisted match — the scan row stores only the raw parsed statement. Any violation rejects the whole apply. `impliedPaid` is a separate input, validated separately, never folded into `confirmed` — and rejects **`DRAFT` as well as `VOID`** (marking a never-received draft PAID would lock it out of edit/revert/delete and the needs-mapping queue permanently).
  - **`resolveBackingLine(matches, billId, lineIndex?)`** picks the capping line. It searches **every line's `candidates`**, not just `match.billId` — the review screen's picker offers all candidates, so a primary-pick-only lookup 400s on any hand-picked alternative and fails the whole apply. Optional `lineIndex` (sent by the web client) says which row the operator used and the bill must be a candidate of THAT line; without it, the bill's primary-pick line wins, then the first line listing it. The same `billId` twice in `confirmed` is rejected up front (one bill, one line).
  - **NEVER mints `SupplierCredit`** — `ApplyStatementResult.excess` is always `0` (kept only for wire parity with `recordSupplierPayment`). PR-E's excess is `dto.totalAmount − allocatedTotal`, i.e. real cash minus allocations; this DTO has **no cash total**, and every amount is already capped at the bill's outstanding, so a surplus is impossible. Banking `line.amount − confirmed` would fabricate spendable credit from an accounting discrepancy and let the tenant under-pay the supplier.
  - **`paidAt` is left to the column default (`now()`)**, never `line.date` — the matched line is an INVOICE line, so its date is the supplier's issue date; backdating would move cash into a closed period (`bookkeeping.getCashFlow` buckets by `paidAt`).
  - The tx opens with **`SELECT id FROM "SupplierStatementScan" … FOR UPDATE`** before re-reading status: `tenantTransaction` is READ COMMITTED, so without the lock two concurrent Applies both see `SCANNED` and both write a full payment set under different groups.
- **`dto/statement.dto.ts`** — interfaces `ScanStatementFile`, `StatementLineKind` + `STATEMENT_LINE_KINDS`, `ParsedStatementLine`, `ParsedStatement` (internal shapes, no validation — the scan body is multipart), **plus the `class-validator` classes for the one JSON body**: `ApplyStatementBodyDto` (`confirmed` `@ValidateNested`+`ArrayMaxSize(500)`, optional `impliedPaid`, optional `notes` `@MaxLength(1000)`+`@StripHtml`), `ApplyStatementConfirmedDto` (`@IsUUID` billId, `@IsNumber`+`@Min(0)` amount, optional `@IsInt`+`@Min(0)` `lineIndex`), `ApplyStatementImpliedPaidDto` (`@IsUUID(…,{each})` billIds). It is the validated wire form of the service's `ApplyStatementDto` interface — the controller call site is what compiler-enforces the two staying identical.
- **specs** `statement-matcher.spec.ts` (pure, no mocks), `supplier-statements.service.spec.ts` (hash short-circuit skips the model; each of the four error codes), `statement-apply.spec.ts` (double-apply writes once; over-cap rejects and writes nothing; **no credit is ever minted from a line/confirmed gap**; `paidAt` never backdated; a non-suggested candidate is accepted and a wrong `lineIndex` rejected; the same bill twice rejects; an implied-paid DRAFT rejects; `FOR UPDATE` precedes the in-tx status re-read; one payment group ties the rows).

### `tobacco/` (tobacco_dealer addon)

- **controller** `tobacco` — class-level `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard, AddonGuard)` + `@RequireAddon("tobacco_dealer")`: overview, inventory, purchases (PURCHASE movements of `isTobacco` products w/ supplier `tobaccoLicenseNo`), sales (InvoiceItems on non-DRAFT/VOID/WRITTEN_OFF invoices w/ customer license, tax = subtotal×taxRate), monthly, reports list/generate/:id/{csv,pdf}, settings (GET; PATCH = TENANT_ADMIN, writes SystemConfig `tobacco.excludeFromMainAnalytics`, audit-logged).
- **`tobacco-report.service.ts`** — `generateForPeriod(year,month,{userId?})` (past months only): per-product rows + totals, ending stock = `currentStock − Σ(movements after periodEnd)` (SALE rows negative), ending value at CURRENT averageCost (disclosed); CSV + react-pdf PDF at deterministic keys `tobacco-reports/<tenantId>/<yyyy-MM>.{csv,pdf}`; upsert per (tenant,period) via findFirst+create/update (forTenant can't composite-upsert), `generationCount` bump + audit on regen. `@Cron("0 2 1 * *")`: active tenants ∩ active addons, per-tenant `tenantCtx.run`, idempotent skip, FAILED rows on error. Template `tobacco-report-pdf.tsx` (jest-mocked via moduleNameMapper like the invoice template).
- **Test infra (2026-07-23): `ioredis` globally mocked** — jest `moduleNameMapper` maps `^ioredis$` → `test/__mocks__/ioredis.js` (inert socket-free stub). Services that construct a live `new Redis(...)` (`auth/google-oauth.service.ts`, `common/redis-throttler.storage.ts`) were opening a real TCP connect to `localhost:6379` in specs (e.g. google-oauth `this.redis.set(state)`); the failed-connect reconnect timer lingered in a jest worker → intermittent "worker failed to exit gracefully" hang that forced `--forceExit`. Root-fixed; no unit spec asserts real Redis behavior, prod untouched. `npm run test -w apps/api` now exits cleanly without `--forceExit`.
- **Analytics exclusion** — see `analytics/` note; helper `tobaccoExclusionActive()` = addon active AND config key true. Schema: `Product.isTobacco`, `TobaccoReport`, `Supplier.tobaccoLicenseNo`, `Customer.tobaccoLicenseNo/Expiry` (migration `20260704100000_tobacco_compliance`). **Phase 4 (W1) generalizes `isTobacco` → `Product.trackedCategoryId` (FK to `TrackedCategory`); `isTobacco` is kept as a SHADOW column for one release, so all reads here still use it until W2 flips them.**

### tracked-categories / regulated (Phase 4 — generalizes tobacco)

- **Schema (W1, migration `20260706120000_regulated_items_foundation`)** — 4 new tenant-scoped models + 6 enums, all ADDITIVE:
  - **`TrackedCategory`** — tenant-defined regulated class (tobacco=seed row #1). Fields: `name`, `taxType` (EXCISE_PER_UNIT|PERCENT_OF_SALE|PER_VOLUME|DEPOSIT_PER_CONTAINER|NONE), `rate` Decimal(12,4), `unitBasis`, `priceIncludesTax`, `invoiceTreatment` (SEPARATE_INVOICE|SEPARATE_SECTION|LINE_TAX), `appliesScope` Json, `requiresLicense`, `reportTemplate`, `reportCadence`, `active`. `@@unique([tenantId,name])`.
  - **`CustomerAuthorization`** — per-customer, per-category license state for one seller. `status` (NONE|PENDING_REVIEW|VERIFIED|EXPIRED|REJECTED), `source` (RETAILER_SUBMITTED|WHOLESALER_ADDED), `licenseNumber`, `expiresAt`, `documentKey`, `verifiedBy{Id,Name}/At` (FK-less actor SNAPSHOT). `@@unique([customerId,trackedCategoryId])`.
  - **`AuthorizationOverride`** — seller "sold under responsibility" audit record (`scope`, `reason`, `acknowledgedTenant`, `acceptedBy{Id,Name}/At`); append-only, FK-less actor snapshot survives user deletion.
  - **`RegulatedSalesLedger`** — immutable per-line regulated-sales ledger (source of truth for filings). `entryType` (SALE|REVERSAL, amounts negative on reversal), order/invoice/invoiceItem/creditNote pointers (plain, no FK), `qty`, `unitBasisQty`, `netSales`, `categoryTax`, `soldAt`, `periodBucket` ("YYYY-MM"). **RF-3 (`20260726000000_add_ledger_subcategory`, additive):** nullable `trackedSubcategoryId` + `@@index` + a **hard** `trackedSubcategory` FK (`onDelete:SetNull` — a subcat delete keeps the section attribution; back-relation `TrackedSubcategory.ledgerEntries`), for the reporting/filing breakdown ONLY (never read by guards or money math).
  - **New columns**: `Product.trackedCategoryId` (FK, SetNull); `OrderItem`/`InvoiceItem`.{`trackedCategoryId`(FK,SetNull),`categoryTaxAmount`}; `Invoice.invoiceGroupId` (paired siblings); `Order.hasRegulated`; `RouteRunStop.{ageCheckRequired,identityCheckRequired}`.
  - **Backfill** (in-migration, idempotent): one Tobacco category per tenant with `isTobacco` products; link those products; flag orders with a tobacco line `hasRegulated`. Tobacco seed = `taxType=NONE` + `requiresLicense=false` (**exact current warn-only behavior preserved** — enabling tax/license is an explicit W2/W6 per-tenant action, never an auto-flip), CA_CDTFA/MONTHLY.
- **`tracked-categories/` module (W2, wired)** — CRUD for `TrackedCategory`, tenant-scoped via `prisma.forTenant()`, guard `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard) @Roles(OPERATOR)` (TENANT_ADMIN satisfies OPERATOR; no addon gate — generic feature). Routes: `GET /tracked-categories` (search + active filter, returns each w/ `productCount`), `GET /:id`, `POST /` (injects tenantId; P2002→409), `PATCH /:id`, `PATCH /:id/toggle` (flip active), `POST /:id/products/assign` + `/unassign` (bulk set/clear `Product.trackedCategoryId` via `updateMany`). **Assign sets ONLY the generic pointer — `isTobacco` stays owned by the product flow this release, so tobacco reports are unaffected.** Web hooks `apps/web/lib/api/tracked-categories.ts` (TanStack: `useTrackedCategories`/`useTrackedCategory`/`useCreate|Update|Toggle`/`useAssign|UnassignProductsToCategory`). Spec `tracked-categories.service.spec.ts`. Prisma-mock gained the 4 Phase-4 models.
  - **Subcategories (Phase 4b, `20260719000000_add_tracked_subcategories`, ADDITIVE)** — NEW `TrackedSubcategory` model (classification child of a section; `@@unique[tenantId,trackedCategoryId,name]`, `name`+`active` only, **no** license/tax/invoice semantics — those stay on the section). Nested CRUD on the SAME controller (`@Roles(OPERATOR)`): `GET/POST /tracked-categories/:id/subcategories`, `PATCH /:id/subcategories/:subId`, `.../toggle`; `getSubcategoryOrThrow` enforces section+tenant ownership. Nullable `trackedSubcategoryId` added to `Product`/`OrderItem`/`InvoiceItem` (SetNull). **RF-3 (2026-07-16) now wires the full reporting breakdown:** `orders.service` snapshots `OrderItem.trackedSubcategoryId` at sale time at every write site (create nested + updateOrderItems buyer-edit/operator-replace/individual-add/substitute + change-request add-at-door; product fetches read full rows so it's available); `invoices.service.buildInvoiceItemData` + `create()`/`update()` snapshot `InvoiceItem.trackedSubcategoryId` (order-line snapshot → live-product fallback; the 6 order-line product selects now select it); all 3 `writeSaleEntries` call sites (create RF-1 / createSplitInvoices / resyncInvoiceLedger) pass it onto the SALE row, and every reversal (`reverseInvoiceEntries`/`reverseReturnEntries`/`reverseCreditNoteEntries`) carries it through (pure passthrough, no money/cap change). **Still deferred (Phase E):** filing-side aggregation — `prepareFiling` still groups by section only (see below). `products.service` create/update now accept `trackedCategoryId`+`trackedSubcategoryId` (DTOs, `emptyToNull` transform to allow clearing) with app-side validation `assertSubcategoryInSection` (subcategory must belong to the chosen section; clearing the section auto-clears the subcategory; variants inherit the parent's pair). `findOne` include gained `trackedSubcategory{id,name,trackedCategoryId}`.
- **`regulated/` module (W5a, wired)** — the regulated-sales ledger. `regulated-ledger.service.ts`: **`writeSaleEntries`** (one `RegulatedSalesLedger` SALE row per REGULATED invoice line, built from the created `invoice.items` so `invoiceItemId` is real; `netSales`=line subtotal, `categoryTax`=snapshot [0 today]; `periodBucket`=UTC YYYY-MM of `issueDate`; skips standard lines + null-tenant) hooked into `invoices.service.createSplitInvoices`; **`reverseInvoiceEntries`** (negated REVERSAL row per prior SALE, idempotent via already-reversed `invoiceItemId` set, books into current period) hooked into `voidInvoice` + `deleteInvoice` (inside their `tenantTransaction`). **W5c: `reverseReturnEntries`** (return→ledger: bridges ReturnItem.productId→InvoiceItem→SALE rows by `orderId`, PRO-RATES partial returns off the LIVE SALE row's snapshot with a closing-chunk exact-balance so a fully-returned line nets to 0, idempotent per `returnId` [new nullable column]; the cumulative-reversal cap is keyed on the **ORDER LINE** [`orderItemId ?? invoiceItemId`] and computed as the per-line NET of ALL its ledger rows [Σ signed SALE+REVERSAL across pre/post-reconcile invoice items] so cumulative reversal ≤ current un-reversed balance and SURVIVES a delivered-basis reconcile that rotates invoiceItemId — a 2nd full return after a reconcile floors at 0; ignores `restock`) + **`unreverseReturnEntries`** (delete on cancel) hooked into `returns.service` `receive()`/`cancel()`; both `receive`/`cancel` gained an atomic `updateMany`-status CLAIM to serialize concurrent calls (no double-reverse/double-restock). `regulated.service.getLedger` = `GET /regulated/ledger?category=&from=&to=&bySubcategory=` (forTenant groupBy category+period, `_sum` of pre-signed amounts nets SALE+REVERSAL). **RF-3:** `ListLedgerDto.bySubcategory` (bool query flag) adds `trackedSubcategoryId` to the groupBy `by` array and joins `TrackedSubcategory.name` onto each row (`trackedSubcategoryId`+`subcategoryName`); default (unset) is byte-identical — no extra `by` dim, no subcat keys on rows. `period.ts` = `periodBucketOf`/`monthRange`. Controller `@Roles(OPERATOR)`, no addon gate. Spec `regulated-ledger.service.spec.ts`.
  - **`regulated-filing.service.ts` (W5b, wired)** — the generic superset of `tobacco-report.service`. **`prepareFiling`** ((re)generates the filing for a PAST period: past-period guard on `to`; resolves cadence from param or `category.reportCadence`; `regulated.getLedger(..., {exclusiveTo:true})` for an EXACT half-open `[from,to)` window; filters rows to the `filingPeriod` bucket set [honors per-period netting]; sums pre-signed nets [never re-signs/clamps — reversal-heavy period nets negative]; `roundMoney` money + `roundQty` 3dp so JSON rows == DB Decimal == CSV; `findFirst`+create/update upsert with **P2002 retry-as-update**; audit `regulated_filing.prepared`); **`listFilings`**, **`downloadUrl`** (presigned CSV). `filing-csv.ts` = `buildFilingCsv` (reportTemplate-driven columns CA_CDTFA/CA_ABC/CALRECYCLE/GENERIC-fallback + coverage disclosure footer). **RF-3:** optional `FilingCsvData.withSubcategory` splices a "Subcategory" column in after Period (blank when null, comma-escaped); off by default → byte-identical. NOTE: `prepareFiling` does NOT yet set it (still section-only aggregation) — the CSV column + `getLedger bySubcategory` are the wired reporting surfaces; threading subcategory rows through the persisted filing is the remaining Phase-E work. `period.ts` gained **`filingPeriod`** (MONTHLY/QUARTERLY/ANNUAL → periodKey + buckets + UTC `[from,to)`). Routes `GET /regulated/filings`, `POST /regulated/filings/prepare`, `GET /regulated/filings/:id/{csv,pdf}`. Model `RegulatedFiling` (`@@unique[tenantId,trackedCategoryId,periodKey]`, FK `onDelete:Restrict`), migration `20260707120000_add_regulated_filing` (additive). **RF-5: `regulated-filing-cron.service.ts`** — `@Cron("0 4 * * *")` sweeps every tenant's active TrackedCategories and auto-prepares the most-recently-closed period per its `reportCadence`. `previousClosedPeriod(cadence,now)` = absolute-index arithmetic (`y*12+m-1` / `y*4+q-1`) so Jan→Dec / Q1→Q4 prior-year rollover needs no special-casing; runs each prepare inside `tenantCtx.run(cat.tenantId, …)` so `prepareFiling`'s `getTenantId`/`forTenant` resolve; idempotent (skips a period whose `RegulatedFiling` already exists — belt-and-suspenders with the `@@unique`); per-tenant try/catch. Registered in `RegulatedModule` providers. **RF-2-lite:** `invoices.service.findOne` items include now joins `trackedCategory{id,name,invoiceTreatment}` + `trackedSubcategory{id,name}` so clients can label regulated lines (was id-only). Web: filing hooks in `tracked-categories.ts` + compliance-hub "Prepare filing" + filings list. **Uploads** now tenant-scope `regulated-filings/`+`tobacco-reports/` prefixes (JWT path). Specs: `regulated-filing.service.spec.ts`, `filing-csv.spec.ts`, `period.spec.ts`, `regulated.service.spec.ts`, `uploads-tenant-scope.security.spec.ts`.
  - **Per-product TX config + custom report columns (2026-07-30 fix round) — supersedes WP11's category-level TX config below.** New `regulated/template-registry.ts` (`REPORT_TEMPLATES`, `templateByKey`/`defaultColumnKeys`/`allColumnKeys`/`isValidItemType`/`isValidUom`/`itemTypeLabel`) is the single source for report-template vocabulary (item types → their legal UoM codes) and each template's column superset; served by new `GET /regulated/templates` (`RegulatedController.getTemplates`), retiring the hardcoded TX vocab copies previously duplicated in web and mobile. `Product` gained `regItemType`/`regUomCase`/`regUomUnit` (migration `20260801000200_product_regulatory_reporting_config`, additive; 2 idempotent COALESCE backfills seed them from `TrackedCategory.txItemType`/`txUom` for products in a TX section — one by current `trackedCategoryId`, one by historic invoice-line snapshot for products since detached; `regUomCase` deliberately left NULL). `TrackedCategory.txItemType`/`txUom` are now DEPRECATED shadow columns — no reader, kept one release only because `forbidNonWhitelisted` would 400 an older client that still sends them. `TrackedCategory.reportColumnPrefs` (Json, same migration) stores a saved column layout per template (`{ "TX_COMPTROLLER": ["wholesalerPermit", ...] }`); `tracked-categories.service.ts#toData()` maps a bare `null` → `Prisma.DbNull`; `create`/`update` gate it through `assertValidReportColumnPrefs` (every key a known `templateByKey`, every value a non-empty array of that template's `allColumnKeys`). `tx-report.ts#buildTxReport` now resolves item type/UoM PER PRODUCT (`TxProductConfig`, joined via `linesById`/`productsById` off the invoice line the ledger row was booked from) instead of per category — one regulated section can legitimately hold cigarettes, cigars and loose tobacco at once — and buckets by `(invoiceId, itemType, uom)`, or by `(invoiceId, productId, uom)` once the optional `itemDescription` column is requested (a product-name column forces per-product rows). **Back-compat invariant — do not touch:** a product's `regUomCase == null` ⇒ its ledger rows' signed `unitBasisQty` passes through RAW, no `unitsPerBox` conversion, one bucket per invoice; this is what makes a backfilled tenant (every product's `regUomCase` NULL) reproduce pre-change report numbers byte-for-byte — case/unit splitting is a per-product opt-in, never automatic. Two new warnings: `UNMATCHED_LEDGER_LINE` (a ledger row's `invoiceItemId` no longer resolves — the ledger carries no FK and a delivered-basis reconcile rotates ids) and `UNLISTED_PRODUCT_LINE` (a resolved line has no catalog product, so no item type/UoM to report). New `regulated/report-projection.ts#projectReportColumns(report, keys)` remaps `columns`/`rows`/`totalsRow` through one index map built from `report.columns`, so a custom layout's JSON preview and CSV download can never disagree; `warnings`/`displayTotals`/`csv.preamble`/`csv.footer` pass through untouched. `regulated-report.service.ts#buildReport` gained a `columns` query param (`ReportQueryDto.columns`, comma-separated keys, order = output order): validates against `template-registry.allColumnKeys`, computes `isCustom` by ordered comparison against `defaultColumnKeys`, then `projectReportColumns`s the built report; a custom layout sets `report.custom = true`, forces `csv.includeHeader = true` (the official TX layout stays headerless/filing-ready) and adds a `-custom` filename token. `products.service.ts` — `assertRegConfigValid` validates `regItemType`/`regUomCase`/`regUomUnit` against the section's `reportTemplate` via the registry; a variant with no explicit trio inherits its parent's; `update()` gates on a real TRANSITION, never on an echo (both product forms resend the section AND all three codes on every save): it re-validates the whole EFFECTIVE trio iff a trio value actually CHANGES (`dto[f] !== undefined && (dto[f] ?? null) !== (existing[f] ?? null)` — a key merely being PRESENT is not a change) OR the effective section id differs from the stored one (`sectionChanged`, `?? null`-normalized so a detached product echoing `trackedCategoryId: null` isn't a move); and it clears all three iff the section TRANSITIONS non-null→null (`clearingSection`), which also suppresses the validation (`!clearingSection` is load-bearing — a legitimate clear would otherwise 400 on "requires a regulated type" before the auto-clear ran). A section MOVE carrying codes the new template can't express is REJECTED (400), never auto-cleared: the ledger is append-only and keeps the OLD section while the report resolves item type/UoM LIVE from the product row, so silently nulling `regUomCase` would flip the raw-vs-per-case basis above and restate already-filed periods. Both forms clear the trio client-side on a section change, so that 400 only reaches API-direct callers (imports/older clients), which must send explicit nulls alongside the new section. Consequences: a stale-config product left behind by a template switch survives unrelated PATCHes (rename, price) untouched with no `trackedCategory` query; a sectionless product still 400s on a request that CHANGES its codes (null→null is no transition, but the change gate catches it — matching `create()`) while an echo of its stranded codes is preserved; an unconfigured product moves between sections with no lookup at all (`assertRegConfigValid` returns early on an empty trio). Web Quick-Edit "Type" PATCHes only the section pair, so quick-editing a TX-configured product into a non-TX template now 400s (loud, recoverable via the detail form). Specs: `template-registry.spec.ts`, `report-projection.spec.ts`, TX per-product cases in `tx-report.spec.ts`.
  - **WP11 (2026-07-30, wired) — stateless range-preview reports + TX Comptroller.** New shared row model `report-types.ts` (`RegulatedReport`: `columns`/`rows`(unescaped formatted cells)/`totalsRow`/`displayTotals`/`warnings`/`csv{preamble,includeHeader,includeTotals,footer}`) feeds BOTH the JSON preview and the CSV download from one value. `report-csv.ts` = `esc` (moved verbatim from `filing-csv.ts`) + `serializeReportCsv(report)` (the ONLY place CSV-escaping happens). `filing-csv.ts` refactored: `buildAggregateReport(template, data & {periodLabel, categoryId?, from?, to?})` builds the `RegulatedReport` for CA*CDTFA/CA_ABC/CALRECYCLE/GENERIC; `buildFilingCsv` is now `serializeReportCsv(buildAggregateReport(...))` — **byte-identical**, `filing-csv.spec.ts` passes unmodified (the byte-identity gate). New `tx-report.ts` (pure, no Prisma): `buildTxReport({category,ledgerRows,invoicesById,customersById,from,to})` groups pre-signed `RegulatedSalesLedger` rows by `invoiceId` (nets SALE+REVERSAL automatically), drops rows that round to zero, emits the 12 TX Comptroller columns (Wholesaler Permit #/Retailer Taxpayer ID/Retailer Name/Street/City/State/ZIP/Retailer Permit #/Item Type/UOM/Quantity/Invoice Amount — no CSV header row), rounds qty+amount to whole numbers, truncates text fields to spec lengths, sorts by issue date then invoice number; exports `digitsOnly`/`pickReportAddress`(default+BILLING→any BILLING→any default→first)/`TX_UOM_CODES`/`TX_ITEM_TYPE_LABELS`; warns via `ReportWarningCode` (category-level MISSING_WHOLESALER_LICENSE/MISSING_ITEM_TYPE/MISSING_UOM deduped once per report; per-invoice MISSING/INVALID_TAXPAYER_ID, MISSING/INVALID_RETAILER_LICENSE, MISSING_ADDRESS, NEGATIVE_NET_INVOICE, FRACTIONAL_QTY; report-level UNLINKED_LEDGER_ROWS for null-`invoiceId` rows). New `regulated-report.service.ts` (`RegulatedReportService`, exported from `RegulatedModule`): `buildReport({category,from,to,template?})` validates `YYYY-MM-DD`+`from<=to`+≤366-inclusive-day span, queries the half-open `[from,to+1day)` window; non-TX templates reuse `regulated.getLedger(...,{exclusiveTo:true})` → `buildAggregateReport`; TX does a **manual 3-step join** via public `buildTxReportForRange(category,fromDate,toExclusive,labels)` (`RegulatedSalesLedger` has no Prisma relations: `regulatedSalesLedger.findMany` → `invoice.findMany` → `customer.findMany` with `addresses`+`authorizations` filtered to the category) — reused by `regulated-filing.service.ts`. `buildReportCsv(...)` → `{csv, filename: "<slug>-<template>-<from>-<to>.csv"}` — the template is caller-supplied (`?template=`, echoed back verbatim even on the unknown→GENERIC fallback) and the filename is interpolated into a quoted `Content-Disposition`, so it goes through `safeToken()` (`[^A-Za-z0-9*-]`→`-`, case preserved) to keep quotes/semicolons/CRLF out of the header. Controller gained `GET /regulated/reports/preview`(JSON) +`GET /regulated/reports/csv` (`text/csv`via`@Res({passthrough:true})`), registered above `filings/:id/...`, DTO `dto/report-query.dto.ts` (`category`/`from`/`to` YYYY-MM-DD/`template?`). `prepareFiling`now branches on`category.reportTemplate==="TX_COMPTROLLER"`: calls `buildTxReportForRange`and stores`serializeReportCsv(txReport)`as the filing CSV with`rows: {txRows, warnings}`in the Json column (the 4 Decimal totals are UNCHANGED — still the ledger aggregate either way).`TrackedCategory`gained 3 nullable columns (WP0, migration`20260801000100_add_tx_comptroller_config`): `wholesalerLicenseNo`/`txItemType`(1=Cigarettes/2=Cigars/3=Tobacco)/`txUom`; threaded through `tracked-categories/dto/{create,update}-tracked-category.dto.ts` (`@IsIn([1,2,3])`/`@IsIn(TX_UOM_CODES flattened)`, imported from `regulated/tx-report.ts`) — `tracked-categories.service.ts`needed NO change for those (its`toData()`already spreads all DTO fields generically) — but the later `reportColumnPrefs`Json column DID:`toData()`now destructures it out and maps`null`→`Prisma.DbNull`(a bare`null`is not a legal Prisma input for a nullable Json column), alongside the existing`appliesScope`cast;`create`/`update`gate it through`assertValidReportColumnPrefs`(every key a known`templateByKey`, every value a non-empty array of that template's `allColumnKeys`; `null` clears). Specs:`report-csv.spec.ts`(4 csv.* section toggles),`tx-report.spec.ts`, `regulated-report.service.spec.ts`(mocked Prisma), TX block added to`regulated-filing.service.spec.ts`.
  - **Credit-note ledger reversal (W5c, wired)** — `credit-notes.service` injects `RegulatedLedgerService`: issuing a credit note against a regulated sale calls `reverseCreditNoteEntries` (negated rows, idempotent per creditNoteId, tenant-scoped; `liveSaleByItem` = credited invoice item's live SALE for the SALE-existence guard + reversal source-id stamping; the over-reversal cap keys on the **ORDER LINE** via `saleByLine`/`revByLine` over ALL the line's SALE+REVERSAL rows [pulled by `orderItemId in orderLineIds OR invoiceItemId in invoiceItemIds`] so a 2nd full credit after a reconcile-rotated invoiceItemId floors at 0); voiding it calls `unreverseCreditNoteEntries`. **Creation** (`create`) enforces BOTH a cumulative header cap (Σ non-void credit-note amounts ≤ invoice total) AND a cumulative **per-line** cap (Σ prior non-void `CreditNoteItem.amount` per `invoiceItemId` + new amt ≤ line subtotal) inside the Serializable tx, so an under-credited line's slack can't over-credit another line. The per-line creation cap is BEST-EFFORT across a reconcile (it keys on `invoiceItemId`, which a reconcile rotates, and `CreditNoteItem` has no `orderItemId` to bridge the deleted pre-reconcile item) — a post-reconcile double-credit of a line is caught not here but by the stable header invoice-total cap (AR) + the ledger's order-line-keyed `reverseCreditNoteEntries` cap (filing floors at 0); it's a within-invoice line-attribution quirk, never a net over-refund or negative filing. New `CreditNoteItem` model + migration `20260710000000_add_credit_note_item` (additive); mirrors the invoice-void + returns reversal patterns. Specs `credit-notes.service.spec`, `regulated-ledger.service.spec`. **W5 ledger coverage — manual-create + draft-update now WIRED (RF-1):** `invoices.service.create()` snapshots each product line's `trackedCategoryId`/`trackedSubcategoryId` and, inside a `tenantTransaction`, calls `writeSaleEntries` (orderId null) for regulated lines — so a hand-built regulated invoice reaches the filing; `update()`'s items path re-snapshots the category and (atomically, in a tx) calls `resyncInvoiceLedger` (reverse prior SALE via `reverseInvoiceEntries({preserveReturns:true})` + rewrite from the new items) so a draft edit that changes qty/price or adds/removes a regulated line keeps the ledger in step (no-op for non-regulated). **Still DEFERRED:** partial-invoice (`createPartialFromOrder`) ledger sync; `orderItemId` provenance; `unitBasisQty` conversion; filing cron; compliance-hub `/regulated/ledger` KPI wiring; **`SEPARATE_SECTION`/`LINE_TAX` invoice presentation** — `invoices.service.groupOrderLinesForInvoicing` (:465-515) only hives off `invoiceTreatment==="SEPARATE_INVOICE"` categories into paired sibling invoices (`invoiceGroupId`); `SEPARATE_SECTION`/`LINE_TAX` regulated lines fold flat into the standard invoice with a warn log (:497-501). Rendering regulated lines under an in-invoice section HEADING (web detail/PDF/mobile) is unbuilt — the per-line `InvoiceItem.trackedCategoryId` snapshot is returned but the category NAME is never joined onto invoice payloads (`findOne` items include is product-only, :1325-1327).
