- **`authorizations/` module (W6a, wired)** — customer authorization lifecycle + regulated-sale license guard + §8 override. `authorizations.service` (lifecycle `create`[WHOLESALER_ADDED→VERIFIED, upsert+P2002 retry], `approve`/`reject`[PENDING_REVIEW only], `renew`[VERIFIED/EXPIRED only — blocks laundering], `findForCustomer`; **W6b: `submit`[buyer self-serve → RETAILER_SUBMITTED/PENDING_REVIEW, same upsert+P2002, clears verifier snapshot, refuses to clobber a live VERIFIED row, notifies seller operators push+email, audits `regulated_authorization.submitted` w/ shareConsent] + `listForBuyer`[every requiresLicense category ⟕ buyer's row → status NONE/lazy-EXPIRED]**; validates customer+category belong to tenant; immutable verifier snapshot). Now injects Notifications+Email; **module exports `AuthorizationsService`** (was guard-only) for buyer reuse. `authorization-overrides.service` (§8 `createOverride`[append-only row + AuditLog], `isOverrideActive`[scope parse]). `authorization-guard.service` **`checkAuthorized`/`assertAuthorizedOrThrow`** — the enforcement: for each line's `requiresLicense` category, customer needs a VERIFIED non-expired auth OR active override, else structured 409 `REGULATED_AUTH_REQUIRED`. **Tobacco (requiresLicense=false) is NEVER gated** (warn-only preserved — the release gate). Lazy expiry (`expiresAt<now`). `authorization-scope.ts` = `isScopeActive`(ORDER/UNTIL, fail-CLOSED) + `scopeApplies`(city geo, fail-CLOSED on unknown city). Controller `@Controller("customers/:customerId")` `@Roles(OPERATOR)`: `GET/POST authorizations`, `POST authorizations/:id/{approve,reject,renew}`, `POST authorization-overrides`. **Guard hooks in `orders.service`**: `create()` (before stock tx), `updateOrderItems()` (edit/buyer-merge — before mutation), `changeStatus()` (DRAFT→live promotion) — all non-draft, orderId passed for ORDER-scoped overrides. No migration (W1 models). Specs: `authorization-{guard,scope}.spec`, `authorizations.service.spec`, `authorization-overrides.service.spec` + orders guard-block tests.
  - **`authorization-expiry.service` (W7 part 1, wired)** — daily `@Cron("0 3 * * *")` `runExpirySweep` (mirrors tobacco cron: active tenants w/ ≥1 requiresLicense category, each inside `tenantCtx.run`). `processTenant` flips VERIFIED past-`expiresAt` → EXPIRED (guard already lazy-blocks; makes status truthful + audits `regulated_authorization.expired`) and sends 30/7/1-day expiring-soon warnings to buyer (`sendToCustomer`) + operators (`sendToUser`+email). Idempotency via `CustomerAuthorization.expiryNotifiedAt` + `expiringSoonNotifiedBucket` (re-fires only as bucket shrinks; reset on create/approve/renew). Tobacco (requiresLicense=false) never swept. Migration `20260709000000_add_authorization_expiry_notify` (2 nullable cols + `[status,expiresAt]` index, additive). Spec `authorization-expiry.service.spec`.
  - **Buyer authorizations endpoint (W6b, wired)** — `buyer.controller` `GET/POST /buyer/authorizations` (seller-scoped: `BuyerSellerContextGuard` + `BuyerTenantInterceptor`; `customerId` from `@CurrentBuyerCustomer()` ctx, never the client) → `authorizations.service.listForBuyer`/`submit`. `BuyerModule` imports `AuthorizationsModule`. DTO `submit-authorization.dto` (license#+expiry required, `shareConsent` must be true). No migration (W1 models). Specs: buyer submit/list cases in `authorizations.service.spec`, delegation cases in `buyer.controller.spec`. **Per-seller by design** — `TrackedCategory` is per-tenant so one submission can't fan out across sellers; each connected seller verifies independently (spec §8), reached via the portal seller switcher.
  - **Invoice-time backstop (W6b, wired)** — the license guard re-runs in `invoices.service.createSplitInvoices` (inside the tx) to catch a regulated line added via buyer-merge after order creation and a license that EXPIRED between order creation and invoicing. See `invoices/` entry.
  - **Still DEFERRED:** license-document upload on both operator + buyer sides (`documentKey` optional/unwired — matches operator create); §8 create-path override post-create binding (run guard inside the order tx to get orderId); geo `deliveryCity` threading into the create hook; cross-seller license propagation (needs a cross-tenant category-identity concept).
  - **Buyer visibility gate (W7 part 3, wired)** — `buyer-catalog.service.computeGate` (2 batched queries, no N+1) hides `requiresLicense` categories the buyer has no VERIFIED-non-expired auth for (predicate matches the sale guard). `getCatalog` excludes them via `products.service.findAll(query, {excludeTrackedCategoryIds})` (query-level → correct pagination) + returns `hiddenCategories`; `getProductDetail` 404s a locked deep-link; `getFavorites` filters. Fast-paths empty for non-regulated tenants. Spec `buyer-catalog.service.spec`.
  - **Catalogue v2 (P5-02, additive):** `products.service.findAll` gained an internal-only `opts.andWhere` escape hatch (extra AND clauses, never from client input) folded after the stock/search OR. `BuyerCatalogService` injects `ReplenishmentService`+`PromotionsService`; `getCatalog` gained a `collection` param (usuals/favorites/new/deals — resolved server-side via `andWhere` so pagination counts stay correct AND the regulated exclusion still composes) + `sort=best` (replenishment-frequency slice, fetch-all+slice like the price sorts); rows now carry `isNew`/`isDeal`/`imageUrls` (presigned ≤4, dot-pager)/`stockLeft` (only when LOW). NEW `getCatalogCounts` (→ `GET /buyer/products/counts`, declared BEFORE `products/:id`) returns total + per-category groupBy counts + collection counts + locked categories — **every count carries `baseWhere.trackedCategoryId notIn hidden`** so a locked category is never countable. Shared private `dealsWhere(promos)` (isDeal OR active-promo scope; ALL→`{}`) used by both the deals collection + the counts so they can't drift. **`ids` filter (2026-08-21, #382):** `GET /buyer/products?ids=a,b` (deduped, capped 200) → `getCatalog({ids})` composes `{id:{in}}` through the same `andWhere` path so the regulated gate still applies, and forces fetch-all (a page boundary is what priced cart lines at $0). Spec `buyer-catalog.service.spec` (catalogue v2 block). No migration (all columns pre-existed).
  - **Detail↔listing payload parity (2026-08-20, review fix):** `getProductDetail` returned a NARROWER object than the listing — no `unitsPerBox`/`thumbnailUrl`/`isNew`/`isDeal`/`isFeatured`/`inStock`/`stockStatus`/`stockLeft` — while web's `BuyerProductDetail extends BuyerProduct` declared them present, so TS hid the gap: the buyer detail page added **1 loose piece** for a boxed product (tile adds 1 box) and read **"In stock"** for an OOS one (Notify-me unreachable, Add enabled). All of those now come back, with the availability triple from a shared private **`deriveStock(p)`** used by BOTH `getCatalog` and `getProductDetail` (they can never drift again). `variants[]` rows additionally carry `category`+`unitsPerBox` so each row prices through `deriveTilePrice` like its own tile instead of showing a raw pre-promo `buyerPrice`. Spec: `buyer-catalog.service.spec` "tile↔detail parity". **Per-variant tier (same fix round):** the tier used to come from ONE `customerPrice.findFirst` for the parent and was reused for every variant row; `getProductDetail` now batch-loads overrides for `[product.id, ...variantIds]` into a `cpMap` (exactly like `getCatalog`) and prices each variant with `cpMap.get(v.id) ?? defaultTier`, so a variant carrying its own override quotes the same price here as on its own tile/detail page. **Inactive variants dropped (same round):** `products.service.findOne` includes variants with NO `isActive` filter (it only sorts `isActive:"desc"`), so a discontinued variant was listed with a price whose link dead-ended on this endpoint's own `isActive` 404; `getProductDetail` now filters `v.isActive !== false` once (`activeVariants`) and uses it for BOTH the `cpMap` id list and the mapped rows. Spec: "omits deactivated variants".
  - **Regulated-delivery POD gate (W7b backend, wired)** — `common/regulated-delivery.ts` (pure, `regulated-delivery.spec`): `loadAgeIdCategorySets`→`deriveStopRegulatedRequirements(db,{stopId,orderItemIds},sets)` derives the age/ID requirement from the UNION of the stop's linked orders **and** the delivered `orderItemIds` (closes a cross-stop/unlinked bypass); `assertRegulatedDeliverySatisfied` throws `400 REGULATED_POD_REQUIRED` (`reason` SAFE_DROP_FORBIDDEN|SIGNATURE_REQUIRED|AGE_CHECK_REQUIRED|ID_CHECK_REQUIRED|ID_TYPE_REQUIRED) + returns the stop patch (`identityVerifiedAt` server-stamped). Wired into **both** live completion paths — `routes.service.completeStop` (the LIVE `POST /route-runs/:id/stops/:stopId/complete`) + `routes.service.completeWithPayment` (`.../complete-with-payment`). **(The former `orders.service.completeStop` shadow duplicate + its `RouteRunDeliveryController` and `orders/dto/complete-stop.dto.ts` were DELETED — Express first-match-wins always routed plain `/complete` to the routes.service handler, so the orders one was dead; regulated capture fields now live only on `routes/dto/complete-stop.dto.ts`.)** `routes.service.createRun` populates `RouteRunStop.ageCheckRequired/identityCheckRequired` at dispatch (driver-UI hint only; enforcement re-derives); `reopenStop` clears the capture cols. Schema `TrackedCategory.requiresAgeCheck/requiresIdCheck` + `RouteRunStop.{ageVerified,identityVerified,identityType,identityVerifiedAt}`; migration `20260710010000_regulated_pod_age_id` (additive). Inline complete bodies promoted to `routes/dto/complete-stop.dto.ts`+`complete-with-payment.dto.ts` (which carry the regulated capture fields). **W7b surfaces (all wired):** buyer unlock tile (web `buyer/portal/[seller]/shop`, mobile `(customer)/(tabs)/catalog` — consume `hiddenCategories`); expiry bell via new read endpoints `GET /authorizations/expiring-soon` (operator, `ExpiringAuthorizationsController`; `AuthorizationsService.findExpiringSoon` guards a null-tenant SUPER_ADMIN to `[]`) + `GET /buyer/authorizations/expiring` (buyer, `findExpiringForCustomer`; 30/7/1 buckets), rendered in the existing web operator + buyer bells and a mobile buyer catalog bell; driver POD age/ID capture (mobile `route/stop/[stopId]/{index,payment}` + `podStore` capture fields + `StopCard` 🔒 badge). `AuthorizationsModule` now exports `AuthorizationsService` (buyer portal imports it).
- **Not yet wired** (later W-blocks): product-form category picker + scope selector. See `docs/design-package/PHASE-4-PLAN.md`.

### `recurring-invoices/`

- **controller** `recurring-invoices` — CRUD + `:id/run`. `PATCH :id` takes **`UpdateRecurringInvoiceDto`** (F13/REG-B92 — was an inline `Partial<CreateRecurringInvoiceDto>`); `DELETE :id` is **deactivate, never a row delete**; `POST :id/activate` is the resume path.
- **`dto/update-recurring-invoice.dto.ts`** (F13/REG-B92, new) — `UpdateRecurringInvoiceDto extends PartialType(CreateRecurringInvoiceDto)`. A mapped type written inline in the handler signature erases to `Object` in `design:paramtypes`, which the global ValidationPipe skips entirely, so the PATCH body was never validated **or** whitelisted. Deliberately carries **no `isActive`** (pause/resume are the two dedicated endpoints), so a `{isActive:true}` PATCH is now a 400 under `forbidNonWhitelisted`. Spec `dto/update-recurring-invoice.dto.spec.ts` (REG-B92 T29 + partial-body / nested-item / `ArrayMinSize(1)` pins).
- **service** — `create`, `findAll`, `findOne`, **`update`**, `deactivate`, `activate`, `runNow`, `generateDueRecurringInvoices` (cron), private `generateInvoiceFromTemplate` + `calcNextRunAt`. Side effects: RecurringInvoice(+Item)/Invoice writes on run. **Exports the run-outcome constants** `RUN_STATUS_SUCCESS`, `RUN_STATUS_FAILED`, `RUN_INTERRUPTED_ERROR`, `RUN_UNFINALIZED_ERROR` — the last is MIRRORED by web's `RUN_UNFINALIZED_PREFIX` (`apps/web/lib/api/invoices.ts`) through a `startsWith` check; **never reword one without the other**.
- **REG-B46 — `calcNextRunAt` MONTHLY.** The old branch called `d.setDate(1)` _before_ testing `d.getDate() > dom`, so the test was always `1 > dom` (false) and the month never advanced: every MONTHLY template froze at its original due date and the midnight cron re-selected it nightly. The branch now decides from `from` itself — `dayOfMonth` in the earliest month whose occurrence is STRICTLY after `from`'s calendar day, re-applied from `dom` each month and clamped to that month's length (dom 31 → Feb 28 → Mar 31, no drift; Dec → Jan of the next year). `dom` is clamped `1..31` first: the column is a nullable Int with no DB constraint, and an unclamped 0/negative made the advance loop non-terminating (the occurrence lands in month `m-1`), hanging the cron and with it the API process. WEEKLY/BIWEEKLY untouched.
- **REG-B46 — the claim advances from `max(dueDate, now)`.** `generateInvoiceFromTemplate` advances from the LATER of the stored due date and now, so a months-overdue template (the state B46 itself created) is claimed into the FUTURE in one step instead of minting one real invoice per night until it caught up. Missed cycles are deliberately not billed retroactively: the run is a single make-up invoice and the schedule resumes at the first occurrence after today.
- **REG-B106 — outcome recording, and the claim/rollback contract.** The cycle is CAS-claimed on `nextRunAt` (B9) and that same `updateMany` now stamps a PROVISIONAL outcome (`lastRunAt`, `lastRunStatus: FAILED`, `lastError: RUN_INTERRUPTED_ERROR`), so a crash between the claim and the create leaves an honest FAILED behind rather than a fresh `lastRunAt` that reads as success. SUCCESS is written LAST, after the invoice exists AND is linked. Three failure shapes, deliberately different: (1) **create throws** — nothing was billed, so the schedule is given back, but as a **compare-and-set `updateMany({where:{id, nextRunAt: <the ADVANCED value this run's own claim wrote>}})`**, never a plain `update({where:{id}})`; `count === 0` means a newer claimant (Run Now, or the next tick) owns the row and may already have billed the cycle, so it logs at warn and **writes nothing**, and the original error still rethrows. (2) **link/finalize throws after the invoice exists** — the cycle IS billed: `nextRunAt` is never restored and `lastError` is rewritten to `RUN_UNFINALIZED_ERROR` so operator surfaces suppress the retry hint; the error is not rethrown (the caller gets the invoice). (3) **lost claim** — log + return null. Specs: `recurring-invoices.schedule-outcome.spec.ts` (REG-B46 T1/T1b/T3/T5/T6/T7/T7b, REG-B106 T17/T17b/T17c/T18/T19, REG-B92 T31), `recurring-invoices.service.spec.ts` (cron-continues-past-a-failing-template pin + claim pins).
- **REG-B92 — `update(id, dto)`.** A scalar-only PATCH stays a single `recurringInvoice.update`; a body carrying `items` REPLACES the lines inside ONE `tenantTransaction` (`recurringInvoiceItem.deleteMany` → `recurringInvoice.update` with nested `create`), every nested row stamped with `tenantId` (nested creates bypass the `forTenant()` extension — the L-021 class). `items: null` clears `@IsOptional()` at the pipe and arrives typed-but-absent, so it is treated as absent rather than mapped over; `items: []` stays a 400.

### `sales-agents/` (flag.sales_agents — PR-C, engine ships DARK)

Agents bring customers in and earn a % on those customers' invoices. **Engine + API here; the
UI shipped in PR-D** — web `app/(dashboard)/sales-agents/` + `finance/commissions/` + the
customer-page agent box (see web.md) and a read-only mobile row (mobile.md). Everything is
gated by `flag.sales_agents`, granted ONLY by the `SALES_AGENTS` addon (no plan grants it) —
flag OFF ⇒ the engine returns before touching a commission table and every route 403s.

- **`commission-math.ts`** (pure, `pricing.spec.ts` style — no Nest, no mocks) — `COMMISSION_EPS = 0.005`, `commissionBase`, `collectionRatio`, `resolveRate`, `passesGrandfathering`, `accruedCommission`, `payableCommission`. **Money semantics (owner-locked):** base = `max(0, subtotal − discount − nsfFees)` — tax, shipping, and NSF penalty fees NEVER enter (NSF items identified by the server-written `NSF_FEE_DESCRIPTION_PREFIX` on the invoice item, exported here, written by `invoices.service` setCheckStatus); `collectionRatio` deliberately keeps NSF fees in its denominator (conservative under-release, commented in-file); a CREDIT_NOTE application reduces base by its **pre-tax share** (`creditApplied × goods / total`), not its face value. `ratio = cashCollected / (total − creditApplied)` with a full-payment snap at ε; credit rows are excluded from BOTH sides. Commission is computed ONCE per document, never per line.
- **`commission-engine.service.ts`** — `syncInvoiceCommission(invoiceId, db?)` (derive-from-current-state, idempotent: unchanged state writes nothing), `syncInvoiceCommissionSafe` (**the hook wrapper — catches everything, logs, never throws**; a commission bug must never block an invoice/payment write), `syncOrderInvoices(orderId, db?)`, `removeInvoiceCommission(invoiceId, db)` (409s when `claimedAmount > 0`), `recomputeCommissionRange({agentId?,customerId?}, fromDate)` (backdate sweep, own tx per invoice, audit-logged `commission.recompute`).
  - **Issued** = `status !== DRAFT && !== VOID` (`WRITTEN_OFF` counts — it froze, it didn't un-happen). **Cash math counts ONLY `InvoicePayment.status = PAID`** (the DRAFT-payment trap; VOID are dead) — deliberately diverges from `invoices.service.ts recomputeStatus`, which filters only `!== VOID`.
  - `basisDate = order.orderDate ?? invoice.issueDate`; attributed agent = the `AgentAssignment` covering it. **Rate precedence:** `Order.commissionRatePct` (`0` IS a value = exempt) → newest `CustomerCommissionRate` → newest `SalesAgentRate` → `NONE` (writes a zero row so a later backdated rate has something to fix).
  - **Ledger invariant:** `drift = payable − claimedAmount − Σadjustments`. Accrual rows are mutable until claimed; claims move ONLY via statements. `drift < −ε` ⇒ append exactly ONE `CommissionAdjustment` (MANUAL is never auto-emitted) — the formula self-limits, so a re-run emits none. **Kind is chosen by CAUSE, not by the forced-zero target:** agent no longer holds the customer ⇒ `REASSIGNMENT`; invoice no longer issued (void / revert-to-draft / reopen) ⇒ `CLAWBACK`; rate or rateSource moved **while the row still earns** ⇒ `RATE_CHANGE`; otherwise `CLAWBACK`. Positive drift is just unclaimed payable that the next statement sweeps. Convergence is why backdating, clawbacks, reassignment and order edits are all ONE code path.
  - **Row retention:** a row is deleted only when it stopped **earning** (agent lost the customer, or invoice un-issued) AND has no claims and no adjustments. A resolved agent at rate 0 — `rateSource NONE`, or an explicit `0` = exempt order override — **keeps its placeholder row** (that is the row the backdated-rate sweep later fixes), so a re-sync of it writes nothing. New rows are inserted after a `findFirst` re-check of the double-accrual lock, **never** by catching its P2002: `db` is usually the caller's invoice/payment tx and Postgres aborts the whole tx on a constraint violation, so swallowing it would only defer the failure to COMMIT — the one thing `…Safe` cannot save the caller from.
  - Lifecycle: `PAUSED` blocks **creation** only (existing rows keep releasing; pause DEFERS, never forfeits). `STOPPED_FOR_NEW` + `stopNewBusinessAt` grandfathers via `order.orderTemplate.createdAt` / `invoice.recurringInvoice.createdAt`. Soft-delete only.
- **`commission-reconciliation.service.ts`** — `@Cron("30 * * * *")` (the other hourly jobs fire at :00). Multi-tenant cron shape copied from `recurring-invoices.service.ts`: ACTIVE tenants → `tenantCtx.run(tenant.id, …)` → skip unless `hasFlag` → invoices `status != DRAFT` updated in the last **25h** → `syncInvoiceCommissionSafe`. The 25h/1h overlap is harmless (idempotent) and is what heals whatever the Safe wrapper swallowed.
- **`sales-agents.service.ts` / `.controller.ts`** (`@Controller("sales-agents")`) — agent CRUD, `PATCH :id/status` (sets/clears `stopNewBusinessAt`, audit-logged), soft-delete guards, rates (`POST :id/rates`, insert-only; `DELETE` future rows only), `POST :id/customer-rates`, assignments (`POST :id/assignments`, `/bulk` ≤500, `POST assignments/close`), `GET :id/accruals` (ledger + per-row drift), `POST :id/recompute`, and (PR-D, the ONE API addition that PR made) `GET assignments/current?customerId=` → `currentAssignment(customerId)`: the open `AgentAssignment` (`effectiveTo: null`, at most one per the partial unique index) + the customer's currently-effective `CustomerCommissionRate` (`effectiveFrom <= now`, `Number`-coerced) — declared as a two-segment route beside `assignments/close` so it cannot collide with `:id`, and the read behind web's agent box + the mobile parity row. Any **backdated** rate/assignment write calls `recomputeCommissionRange` and returns `{invoicesSynced}`. ⚠️ All three assignment paths (`/assignments`, `/bulk`, `assignments/close`) **400 when the new date is at or before the open row's own `effectiveFrom`** (`assertClosesAfterStart`): an inverted `[from, to)` covers no date at all, so the outgoing agent's whole history would be silently reattributed with nothing left to recover the real window from. Bulk fails the entire batch (one tx) rather than half-applying.
- **`commission-statements.service.ts` / `.controller.ts`** (`@Controller("commission-statements")`) — `POST /generate` (409 if a PENDING statement exists; Serializable tx; CLAIM lines = drift, sweeps unclaimed adjustments + prior negative residuals as CARRYFORWARD, each **exactly once** via the unique `adjustmentId` / `carriedFromStatementId` refs; number = max+1 scan, prefix `CST-YYYY-`, same pattern as `generateInvoiceNumber` — NOT `NumberingSequence`), `POST :id/approve` (re-validates live drift, 409 `"stale — regenerate"`), `POST :id/void` (PENDING only; deleting lines decrements `claimedAmount` and frees the swept refs), `POST :id/payouts` (APPROVED only; cap **re-derived from payout rows inside the tx** — the `recordSupplierPayment` landmine — and creates ONE linked `COMMISSIONS_AND_FEES` `Expense` (`status: PAID`, `date = paidAt`) so payouts reach P&L with **zero `bookkeeping.service.ts` changes**; negative statements block payouts).
- **Both controllers gated at CLASS level:** `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard, PlanFlagGuard)` + `@Roles(UserRole.OPERATOR)` + `@RequirePlanFlag("flag.sales_agents")` (`PlanFlagGuard` reads metadata with `getAllAndOverride([handler, class])`, so class level suffices).
- **`commissions.module.ts`** — imports `PrismaModule` + `EntitlementsModule`; exports ONLY `CommissionEngineService`. Consumer modules (`invoices`, `credit-notes`, `orders`) import it **one-way** — no `forwardRef`, no cycles. Registered flat in `app.module.ts`.
- **Hook sites (WP4 — every one is a one-liner `syncInvoiceCommissionSafe(id, tx)`):** `invoices.service.ts` `send`, `sendEmail`, `voidInvoiceInTx`, `revertInvoiceToDraft`, `reopenInvoice`, `writeOff` (the last three were **wrapped in a new `tenantTransaction`** for this), `recordPayment`, `recordDeliveryPaymentInTx` (**per invoice inside its loop, BY DESIGN**), `updatePayment`, `deletePayment`, `voidPayment`, `setCheckStatus` (**BOUNCED branch only**), `applyPriceAdjustment` (rides along un-tx'd — that method is pre-existingly non-atomic; the cron heals), `rebuildSiblingDrafts` (gated `nextStatus !== DRAFT` — the real order-edit resync site), `deleteInvoice` (→ `removeInvoiceCommission`). `credit-notes.service.ts`: `applyCreditInTx`, `restoreCreditFromPaymentInTx`. `orders.service.ts`: `deleteOrder`'s invoice cascade (**a real bypass — it hard-deletes invoices without going through `deleteInvoice`**; F07/B65 added the matching `ledger.reverseInvoiceEntries` call so the bypass no longer skips the regulated reversal too — any FURTHER invariant `deleteInvoice` gains must be mirrored here by hand) + `setCommissionRate` (`PATCH /orders/:id/commission-rate`). **Deliberate NON-sites** (DRAFT-only / no balance change): `create`, `update`, `unvoidInvoice`, `reconcileOrderDraftInvoice`, `createSplitInvoices`, `createPartialFromOrder`, `duplicate`, `estimates.service.ts convertToInvoice`.
- **DTO surface:** `CreateOrderDto.commissionRatePct` + `CreateSaleDto.commissionRatePct` (staff-gated by `parseCommissionRatePct`, exactly like `parseOrderDate` — `0` valid, CUSTOMER/DRIVER 403), `CreateCustomerDto.salesAgentId` (opens the first `AgentAssignment` inside the existing create tx; no entitlement check — inert while the flag is off).
- ⚠️ **Every TestingModule that builds `InvoicesService` / `CreditNotesService` / `OrdersService` needs a `{ provide: CommissionEngineService, useValue: {syncInvoiceCommissionSafe, syncOrderInvoices, removeInvoiceCommission} }` mock** or Nest DI fails at `beforeEach`: `invoices.service.spec`, `credit-notes.service.spec` (×3 blocks), `credit-notes.security.spec`, `orders.service.spec`, `orders-promo-bogo.spec`. Customers specs need nothing — `CustomersService` got no new dependency.
- **Schema** (migration `20260901000000_add_sales_agents_commissions`): `SalesAgent`, `SalesAgentRate`, `CustomerCommissionRate`, `AgentAssignment`, `CommissionAccrual`, `CommissionAdjustment`, `CommissionStatement`, `CommissionStatementLine`, `CommissionPayout` + 6 enums + `Order.commissionRatePct Decimal?(5,2)`. Money `Decimal(10,2)`, rates `Decimal(5,2)`. **`@@unique([tenantId, invoiceId, agentId])` on `CommissionAccrual` is the double-accrual lock.** Hand-appended raw SQL: partial unique `AgentAssignment_open_assignment_key ON ("tenantId","customerId") WHERE "effectiveTo" IS NULL` (one OPEN assignment per customer — Prisma DSL can't express it). `SalesAgent.userId` is a nullable seat for a future AGENT role — **nothing is built on it**.
- **Onboarding = backdating.** Enabling the addon creates NO retroactive accruals; bulk-assign customers, insert a backdated rate, and `recomputeCommissionRange` builds the history. There is no backfill script, by design.

### `bookkeeping/`

- **controller** `bookkeeping` — summary, dashboard, transactions (+/:id, payments, pdf), expense-categories CRUD, mileage-rates CRUD, expenses (+bulk, batch-status).
- **service** — `getSummary`, `getDashboard`, `getTransactions`, `getTransaction`, `recordPayment`, `recordExpense`/`recordBulkExpenses`, expense-category + mileage-rate CRUD. side effects: Transaction(+Item)/Expense/ExpenseCategory/MileageRate writes; PDF. **`getProfitAndLoss` (2026-07-31): COGS re-sourced from invoiced sales — same PAID/paidAt invoice set as revenue (basis-aligned; was SALE movements by createdAt = 0 in prod), each line costed at its issueDate via `common/invoiced-sales.ts`; cogs/grossProfit/opEx/netProfit now `roundMoney`ed. No tobacco exclusion (accounting carve-out).**
- **`extractExpenseItems(id)` (AI receipt extraction, metered 2026-08-28)** — downloads the expense's receipt, extracts line items via Anthropic (`RECEIPT_MODEL = "claude-opus-4-5-20251101"`, module-scope constant), REPLACES `ExpenseLineItem`s and re-totals the expense. Key via `platformConfig.resolveAnthropicKey(tenantKey)` (same chain as the scanners); records `AiUsageEvent` feature `ocr.expense_receipt` (tokens on success / `success:false` + rethrow on call failure). Spec block in `bookkeeping.service.spec.ts` (SDK jest-mocked; `expenseLineItem` grafted onto the prisma mock).
- **Cash basis = settled date when present (2026-08-07):** module-level `settledDateFilter(from, to)` (typed `Prisma.InvoicePaymentWhereInput`, the house pattern) returns `OR: [{settledAt: {gte,lte}}, {settledAt: null, paidAt: {gte,lte}}]` and is spread into the existing `where` so it ANDs with the status filter rather than widening it. Exactly TWO consumers: `getCashFlow` money-in and `getPaymentsReceivedReport` (whose returned row date is `settledAt ?? paidAt ?? createdAt`). Every legacy row has `settledAt = null`, so day-one output is identical and numbers only move as bank dates get recorded; a future-dated `settledAt` naturally drops out of the current period. **Deliberately NOT switched:** P&L revenue (`Invoice.paidAt`), AR aging (`dueDate` only), buyer statements, Time-to-Get-Paid, Refund History, Receivable Summary. Note `getFinanceDashboard`/`getMobileDashboard`/`getPeriodSummary` aggregate `invoicePayment` on `createdAt`, not `paidAt` — untouched by this change.
- **`bulkMarkPaid(BulkMarkPaidDto)` (PR-E, 2026-08-20)** — `POST /bookkeeping/bills/bulk-mark-paid`. Partition-and-report like `vendor-bills.bulkDelete`: never throws per item, returns `{paid, totalAmount, skipped:{id,billNumber,reason}[]}`. `ids` may mix VendorBill and Expense ids; the pre-loop `vendorBill.findMany` / `expense.findMany` selects are **identity only (`id`,`billNumber`)** — a bill-linked expense (landmine 2: `Expense.vendorBillId` has no Prisma relation) pays its BILL and then `updateExpense(status:"PAID")` for the expense's own stamp; an unlinked expense just flips status, no ledger row. `payBillFully(billId, dto, paidAt)` does ALL of the money work inside its own `tenantTransaction`: re-reads the bill with `include:{payments:true}`, totals `alreadyPaid` off the **BillPayment ledger** (as `recordPayment` does), skips VOID / `remaining ≤ 0.001`, writes one full-remaining `BillPayment`, then `newPaid >= totalOwed - 0.001 ? PAID : PARTIAL`. **Never pass a pre-loop snapshot's `totalPaid` in** — one request can reach the same bill twice (a bill id AND the expense linked to it) and a stale column double-pays it, desyncing the ledger from `totalPaid` and making every ledger-summing reader (`recordPayment`, `recordSupplierPayment`, `getSupplierStatement`) report a phantom supplier overpayment. Spec: `bulk-mark-paid.spec.ts` (fixtures carry `payments[]`, not `totalPaid`).

### `analytics/`

- **controller** `analytics` — overview, revenue, top products/customers, route/driver performance, inventory turnover/dead-stock/margin-alerts, dso, sales-by-category, gross-margin, aov, price/cost history, **demand/:productId**. `GET /analytics` returns a self-describing `endpoints[]` — ADD NEW ROUTES THERE or it goes stale. No DTO classes here by convention: raw `@Query` parsed inline (an empty `?range=` is treated as absent; a non-empty unrecognised one 400s rather than silently returning the wrong window under the right label).
- **service** — corresponding read aggregates. **(2026-07-31) Sales readers re-sourced to INVOICED SALES via `common/invoiced-sales.ts`** — `StockMovement type:"SALE"` has had NO writer since c5f579c2 removed the route-delivery `recordSale` call (and `TransactionItem` never had one), so movement-sourced readers were empty in prod. `getTopProducts(metric, limit, from?, to?)` now returns `{id,name,unitsSold,totalRevenue}[]` (both metrics per row, `metric` = sort key only, `dateRange` window — the shape web+mobile always expected); `getInventoryTurnover` sums line qty per product; `getGrossMarginTrend` estimates COGS as Σ line qty × point-in-time `avgCostAfter` snapshot at the invoice's issueDate (fallback STANDARD→standardCost→averageCost→0), one invoice fetch feeding revenue AND COGS; `getDeadStock` treats a product as dead only when NEITHER a movement NOR an invoiced sale happened since the cutoff (was movement-only ⇒ false positives; N+1 findFirst loop replaced with set queries). **B40 date-range wiring (2026-08-28):** `getDso(from?, to?)` + `getDeadStock(daysInactive, from?, to?)` now honor the analytics-page picker — DSO windows PAID invoices on `issueDate` via the private `issueDateFilter` (no bounds ⇒ no filter, so mobile's parameterless calls stay all-time; range selects which invoices count, the payment may land after `to`); for dead stock a range REPLACES the rolling `daysInactive` window (dead ⇔ no movement AND no invoiced sale inside `[from,to]`; stock levels stay CURRENT-state, `lastMovement`/`daysInactive` stay most-recent-ever). `getMarginAlerts` deliberately takes NO from/to (today's price vs today's cost — nothing to window); the web card carries a caption saying so instead of pretending to filter. `getCostHistory` includes COST_BASIS + returns `{unitCost, avgCostAfter, type}`. **Tobacco exclusion toggle** (`tobaccoExclusionActive()` = tobacco_dealer addon + SystemConfig `tobacco.excludeFromMainAnalytics`): excludes tobacco items from revenue/top-products/top-customers/sales-by-category/gross-margin/aov/turnover/dead-stock/margin-alerts — NOT bookkeeping/P&L/DSO/route-driver perf. side effects: read-only. Specs: `analytics.service.spec.ts`. **`getProductDemand(productId, range)` (2026-08) — per-product demand series for the product-detail chart.** Buckets via `analytics/demand-range.ts` (pure, UTC-only, `now` INJECTED so it is testable without fake timers; `Date.UTC` month math never `setMonth`, half-open `[from,to)`): 30d=30 day, 6m=26 Monday-anchored week, 1y=12 month, 5y=60 month. Source is **invoiced sales** (`Invoice.issueDate`, status notIn DRAFT/VOID/WRITTEN_OFF) — NOT `StockMovement type:"SALE"`, which is written only on the route-delivery path and is empty for tenants that invoice directly. ⚠️ **Queried THROUGH `Invoice`, never `invoiceItem.findMany`**: invoice lines are nested-created so they bypass the tenant extension's `data.tenantId` injection and imported history can carry `tenantId = null`, which `forTenant()`'s `where.tenantId` would silently drop — the majority of the history the 6m/1y/5y ranges exist to show. A spec asserts `invoiceItem.findMany` is never called. Revenue = Σ`subtotal` (NEVER qty×unitPrice — boxed-line overcharge); units = Σ`qty` rounded to 3dp via `roundQty` (now exported from `common/invoiced-sales.ts`), not `roundMoney`'s 2dp. Zero-filled buckets + product-level `hasAnySales`/`firstSaleAt`/`lastSaleAt` so the client can tell "never sold" from "zero this window". Tobacco exclusion deliberately NOT applied (same carve-out as price/cost history). Specs: `demand-range.spec.ts`, `analytics.controller.spec.ts`.

- **Route & driver performance ops metrics (2026-08-28, no migration):** `getRoutePerformance(from?, to?)` / `getDriverPerformance(from?, to?)` — from/to window on `RouteRun.scheduledDate` via private `runDateFilter` (no params ⇒ NO filter, preserving all-time behavior for mobile's parameterless calls; the web Operations tab always sent from/to and they were silently ignored until now). Both rows gained `onTimeRate`/`stopsPerHour`/`avgRunDurationMinutes` (nullable — null = "no measurable data in the window", never NaN/Infinity/0) via shared `accumulateRunMetrics`/`finalizeRunMetrics`. **On-time definition** (schema-verified: RouteRunStop has NO promised-ETA/time-window field): a completed stop is on-time when `completedAt` ≤ END of its run's `scheduledDate` UTC calendar day; denominator = completed stops only (never-completed stops are incomplete, which completionRate already captures — they are NOT "late"). **Duration/stops-per-hour** admit only runs with a positive `startedAt→completedAt` span; invalid runs are excluded from the stops-per-hour numerator AND denominator so they can't poison averages (their completed stops still count for on-time %). Specs: `analytics.service.spec.ts` "route & driver performance" — plus (2026-08-29) a `analytics.controller.spec.ts` "route/driver performance" block pinning that `getRoutes`/`getDrivers` forward from/to to BOTH service methods and forward absent params as `undefined` (all-time, the parameterless mobile call); the shipped from/to wiring had NO controller coverage until then, so the controller mock now stubs `getRoutePerformance`/`getDriverPerformance` alongside `getProductDemand`. Web: `analytics/page.tsx` OperationsTab +3 columns via `opsMetricColumns`; mobile: `lib/api/admin.ts` hooks accept `(from?, to?)` + `RunOpsMetrics` on both row types (no mobile screen renders them yet).
- **PR-B product sales history (2026-08-20, no migration):** `GET /analytics/product-sales/:productId?limit=` (OPERATOR; limit CLAMPED to 1-500, default 200 — never rejected) → `getProductSales` returns `{productId, lines[], summary}`: one row per invoiced line (date, invoice #, orderId|null, customer, qty + boxes/pieces/unitsPerBox, unitPrice, lineTotal, originalPrice, overridden) newest-first, plus `{count, buyers, totalQty, totalRevenue, minPrice, maxPrice, avgPrice}`. **`avgPrice` is REVENUE-WEIGHTED (Σsubtotal / Σqty), not a mean of unit prices** — guarded on `totalQty > 0`. Same tenancy trap as `getProductDemand`: queried THROUGH `Invoice` with a nested `items.where.productId`, NEVER `invoiceItem.findMany` (nested-created lines can carry `tenantId = null` and `forTenant()` would silently drop them); `REAL_INVOICE_STATUSES` so DRAFT/VOID/WRITTEN_OFF are not sales. `subtotal` is authoritative — never re-derived as qty × unitPrice (that over-charges boxed lines by `unitsPerBox`). Tobacco-exclusion deliberately NOT applied (per-product drill-down, same carve-out as price/cost history). A deleted customer renders "—". Specs: `analytics.service.spec` "getProductSales".
- **PR-B orders-by-product filter:** `ListOrdersDto.productId` → `findAll` AND-s `where.lineItems = { some: { productId } }` onto the existing customer/status/date filters (never replaces them); `OrderItem.productId` is already indexed, and equality can never match NULL so unlisted lines drop out for free. A CUSTOMER caller stays scoped to their own orders. Specs in `orders.service.spec` "findAll".

- **⚠️ NULL-safe regulated gate (2026-08-20 hotfix, money/visibility landmine):** Prisma's `notIn` NEVER matches NULL rows. The W7 license gate's filter `trackedCategoryId: { notIn: hidden }` therefore hid EVERY untracked product the moment a tenant had one `requiresLicense` category — on a live tenant, 1,757 of 1,823 products invisible to every unlicensed buyer ("the shop is empty"). Proven by a reversible e2e-tenant experiment (one gated category with zero tagged products: catalog 5 → 0 → 5). The gate is now `(trackedCategoryId IS NULL OR notIn hidden)` at ALL THREE sites: `products.service.findAll` (AND-pushed so it can't clobber a search OR — spec `products-tracked-filter.spec.ts`), `buyer-catalog.getCatalogCounts`, `buyer-dashboard.gateWhere`. **Never write a bare `notIn` against a nullable column** — two specs previously ASSERTED the broken shape and were inverted. `computeGate` (regulated-visibility.service) only gates `requiresLicense: true` categories.

### `suppliers/`

- **controller** `suppliers` — get/patch/deactivate/delete.
- **service** — `findAll`, `findOne`, `create`, `update`, `deactivate`, `delete`. side effects: Supplier writes; ContactPerson updates. `remove()` pre-delete guard counts VendorBill **+ PurchaseOrder + SupplierCredit** (all three FKs are non-nullable / `ON DELETE RESTRICT`) and 400s with a "Cannot delete: supplier has …" list; nullable `Expense.supplierId`/`StockMovement.supplierId` are cleared in the delete transaction instead.

### `notifications/` & `messages/`

- **notifications controller** `notifications` — register-token, delete token, test, status. `sendNotification`/`broadcast` via FCM/Expo push; DeviceToken writes.
- **messages controller** `messages` — driver↔operator run-chat. `messages.service.ts` create sets only `{runId,text,senderId,senderRole}`; `findByRun` reads `where:{channel:INTERNAL, ...runId?}` (P6-2: scoped to INTERNAL so engine messages don't leak into run-chat, incl. the no-runId path). **`messages.service.spec.ts` locks the create/read shape** (P6-1 regression guard).
- **messaging engine (P6-2, `src/messaging/`)** — the send engine on the F0 schema. `messaging.service.ts`: `sendMessage` (gates invoice-policy[G12]→consent→opt-out→contact→quiet-hours, dispatch via `MESSAGE_PROVIDER`, record `Message` on the customer's `MessageThread`, bump thread, meter WA/SMS via `MeterService.increment(MSGS)`; failed dispatch → not recorded/metered), `notify(eventKey,{customerId,senderId,vars})` (resolve enabled `NotificationRule` → render `MessageTemplate` → sendMessage per channel, fallback chain; meters WA/SMS ONCE), `upsertThread`, `listThreads`/`getThread`. **P6-5 additions:** `resolveSystemSenderId(tenantId)` — oldest ACTIVE `TENANT_ADMIN`→oldest ACTIVE `OPERATOR` (no `Tenant.ownerId`); null tenant or no match → `null`. `notifyEvent(eventKey, {customerId, senderId, vars?})` — trigger-facing wrapper around `notify()`; **never rejects** (fire-and-forget, catches+warns internally); `senderId:null` resolves via `resolveSystemSenderId(this.prisma.getTenantId())` and SKIPs (warn, no throw) if unresolvable; auto-fills `vars.customerName` from `Customer.businessName` unless caller supplied it; does NOT re-meter (delegates to `notify()`). `messaging.helpers.ts` pure gating (`renderTemplate`/`isQuietHours`/`isInvoicePolicyViolation`/`isMetered`/`requiresConsent`) **+ P6-5 formatters** `formatMoney(value)` (Intl USD, STORED values only — never recompute) and `formatDate(value)` ("July 14, 2026" style, `""` on null/invalid). `providers/message-provider.interface.ts` (`MessageProvider` + `MESSAGE_PROVIDER` token) + `providers/stub.provider.ts` (**StubProvider**, logs only, default-bound; P6-3/P6-4 swap real Meta-WA/Twilio here). Thin operator controller: `POST /messaging/threads/:customerId/messages`, `POST /messaging/notify`, `GET /messaging/threads[/:id]`. `messaging.service.spec.ts` (20 tests, untouched by P6-5) + new `messaging-notify.spec.ts` (formatMoney/formatDate/resolveSystemSenderId/notifyEvent). NO new schema; INTERNAL/EMAIL/PORTAL never metered. Inbox UI, webhooks, quiet-hours queue = later P6.
- **P6-5 transactional trigger wiring (no migration; PAYMENT_REMINDER deferred)** — 5 in-request/cron sites call `MessagingService.notifyEvent` AFTER their business write commits (never inside a `tenantTransaction`, whose mock lacks messaging models): `orders.service.ts` `changeStatus` (CONFIRMED/OUT_FOR_DELIVERY/DELIVERED → `ORDER_CONFIRMED`/`OUT_FOR_DELIVERY`/`DELIVERED`, `senderId: user.sub`); `invoices.service.ts` `send()`+`sendEmail()` (fires `INVOICE_SENT` only on the DRAFT→SENT flip, `senderId: null` — no user param, van-sale calls send userless); `orders/change-requests.service.ts` `create()` (fires `ORDER_CHANGED_AT_DOOR`, `senderId: user.sub`, `summarizeChange()` builds the change text); `routes.service.ts` `completeStop`+`completeWithPayment` (per-order `DELIVERED` after the tx, skips already CANCELLED/DELIVERED, `senderId: user.sub` — driver completion bypasses `changeStatus`); `authorizations/authorization-expiry.service.ts` (fires `LICENSE_EXPIRING` under the existing `expiryNotifiedAt`/`expiringSoonNotifiedBucket` idempotency markers, `senderId: null`, awaited in-loop). All money/date vars are `formatMoney`/`formatDate` over STORED totals (`order.total`/`invoice.total`), never re-derived. `OrdersModule`/`InvoicesModule`/`RoutesModule`/`AuthorizationsModule` each gained a `MessagingModule` import (acyclic: `MessagingModule`→`EntitlementsModule`→nothing). Every affected spec (`orders.service.spec.ts`, `invoices.service.spec.ts`, `change-requests.service.spec.ts`, `routes.service.spec.ts`, `authorization-expiry.service.spec.ts`) provides a `MessagingService` mock (`notify` + `notifyEvent` both `jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(...)`) or DI compile breaks.
- **P6-1 (F0) messaging thread schema (schema-only foundation, PR #163, no service yet):** `Message` additively gained `threadId String?` + `channel MessageChannel @default(INTERNAL)` (+`thread` relation, index) — run-chat rows stay `channel=INTERNAL`/`threadId=null`, unregressed. New tenant-scoped models: `MessageThread`, `MessageTemplate`, `NotificationRule`, `MessageOptOut`, `MessagingSettings` (per-tenant singleton, `tenantId @unique`; provider creds deferred to P6-3), `InboundTriage`; enums `MessageChannel`/`ThreadStatus`/`WaApprovalStatus`/`TriageStatus`/`NotificationEvent`. `Customer` gained `smsConsent`/`waConsent`/`consentUpdatedAt`. Migration `20260712000000_messaging_threads` (additive; applied to prod). Engine/inbox/settings/providers = later P6 increments.
- **P6-6 Settings config (`messaging-config.service.ts`, no migration):** `MessagingConfigService` — `EVENT_CHANNELS`/`DEFAULT_TEMPLATES`/`DEFAULT_ON` consts are the single source of truth for the event×channel matrix, shared with P6-5's triggers. `getMatrix()` lazy-seeds every missing `(eventKey,channel)` `NotificationRule`+`MessageTemplate` pair on first `GET /messaging/config` via `createMany({skipDuplicates:true})` (race-safe — `forTenant()` auto-injects `tenantId`); G12 pairs (`INVOICE_SENT`×WHATSAPP/SMS) are never seeded and render `locked:true` in the matrix. `setRuleEnabled(id,enabled)` throws `BadRequestException` on enabling a G12 pair (disable still allowed), 404 on unknown id. `updateTemplate(id,dto)` re-parses `{{vars}}` into `variables[]` via new `messaging.helpers.extractVariables` on every body edit. `preview(body,vars)` reuses the existing `renderTemplate`, pure. `getSettings`/`updateSettings` get/upsert the per-tenant `MessagingSettings` (quiet hours). Controller (`messaging.controller.ts`) +6 endpoints: `GET/PATCH /messaging/config,rules/:id,templates/:id`, `POST /messaging/templates/preview`, `GET/PATCH /messaging/settings` — reads `@Roles(OPERATOR)` (class default), writes method-level `@Roles(TENANT_ADMIN)` (overrides class per the settings/margin pattern; `RolesGuard.getAllAndOverride`, TENANT_ADMIN⊃OPERATOR). New DTOs in `messaging/dto/` (`update-rule`, `update-template`, `preview-template`, `update-messaging-settings.dto.ts`); global `ValidationPipe` (main.ts:160) validates, no `@UsePipes`. `messaging-config.service.spec.ts` (new) covers seeding/idempotency/G12-lock/meter-failure-tolerant/toggle/template/preview/settings; `messaging.service.spec.ts` untouched+green.

### `uploads/`

- **controller** `uploads` — `@Get *path` (HMAC-verified presigned fetch); multipart upload. `RENDERABLE_INLINE_MIMES` (inline vs `attachment` Content-Disposition) = `image/jpeg`, `image/png`, `image/webp`, **`application/pdf`** (added 2026-07-30, WP15 — customer PDFs now render in the web iframe viewer instead of downloading; safe because customer-document uploads are MIME-allowlisted to jpeg/png/webp/pdf, the browser PDF viewer is sandboxed, and nosniff + the signed-URL gate are unchanged). SVG/HTML stay excluded — deliberate XSS control, do not widen further.
- **service** — `uploadFile`, `generatePresignedUrl`, `verifyPresignedUrl`. side effects: file I/O to `/data/uploads` (Railway volume); HMAC via `STORAGE_URL_SIGNING_SECRET`.

### `system-config/` (settings)

- **controller** `system-config`/`settings` — config get/patch, anthropic, email (+test), route, invoice, delete financial-data.
- **service** — config CRUD, AI endpoint config, email test. side effects: SystemConfig/TenantConfig writes. **Secret-at-rest (F5-004):** `SystemConfigService` injects `EncryptionService` and encrypts a `SECRET_KEYS` allowlist (`email.smtpPassword`, `anthropic.apiKey`, `zoho.clientSecret`, `zoho.refreshToken`) on `set`, decrypts on `get`/`getAll`; a cipher-shape regex (`IV:TAG:B64`) gates decrypt so legacy plaintext rows pass through until re-saved (empty = cleared, never encrypted). Consumers (bookkeeping/route-analysis/vendor-bills anthropic key; settings email mask/`configured`; `getZohoConfig`) read via the service so decrypt is transparent; email SENDING uses TenantConfig SMTP, not these. Spec `system-config.service.spec.ts`.
- **`settings.controller.ts` `PATCH /settings` — `taxRate` range validation (2026-08-18).** The handler writes `String(dto[k])` for its `allowed` keys with no validation, which is how a QA tenant came to hold `"150"` despite the web form's `min(0).max(100)` (a direct API call bypasses the form, and the stored value is a PERCENT later divided by 100 — see `common/tax-rate.ts`). `taxRate` alone is now checked before the write: `Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 100`, else `BadRequestException("Tax rate must be between 0 and 100 (percent)")`. Every other key's handling is untouched. Spec `settings.controller.spec.ts`.
- **`DELETE /settings/financial-data` hardened (B126, 2026-08-30)** — was a cross-tenant wipe:
  `deleteMany({})` with no `where` on the finance models nukes EVERY tenant's data, and it was
  irreversible with no confirmation step. Now: (1) refuses when `prisma.getTenantId()` is null
  (`ForbiddenException`) rather than falling through to an unscoped delete; (2) requires
  `ClearFinancialDataDto.confirmTenantId` (new, `dto/clear-financial-data.dto.ts`) to equal the
  caller's own tenantId (`BadRequestException` otherwise); (3) parents delete on
  `where: { tenantId }`, children on a **relation filter over the parent's tenantId**
  (`invoicePayment`/`invoiceItem` via `{ invoice: { tenantId } }`, `billPayment`/`vendorBillItem`
  via `{ vendorBill: … }`, `purchaseOrderItem` via `{ po: … }`, `orderCreditNote` via
  `{ creditNote: … }`) — child `tenantId` is nullable and nested-created rows carry
  `tenantId: null`, so scoping a child by its OWN `tenantId` skips them, and that is fatal rather
  than untidy: `InvoicePayment.invoiceId`, `BillPayment.vendorBillId`, `OrderCreditNote.creditNoteId`
  are **ON DELETE RESTRICT**, so one surviving NULL-tenant child aborts its parent's delete (P2003)
  and rolls the whole wipe back. A relation filter is also parameter-free — no id list in memory,
  no 32767-bind-variable ceiling on the biggest tenants. (4) ⚠️ **Runs on `prisma.$transaction`,
  NOT `tenantTransaction`** — see the proxy lesson below; it re-scopes the child filters and
  reintroduces exactly the bug above. Deletion order respects FKs (children before parents).
  ⚠️ KNOWN LIMITATION: `CommissionAccrual.invoiceId` is also RESTRICT and is NOT cleared (that
  would cascade into commission statements/payouts, out of scope), so a tenant holding commission
  accruals still fails this wipe on that FK. (5) `@Roles(TENANT_ADMIN)` on the handler overrides the
  class-level `@Roles(OPERATOR)`. ⚠️ Prove a role gate by RUNNING `RolesGuard`, never by reading the
  `@Roles` metadata: a containment check passes on any SUPERSET, so it green-lights a decorator that
  re-admits `OPERATOR` alongside `TENANT_ADMIN` — and `RolesGuard` grants on
  `requiredRoles.some(...)`, i.e. exactly the role the gate exists to exclude.
  Spec `settings.controller.clear-financial.spec.ts`.
- ⚠️ **Durable lesson, BOTH directions — `prisma.tenantTransaction` is a pass-through when
  `tenantId` is null and an INJECTOR when it is not.** (a) With no tenant it hands back the raw
  client (`prisma.service.ts` — `if (!tenantId) return fn(rawTx)`), same as `forTenant()`; that is
  a fall-through, not a guard, so a destructive bulk handler must refuse a null tenant itself.
  (b) With a tenant it hands back `_wrapTxWithTenant`, whose `SCOPED_METHODS` include `deleteMany`
  and which rewrites EVERY call as `where: { ...args.where, tenantId }`. That injection is
  invisible at the call site and silently re-scopes a deliberate parent-scoped filter back onto the
  row's own `tenantId`. Since `tenantId` is `String?` (nullable) on finance/child models and
  nested-created child rows carry `tenantId: null`, a handler that must reach those rows CANNOT use
  `tenantTransaction` — use `prisma.$transaction` with explicit filters (plus a null-tenant refusal
  to make that safe), and match children through a relation filter on the parent. Mocks hide this:
  both `createMockPrisma` and hand-rolled fakes usually implement `tenantTransaction` as
  `(fn) => fn(models)` with no injection, so a spec that must observe it should call the real
  `_wrapTxWithTenant` (as `settings.controller.clear-financial.spec.ts` does).
- **Remittance / how-to-pay config (P5-14, no migration)** — `REMITTANCE_FIELDS`/`RemittanceField`/`RemittanceConfig` exported from `system-config.service.ts`. Unlike margin config (per-key strings), remittance is stored as **ONE JSON document** under a single key `SystemConfigService.REMITTANCE_KEY = "remittance.config"` (tenant-scoped via existing `get`/`set`). `getRemittanceConfig()` parses+filters to known non-empty string fields (`{}` on missing/corrupt JSON, never throws); `setRemittanceConfig(dto)` merges over the current doc — `undefined` field = untouched, `""` = cleared. **Deliberately NOT in `SECRET_KEYS`** — it's the seller's own remit-to info, buyer-visible by design (same data printed on an invoice). `dto/remittance-config.dto.ts` — `RemittanceConfigDto`, all 10 fields `@IsOptional @IsString @MaxLength` (200 short fields, 2000 instruction/notes fields); validated by the global `ValidationPipe` (no per-method `@UsePipes`). `settings.controller.ts`: `GET /settings/remittance` (any OPERATOR, class-level `@Roles(OPERATOR)`), `PATCH /settings/remittance` (`@Roles(TENANT_ADMIN)`) — returns the config after merge. Spec: `system-config.service.spec.ts` remittance block.

### `import/`

- **controller** `import` — contacts, invoices, payments, expenses (+ batches, repair-inventory, batch delete), products, inventory, expense-suppliers, contacts mark-supplier-only / orphans / adopt-orphans.
- **service** — bulk importers + batch management. side effects: bulk Customer/Invoice/Payment/Expense/Product/Supplier/StockMovement writes; rollback on validation error; audit trail.
- **Migration & Batch Import — Phase 1 (numbering continuity, migration-import-spec §1):** `numbering.controller` (`GET/PUT /import/numbering`, `@Roles(OPERATOR)`) + **`NumberingService`** (exported). Owns per-tenant `NumberingSequence` (docType `INVOICE|ESTIMATE|CREDIT_NOTE|PAYMENT|RETURN|ORDER` — RETURN/ORDER added by F01 for G6, DEFAULTS pinned to the ad-hoc minters' series `RET-` pad 4 / `ORD-` pad 5; prefix/nextNumber/padding/**year** with 0 = the year-agnostic series every pre-F01 row means; `@@unique([tenantId,docType,year])` — the compound-unique input renamed `tenantId_docType`→`tenantId_docType_year`, all 5 call sites updated in F01; plain `tenantId` scalar, no FK). Methods: `getSettings`/`updateSettings` (upsert), `seedFromSource` ("INV-08841"→next "INV-08842"), `parseDocumentNumber`, `format`, `peekNext`, **`reserveNext(docType, exists?)`** — atomic-increment fast path; collision-guarded path skips past existing numbers and **throws** at the 10k cap (never returns an unverified number), `advanced` flag for the "numbering advanced past an imported invoice" notice. **DEFERRED cross-module:** wiring `reserveNext` into live invoice/order minting (off-limits modules) — run it at SERIALIZABLE / `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`; imported docs keep ORIGINAL numbers and must NOT call it. Migration `20260708000000_import_numbering_sequences` (additive; `*.sql` is gitignored → `git add -f`). Web: `apps/web/lib/api/numbering.ts` + Document-numbering card on `settings/import/page.tsx`. Spec `numbering.service.spec.ts`.
- **Migration & Batch Import — Phases 2-5 (migrations `20260708010000`–`040000`, all additive):**
  - **P2 idempotency substrate:** `ExternalRefService` (`ImportExternalRef` side-table upsert by (source,externalId)→entityId, keeps off-limits finance models untouched), `ProductAliasService` (tenant-scoped `ProductAlias`, `""`=any-supplier sentinel, self-healing resolve drops dangling product pointers; fixes the old global `ProductMapping` cross-tenant bug), `DuplicateMatchService.findInvoiceDuplicate` (secondary match: normalized number + total±cent + issueDate±1d, read-only over Invoice). `AliasController` = `GET/POST/DELETE /import/aliases`. All exported.
  - **P3 variant resolution:** `Product.detailsIncomplete` col + `VariantResolutionService` (`createVariant` inherits parent family defaults via `ProductsService.create`; `createBrandNew` sells at cost×1.3, flags detailsIncomplete; `matchExisting` learns an alias; `listIncomplete`/`completeSetup`). `ResolutionController` = `/import/resolution/{variant,brand-new,match,incomplete,:id/complete}`. Web Finish-setup card on `settings/import`. (imports `ProductsModule`.)
  - **P4 migration staging + 24h undo:** `MigrationService` (`MigrationJob`+`MigrationStagingRecord`; `createJob`→`stageRecords` [dedup flags via ExternalRef+DupMatch]→`confirmJob` [commits PRODUCT/SUPPLIER via committers, records ExternalRef, undoDeadline=+24h]→`undoJob` [**atomic tenantTransaction**; deletes created rows + drops ExternalRefs; an in-use row rolls back with ConflictException]). CUSTOMER/INVOICE/PAYMENT committers deferred (reuse CSV importers). `SourceConnectorRegistry` = OAuth stub (CSV/paper direct; Zoho/QB throw upload-CSV). `MigrationController` = `/import/migration` (+ `:id/{stage,confirm,undo}`). Web migration hub + import history + products-CSV stage on `settings/migration`. ⚠️ **Money cells here are still B98-broken — filed as B205, deliberately NOT fixed by F17**: `optNum` does `Number(v)`, so a `"1,234.56"` price is NaN and silently dropped, and the web `parseProductsCsv` that feeds it naive-splits on `,` (a quoted money cell shifts every later column). F17 wrote and then REVERTED a fix: routing `pricePerUnit` through a lenient parser turned a present-but-unparseable price from a loud `products.create` throw into a silent $0.00 commit, and `parseFloat` leniency admitted `-$5`/`12abc`— and this committer calls`ProductsService.create()` in-process, so the class-validator DTO never runs to reject either. B205 needs its own batch (the parser half is web-side, so it needs an e2e — there is no web unit runner).
  - **Batch review learning loop (2026-07-18):** `updateItemLines` now upserts a product mapping when an operator pick sets a line's `productId` (via the VendorBillsService mapping method; guarded on non-empty supplier + extractedName) — batch-review corrections finally teach the scanner. `ScanLine` += additive `sku?`/`candidates?` (former low-confidence lines arrive `matchedProductId:null` → still NEEDS_REVIEW via the unmatched count, now shown as unmatched-with-suggestions instead of silently pre-picked).
  - **P5 batch queue:** `BatchImportService` (`ImportBatch`+`ImportQueueItem`; `scanAndRecord` calls `vendorBills.scanInvoice` → classify CLEAN/NEEDS_REVIEW/DUPLICATE/FAILED [unmatched via line.**matchedProductId**; CLEAN needs all matched lines high-confidence; dup via DupMatch on invoiceDate]; `postBatch` maps scan lines matchedProductId→productId then `vendorBills.create`+`receive` per clean item [stock+cost]; `resolveItem`). `BatchController` = `/import/batch` (+ `:id/{scan,post}`, `items/:id/resolve`). Web queue on `settings/batch-import`. **DEFERRED:** background worker (Bull), scan-meter cap (billing). Specs: `{external-ref,product-alias,duplicate-match,variant-resolution,migration,batch-import}.service.spec.ts` (51 import tests).
  - **Duplicate matcher v2 + post recovery (2026-08-07):** `findVendorBillDuplicate` is now LAYERED and returns an enriched `VendorBillDuplicateMatch` (`{id, billNumber, status, totalOwed, billDate, receivedDate, supplierId, itemCount, matchedBy:"number"|"fuzzy", totalMatches}`): (1) exact on the new `VendorBill.supplierInvoiceNumber` column, `status != VOID`, narrowed by supplier when known — without a supplier it additionally requires total ±0.005 so a number shared across suppliers can't collide; (2) the legacy `notes`-regex fallback (billDate ±1d + total ±0.005) for pre-backfill and old-mobile rows, also VOID-excluded; (3) numberless fuzzy ONLY when supplier+date+total are all present — deliberately tighter than the old first-candidate-wins. **Line items are never compared** (operators edit lines during review, so item equality yields false negatives); document identity is supplier + number + total agreement, surfaced for the operator to judge. An exact-NUMBER lookup must NOT hard-filter on supplier — it matches `{OR:[{supplierId},{supplierId:null}]}` via `supplierScope()` and ranks with `pickNumberMatch()` (caller's supplier wins, supplier-less row is the fallback), because batch import posts `supplierId: item.supplierMatchId ?? undefined` and mobile only links a supplier on exact case-insensitive name equality, so NULL-supplier bills are routine and were previously unreachable on an exact match. Bills owned by a DIFFERENT supplier stay excluded; the notes layer gets the same relaxation (`includeUnassignedSupplier`), the numberless fuzzy layer stays strictly supplier-scoped. Layer-1 `take` is 20 so a run of null-supplier rows cannot push the exact-supplier row out of the page before ranking. Lives in its own **`duplicate-match.module.ts`** (imports PrismaModule, exports the service) so `VendorBillsModule` can consume it without importing `ImportModule` — that would be a cycle. `postBatch` passes `supplierInvoiceNumber` and CATCHES `DUPLICATE_VENDOR_BILL` to recover: it attaches the queue item to the existing bill (receiving it when DRAFT, swallowing the already-received conflict) and marks POSTED + `duplicateOfInvoiceId` — closing the create-succeeded-but-POSTED-update-never-ran window that used to double-create on retry. **Adoption requires `matchedBy === "number"`** (a payload without it is treated as non-identity): adopting a fuzzy match would mark the item POSTED against a possibly-different document and silently discard its own reviewed lines, or receive the adopted bill's lines instead of them — so a fuzzy hit leaves the item `DUPLICATE` + `errorMessage` for a human. Return shape is `{posted, adopted, duplicates, batchStatus}`; `posted` counts only bills this run actually created.
  - **P5 batch-queue defect fixes (2026-07-11, fix/batch-import-queue):** `classifyAndPersist` now resolves `extracted.supplier` to a real `Supplier` via the scored `matchSupplier` (since 2026-08-18 the shared `import/supplier-match.ts`, spec'd in `supplier-match.spec.ts` and also used by `vendor-bills.service.ts`; exact → unique startsWith → unique substring; ambiguous/none → `supplierMatchId: null` + routes to NEEDS_REVIEW) and persists it to `ImportQueueItem.supplierMatchId` (was declared on the model but never written — every posted bill had no supplier). Duplicate check switched from `DuplicateMatchService.findInvoiceDuplicate` (wrong table — customer `Invoice`) to new **`findVendorBillDuplicate`** (same shape, over `VendorBill`, narrowed by the resolved supplier; matches a supplier invoice # embedded in `notes` via new exported `formatSupplierInvoiceNote`/`extractSupplierInvoiceNumber` helpers in `duplicate-match.service.ts`). `postBatch` now uses the extracted `invoiceDate` for `billDate` (was always `new Date()`) and writes notes via `formatSupplierInvoiceNote`. **Review gate**: `resolveItem` no longer blindly flips to CLEAN — it now throws unless every line is either matched (`matchedProductId`) or explicitly `reviewed:true` (set only via the new **`updateItemLines`** — `PATCH /import/batch/items/:itemId`, body `{supplierId?, lines?: [{index, productId?, keepCustom?}]}` — the actual line-remap endpoint) AND, if a supplier was detected, it's linked. New `deriveStatus`/`annotateItem` private helpers centralize the CLEAN/NEEDS_REVIEW decision (`unreviewedLines`/`supplierUnresolved` computed fields added to `getBatch`'s item response, not persisted). New `listBatches()` → `GET /import/batch` (list, most-active-first) backs the web page's refresh-persistence (localStorage `batchId` + list fallback). Web: new `apps/web/components/BatchItemReviewModal.tsx` (SupplierSelect + per-line SearchableProductPicker/"Keep custom") is the only path that can clear `unreviewedLines`/`supplierUnresolved`; `batch-import/page.tsx` gained the drag-and-drop handlers its empty-state copy always claimed to have, plus a "Start new batch" affordance. `duplicate-match.service.ts`'s class doc now states `findInvoiceDuplicate` (customer Invoice) and `findVendorBillDuplicate` (VendorBill) must never be cross-checked. Specs extended in `batch-import.service.spec.ts` (21 tests, incl. supplier resolution/ambiguity, vendor-bill dedup, date/notes, line-review gating).

  - **F17 import robustness (B98/B99/B112, 2026-08-31):** new pure module **`parse-import-number.ts`** —
    `parseImportNumber(raw)` (strips `$`/commas/spaces, **returns NaN for absent/non-numeric**) and
    `parseImportMoney(raw)` (= that + `roundMoney`, NaN passes through). Every monetary/quantity column read
    in `import.service.ts` goes through them (invoice total/subtotal/discount/shipping, Balance Due, line
    qty/unitPrice/Item Total, payment amount, expense amount, product rate, inventory closingStock) — bare
    `parseFloat("1,234.56")` is **1**, so comma money silently imported as $1.00 across four importers.
    ⚠️ The NaN return is **load-bearing**: `importInvoices` passes `|| "NaN"` as the Balance-Due sentinel and
    branches on `isNaN` to mean "no balance info — trust the Zoho status label"; a helper defaulting to 0
    would flip those invoices to PAID. The lat/lng `parseFloat`s (~:263-278) are coordinates, deliberately
    untouched. ⚠️ Use `typeof raw !== "string"`, never `String(raw)` — `@typescript-eslint/no-base-to-string`
    is an **error** and fails `npm run verify`. `parseCsv` gained **`bom: true`** (Excel's "CSV UTF-8" prepends
    EF BB BF, which glues to the first header key and silently blanked column one for every row).
    **`importPayments` fallback dedupe:** rows without an `InvoicePayment ID` are compared against a
    **per-invoice snapshot of payments taken BEFORE this run's inserts** (cent-equal amount, same method,
    `createdAt` ±1 day) — so a re-upload creates nothing while two identical legit rows in ONE file both
    import. The match is **count-aware** (a matched snapshot entry is consumed, so one history row can absorb
    at most one incoming row — otherwise a partially-failed first upload loses the second real payment).
    The snapshot **excludes `reference === "zoho-import"` placeholders and VOID rows**: `importInvoices` mints
    that synthetic placeholder for PAID/PARTIAL invoices and it is deleted just before every real create, so
    counting it would dedupe the first real payment away. Result shape is now
    `{ imported, skipped, duplicates, errors }` (surfaced on `settings/import`). The post-import tenant-wide
    status recalc sums **`sumConfirmed`** (`invoices/payment-predicates.ts`, F03's confirmed-only rule) at a
    `total - 0.001` threshold — it previously summed `status !== "VOID"` at `total - 0.01`, which counted
    unconfirmed DRAFT payments (B11's class) and could silently undo a `repair-f17` repair on the next import.
    Repair lane **`scripts/repair-f17.mjs`** (+ `apps/api/src/scripts/repair-f17.spec.ts`) proposes VOIDing the
    later twin of an exact-signature duplicate pair and recomputing that invoice's status; clusters >2 are
    report-only. ⚠️ `InvoicePayment` has **no `updatedAt` column** — a repair UPDATE that sets one fails.
    Specs: `import-robustness.spec.ts` (REG-B98/B99/B112), e2e `25-migration-hub.spec.ts` (REG-B08) and
    `26-import-duplicates.spec.ts`, both wired as playwright projects.

### `stripe-connect/` + `payment-requests/` (buyer-initiated payments — HARDENED #399/#403/#404, 2026-08-22)

Buyers paying their SELLER — orthogonal to `billing/` (tenants paying RouteFlow). **Stripe
Connect, Standard accounts, DIRECT charges, no application fee.** TEST-mode keys + endpoint are
LIVE on prod (`STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` / `STRIPE_CONNECT_CLIENT_ID` / `STRIPE_CONNECT_WEBHOOK_SECRET`);
demo tenant connected; settlement proven end to end ($4,998.44 → PAID to the cent). Going LIVE
still needs live keys + a live-mode endpoint with the same 9-event subscription.

- **PROVIDER PORTS (only adapters speak Stripe).** `payment-requests/provider/`
  (`PAYMENT_PROVIDER` → `stripe-payment-provider.ts`: hosted checkout, expire-with-re-read,
  `verifyEvent`, cents conversion in ONE place, non-USD throws, `isConfigured` drives the
  webhook 503) and `stripe-connect/provider/` (`CONNECT_OAUTH_PROVIDER`: exchangeCode /
  retrieveAccount / deauthorize). Domain services import NEITHER the SDK nor snake_case
  fields; swapping processors = new adapter, not domain surgery.
- **`stripe-connect.service.ts`** — `buildAuthorizeUrl` / `completeOAuth`: OAuth `state` =
  signed 15-min tenant-bound JWT + **single-use `jti` persisted on the row (`pendingJti`,
  consumed atomically — replay ⇒ state_replayed)**; the public callback redirects with
  CURATED error codes only, never exception text. `chargeableAccount(tenantId)` stays the
  read-side gate (null, never throws). **`applyAccountUpdate(accountRef)` (#403):
  account.updated is a TRIGGER, never a data source — it retrieves the account's CURRENT
  state via the port and persists that.** STANDING WEBHOOK RULE (incident 2026-08-22: a stale
  Restricted-era retry, replayed once #400 fixed rawBody, disabled a live account via the OLD
  handler): an event says SOMETHING changed, never WHAT it is now; ledger-ordering guards
  have a cold-start hole. `assertEventAccount(tenantId, accountRef)` = the anti-spoof check
  before every metadata-resolved webhook write. `markDeauthorized` severs all rows.
- **`payment-requests.service.ts`** — `buildOldestFirstAllocation` policy unchanged
  (account-level, issueDate asc + invoiceNumber tie-break, per-invoice cap;
  `BuyerPaymentRequest.invoiceId` = provenance only). **Card lifecycle: PENDING → SETTLING →
  SETTLED (#399), NON-REOPENING.** `settleByPaymentIntent` is the ONLY money entry point:
  claim (tenantId always in the where) → `recordStandalonePayment` (with
  `assertAllocationsWithinBalance: true`; allocations recomputed at settle time) → flip
  storing `providerPaymentId`. A crash between record and flip is safe: redelivery finds
  SETTLING + a matching `InvoicePayment` by reference and completes WITHOUT re-recording
  (spec-pinned: exactly one record across redelivery). Money arriving for a
  CANCELLED/FAILED/EXPIRED/REJECTED row is RECORDED anyway + CRITICAL log + `outcome` note —
  never discarded. Amount: the provider-reported value wins on a ≥$0.01 mismatch (logged in
  notes). Method: us_bank_account → ACH (`settledAt` = event time), else CREDIT_CARD
  (`settledAt` null — cash basis falls to paidAt). **`cancelOwn` NEVER settles** (no second
  money path): it always attempts expire via the port — including when the account is not
  chargeable; expire outcome "already_completed" ⇒ cancel REFUSED, the row stays for the
  webhook. `expireBySession` (checkout.session.expired) flips PENDING → EXPIRED; EXPIRED sits
  outside the open-request slot. Open-request rule is DB-enforced: partial unique index on
  (tenantId, customerId) WHERE status IN (PENDING, SETTLING); the create catches the unique
  violation into the same BadRequest. `status` query param validated against the enum;
  `fromInvoiceId` validated tenant+customer before the FK write.
  **`providerPaymentId` is THE settled-payment column** (serialized on the operator list
  since #404); `stripePaymentIntentId` is DEPRECATED — legacy rows backfilled by
  `20260828000000_unify_provider_payment_id`, nothing reads or writes it, drop pending.
- **`BuyerPaymentRequest` stays a SEPARATE table** (never a PaymentStatus member) — a pending
  declaration must not move invoice status. `InvoicePayment` rows are written ONLY on
  operator approval (cash) or the signature-verified PI webhook (card), always via
  `recordStandalonePayment` (numbering, grouping, recompute stay in the one path).
- **`connect-webhook.controller.ts`** — `POST /billing/webhook/connect`, its OWN signing
  secret, `@SkipThrottle`. **Settlement fires ONLY on `payment_intent.succeeded`** (Checkout
  sessions are created with `payment_intent_data.metadata = { tenantId,
buyerPaymentRequestId }` so the PI resolves the request); `checkout.session.completed` is
  display-only. **Insert-first `StripeConnectEvent` ledger** (id = the Stripe event id):
  duplicate with a completed outcome ⇒ no-op ack; duplicate whose earlier attempt has no/error
  outcome ⇒ RE-ROUTED (that is the redelivery our own 500 asked for). A settlement failure
  returns 500 for redelivery but the row STAYS SETTLING — never reopened. charge.refunded /
  charge.dispute.created ⇒ audit + operators-room emit (manual void by owner decision);
  account.application.deauthorized ⇒ sever + notify; no subscribed event type is silently
  dropped. `isConfigured` false ⇒ 503 so a pre-configuration delivery is redelivered, never
  ACKed into the void. **⚠️ NEVER `app.use(json())` in main.ts — it destroys `rawBody` and
  with it EVERY Stripe signature check (#400); the body limit goes through Nest's parser
  options instead.**
- **Wiring gotcha unchanged:** `BuyerPaymentsController` lives in `payment-requests/` but is
  registered by `BuyerModule` (rides its buyer guards/interceptor, which are not exported).
- Migrations (all applied to prod): `20260823000000_stripe_connect_buyer_payments` (tables),
  `20260826000000_stripe_connect_event_ledger` (ledger, SETTLING/SETTLED, `pendingJti`,
  partial unique open-request index), `20260828000000_unify_provider_payment_id` (data-only
  backfill).
- **Specs:** `payment-requests.service.spec.ts` (settle crash-path = record exactly once,
  cancel-refused-on-paid, expire flow, amount mismatch, ACH mapping, anomalous-row record),
  `connect-webhook.controller.spec.ts` (ledger dedupe/re-route, 500-on-failure,
  503-unconfigured, dispute attribution), `stripe-connect.service.spec.ts` (jti single-use,
  retrieve-truth heals, anti-spoof), `stripe-payment-provider.spec.ts` +
  `stripe-connect-oauth.provider.spec.ts` (cents round-trip, non-USD throw, expire-outcome
  mapping, event normalization).

### `audit/`

- **module** — global, no controller. `AuditService.log(userId, action, resource, resourceId, before?, after?, changes?)` → AuditLog writes. Injected by other modules.
- **`impersonatedBy` stamped onto every audit row (B165, F14 2026-09-02)** — `AuditLog.impersonatedBy`
  (migration `20260908000000_campaign_schema_foundation`, F01) has been live in the schema since
  F01 but nothing wrote it until now: `CreateAuditLogDto` gained `impersonatedBy?: string | null`,
  `AuditService.log`'s `create({data:{…}})` writes `dto.impersonatedBy ?? null` (unchanged
  try/catch — an audit write still must never fail the request), and `AuditInterceptor` (which
  runs AFTER route guards, so `req.user` is the VERIFIED `JwtStrategy` output) passes
  `user?.impersonatedBy ?? null` into the `log()` call. Net effect: a write made while impersonating
  now names the acting super-admin in the durable trail instead of only the tenant-admin `userId`
  it always recorded — the entire point of impersonation being auditable. R17 deploy precondition:
  the F01 column must be confirmed live in prod (`prisma migrate status`) BEFORE this deploys, or
  `AuditService`'s swallowing try/catch has silently been dropping EVERY audit write already.
  Specs: `audit/audit.interceptor.security.spec.ts` (impersonated + plain-user cases),
  `audit/audit.service.spec.ts` (new).
- **`ImpersonationGuard` now actually observes the claim (B165, F14 2026-09-02)** — see
  `auth/guards/impersonation.guard.ts` below (listed here too since its whole job is feeding this
  module). It is registered as an `APP_GUARD` (`app.module.ts`), and Nest runs global guards
  BEFORE route-level guards while `JwtAuthGuard` is route-level only — so `request.user` was
  ALWAYS undefined at this guard, and its original `if (!user?.impersonatedBy) return true`
  silently no-op'd on every single request since it shipped: the log line it exists to write had
  never once fired. It now decodes the bearer token's payload segment BY HAND (no signature
  verification — acceptable for a log-only side effect, since `JwtAuthGuard` still verifies the
  token downstream; same approach `TenantStatusGuard` uses) when `request.user` carries no claim,
  and prefers `request.user` when a future guard-ordering change populates it first. Logs
  non-GET/HEAD/OPTIONS methods only, path with the query string stripped. Spec:
  `auth/guards/impersonation.guard.spec.ts` (new).

### `buyer/` (multi-tenant customer identity)

- **buyer-auth controller** `buyer/auth` — register, login, refresh, logout, delete account, change-password (`BuyerChangePasswordDto`), profile (`BuyerUpdateAccountDto` — whitelisted, closes the BuyerAccount mass-assignment hole), sessions, **verify-email** (public, `BuyerVerifyEmailDto`, throttled 5/15min) + **resend-verification** (buyer JWT, throttled 5/15min). Login enforces the account lockout (see service).
- **registration email verification (2026-08-20, migration `20260823000000_add_buyer_email_verification`)** — closes the #378 residual exposure. `register` still issues a session immediately but fire-and-forgets `issueVerificationEmail` (hashed single-use token, model **`BuyerEmailVerificationToken`** mirroring the reset-token model, 24h TTL, link `${urls.web}/buyer/verify-email?token=`); `verifyEmail(token)` consumes it + flips `emailVerified` in one tx; `resendVerification` is awaited and honest via `send()`'s `{delivered}` (`sent:false` when transport fails; `alreadyVerified` no-op). The migration also GRANDFATHERED all pre-existing accounts to `emailVerified:true` (flag was write-only before — a naive gate would have pushed every existing password buyer into seller review). `google-oauth.service.handleBuyerPortalAuth` sets `emailVerified:true` on auto-create AND on any sign-in where Google attested the account's own mailbox (`buyer.email === profile.email` — NOT when found via googleId with a different email). Web page `app/buyer/verify-email` requires an explicit button click (mail-scanner JS prefetch must not verify an account the mailbox owner never created). Specs: `buyer/buyer-email-verification.spec.ts`, `auth/google-oauth.buyer-autocreate.spec.ts` (attestation cases). **Squat neutralization (2026-08-22, F1 HIGH fix):** when the Google-attested account carries an UNPROVEN password (`passwordSet && !emailVerified` — anyone could have registered it under this email), `handleBuyerPortalAuth` no longer just flips `emailVerified`: it resets `passwordHash` to a random placeholder with `passwordSet:false` AND `deleteMany`s all `BuyerRefreshToken` rows BEFORE verifying/linking (both the first-link and already-linked paths) — closes the hijack where an attacker pre-registers a victim's email with a password, the victim's later Google sign-in "verifies" the attacker's account, and the attacker's password then passes `requestSeller`'s verified gate. Legit-user collateral (accepted): an unverified password registrant who later uses Google must reset their password (`set-password` re-enabled by `passwordSet:false`). **F3 audit:** `scripts/audit-buyer-verification-grandfather.mjs` (READ-ONLY, owner-run via `railway run --service postgres`; 2026-08-26 connection fixed — bare `new PrismaClient()` throws under Prisma 7 and resolved a stale root client, so the script had never run; now proxy-URL + PrismaPg adapter like demo-seed/prod-migrate; first real run: 16 suspects, 12 e2e fixtures, 1 high-risk reported) flags pre-cutoff tokenless-verified accounts matching customer records + their ACTIVE links — the grandfather `UPDATE ... WHERE emailVerified=false` is time-unbounded and must never be hand-replayed after a restore.
- **buyer controller** `buyer` — sellers, invites details/accept, sellers request/disconnect, profile, orders, invoices(+/:id), statement, products(+categories/:id), orders active/:id, dashboard, **replenishment** (+ `POST|DELETE /replenishment/:productId/snooze`), **shelf** (`GET /shelf`, `POST /shelf/add-all-low`), authorizations, **stock alerts (P5-03)** — `GET /buyer/stock-alerts` (`{productIds}` of the buyer's PENDING alerts), `POST|DELETE /buyer/products/:productId/stock-alert` (subscribe/unsubscribe, `ctx.customerId`+`ctx.tenantId`); `getProduct` (`GET /buyer/products/:id`) now async and returns additive `alertSubscribed: boolean` via `StockAlertService.isSubscribed`.
